# Modern Coverage

> **Headline (manual, 2026-05-28):** Raw 27.5% (6022 / 21917) ·
> Live mtgtop8 Modern top-20 most-played: 20 / 20. Sampled played-pool staple
> coverage ~51% (see Headline numbers note). Refresh the on-disk factory / template
> counts in the table below with `./scripts/update-coverage-headline.sh`
> (CI hook: `--check` exits non-zero on drift).
>
> The card pool itself ships embedded in `Majik.Core` as
> `CardData/Embedded/modern-cards.json.gz`. **Card factory PRs do NOT
> regenerate this file** — `IsImplemented` is derived at load time from
> the `[CardName]` registry (`Majik.Core/CardData/Factories/ImplementedCardNames.cs`),
> so adding a factory flips the flag automatically. Only regenerate the
> seed on a Scryfall data refresh (new cards / errata) via
> `dotnet run --project Majik.Console -- export-modern-cards <scryfall.json>`.

Living tracker for Modern-format card + mechanic implementation in the Majik engine.

All four share the Onslaught cycling-land posture (enters-tapped via `EntersTappedReplacement`/`EntersTappedBinder` + a single `{T}: Add {color}` `ManaAbility` CR 605.1 + Cycling routed through the shared `CyclingFactory.Build` primitive — `DiscardSelfCost` hand-zone gate CR 702.32a + `CardCycledEvent` publish CR 702.32d), differing only in produced colour and the {1}{color} cycle cost. **Last updated:** 2026-05-25 (Midrange trio — Anointer Priest + Bygone Bishop + Lonis, Cryptozoologist. Three new white/Simic creature factories under `Majik.Core/CardData/Factories/` filling the original `feat/midrange-trio` slate; Anointed Procession dropped from scope (token-doubler primitive doesn't exist yet — surface a `TokenCreationReplacementEffect` rail before shipping), Cunning Constructor / Sticky Inheritance verified non-real on Scryfall and substituted with Lonis per the audit prompt's candidate list. `AnointerPriestFactory` (Amonkhet Creature — Human Cleric {1}{W} 1/3) ships the printed lifegain + Embalm-keyword-marker pair — `TriggeredAbility` over `CardMovedEvent` gated on `ToZone == Battlefield`, `Card is Permanent perm && perm.IsToken` (Bridge from Below probe shape), `Card.HasType(Creature)`, and `Card.Controller == owner`; resolved effect calls `Player.GainLife(1)` (CR 119). `KeywordAbility("Embalm")` attached as a scan-surface marker only — alt-cost graveyard-cast + copy-as-token-with-overrides (CR 702.130) needs an `EmbalmAlternativeCost` + copy-into-token primitive, same posture as Ragavan's Dash and Lotus Bloom's Suspend. `BygoneBishopFactory` (Shadows over Innistrad Creature — Spirit Cleric {2}{W} 2/3) ships Flying + cast-creature investigate trigger — `KeywordAbility("Flying")` marker (CombatAbilities reads it) + `TriggeredAbility` over `SpellCastEvent` gated on `Spell.Controller == owner`, `Spell.Card.HasType(Creature)`, and printed mana value ≤ 3 via `e.Spell.Card is Card concrete ? concrete.ManaCostValue.TotalValue : ManaCost.Parse(...).TotalValue` (the Up the Beanstalk / Spell Snare idiom — CR 202.3b). Resolved effect calls `TokenFactory.CreateClue(controller, zoneService)` (CR 701.30 — Clue artifact token under controller). `LonisCryptozoologistFactory` (Streets of New Capenna Commander Legendary Creature — Snake Elf Scout {G}{U} 2/2) ships the v1 investigate-trigger half — `TriggeredAbility` over `CardMovedEvent` gated on `ToZone == Battlefield`, `Card.HasType(Creature)`, `!(Card is Permanent perm && perm.IsToken)` ("nontoken" gate), `Card.Controller == owner`, and `Card != lonis` ("another" — CR 109.2 / CR 603.6e); resolved effect creates a Clue via `TokenFactory.CreateClue`. The activated `{T}, Sacrifice X Clues: opponent reveals top X, may put a nonland perm with mv ≤ X onto the battlefield, rest random-bottom` ability is deferred — needs variable-X activated-cost wiring + reveal-N + choose-and-cheat-with-mv-cap + random-bottom-from-reveal-pile primitives at the activated-ability surface (closest siblings — Tutor's reveal, Charbelcher's random-bottom — work at the spell-resolve layer); surface once a second card needs the same plumbing. Tests: `Majik.Core.Tests/CardData/AnointerPriestFactoryTests.cs` (9 — identity Human Cleric 1/3 {1}{W} + dispatch + Embalm keyword marker + creature-token ETB gains 1 life + two tokens gain 2 life + non-token creature no-trigger + Clue (non-creature token) no-trigger + opponent's token no-trigger) + `BygoneBishopFactoryTests.cs` (8 — identity Spirit Cleric 2/3 {2}{W} + dispatch + Flying marker + cast 2-mana creature → 1 Clue + mv=3 still triggers + mv=4 no-trigger + Instant cast no-trigger + opponent's small creature no-trigger + two small creatures → two Clues) + `LonisCryptozoologistFactoryTests.cs` (7 — identity Legendary Snake Elf Scout 2/2 {G}{U} + dispatch + non-token creature ETB → 1 Clue + creature token no-trigger + Lonis's own ETB no-self-trigger + opponent's creature no-trigger + non-creature ETB no-trigger). All 7429 Core tests pass; full Majik.Core + Majik.Core.Tests builds green. Coverage NamedFactory tier +3 (Anointer Priest + Bygone Bishop + Lonis, Cryptozoologist). Cards-db is being removed — pure factory work, no JSON / SQL paths touched.)
**Last updated:** 2026-05-25 (Modern Mill pillar — Glimpse the Unthinkable + Hedron Crab + Archive Trap. Three new factories under `Majik.Core/CardData/Factories/` shipping the Dimir Mill spine. `GlimpseTheUnthinkableFactory` (Ravnica Sorcery {U}{B}) mirrors `BrainFreezeFactory`'s "target player mills N" SpellDef pattern — single 1..1 "target player" `TargetRequest`, `EffectFactory` resolves the chosen token via the caller-supplied resolver and calls `MillAction.Apply(target, MillCount=10)` (CR 701.13); non-Player resolver result no-ops per CR 608.2b. `HedronCrabFactory` (Zendikar Creature — Homarid 0/2 {U}) added a new `CardSubtype.Homarid` enum entry (no prior Crab / Homarid subtype existed); landfall triggered ability uses the shared `Triggers.OnLandEntersUnderControl(owner)` predicate (same as `TirelessProvisionerFactory` / `TirelessTrackerFactory`) with a 1..1 "target player" `TargetRequest` on the trigger (Bojuka Bog shape); resolve reads `ChosenTargets[0][0]` and mills 3 via `MillAction.Apply`, with v1 fallback to the controller when no target was set (mirrors Bojuka Bog / Tormod's Crypt). `ArchiveTrapFactory` (Zendikar Instant {3}{U}{U}) ships the printed mana cost path — single 1..1 "target opponent" `TargetRequest`, resolve mills 13 via `MillAction.Apply` with a CR 109.1 self-exclusion guard (caster as target no-ops). The **alternative cost** ("If an opponent searched their library this turn, you may cast this spell without paying its mana cost", CR 118.9) is **deferred** — `grep` over `Majik.Core/Domain/`, `Majik.Core/Events/`, `Majik.Core/Game/` confirms no `LibrarySearchEvent` / `TurnState.OpponentSearchedLibraryThisTurn` tracking surface exists yet; the factory class doc flags the alt-cost arm for follow-up when the search-tracking primitive lands. Tests: `GlimpseTheUnthinkableFactoryTests.cs` (6 — identity Sorcery {U}{B} Blue+Black + dispatch + SpellDef shape + mill 10 + short library mills all + illegal target no-op) + `HedronCrabFactoryTests.cs` (6 — identity Creature Homarid 0/2 {U} + dispatch + landfall trigger has target-player request + own-land ETB queues + mills 3 to chosen + opponent's-land doesn't fire + no-target fallback mills controller) + `ArchiveTrapFactoryTests.cs` (6 — identity Instant {3}{U}{U} CMC 5 + dispatch + SpellDef shape + mill 13 + targeting-self no-op + short library mills all). All 7476 Core tests pass; full solution builds green. Coverage NamedFactory tier +3 (Glimpse + Hedron Crab + Archive Trap). Embedded seed `modern-cards.json.gz` regenerated — `isImplemented` count 504 → 507.)
**Last updated:** 2026-05-25 (UW utility 3 — Stoneforge Masterwork + Thalia's Lancers. Two new factories under `Majik.Core/CardData/Factories/` covering the Aether Revolt tribal-Equipment payoff `StoneforgeMasterworkFactory` and the Shadows over Innistrad legendary-tutor body `ThaliasLancersFactory` (substituted for the originally-scoped Stoneforge Acolyte per the audit prompt's obscurity carve-out). `ChampionOfTheParishFactory` already shipped (PR lineage; Innistrad {W} 1/1 Human Soldier with the canonical "another Human enters → +1/+1 counter" `TriggeredAbility` gated on `EventTriggerCondition<CardMovedEvent>` for Battlefield + Creature + Human + same controller + `!ReferenceEquals(self)`); verified intact at audit time — no factory-side changes needed. `StoneforgeMasterworkFactory` (Aether Revolt Artifact — Equipment {1}) ships the dynamic-N "+N/+N for each other creature you control sharing a creature type" body via paired `AttachedBoostEffect(powerFn, toughnessFn)` constructor (CR 613 Layer 7c — same dynamic-N posture as `CranialPlatingFactory`'s artifact-count closure, but both power AND toughness sample the same shared-subtype count since the printed bonus is "+N/+N" not "+N/+0"). Pure helper `CountSharedSubtypeCreatures(Permanent)` enumerates the controller's battlefield creatures excluding the equipped creature itself (printed "other"), intersects their `Subtypes` with the equipped creature's `Subtypes` restricted to creature subtypes via an inline `IsCreatureSubtype` predicate (same carve-out shape as `OkoThiefOfCrownsFactory`'s Layer 4 type-replacement helper — excludes Land / Artifact / Enchantment / Planeswalker subtype ranges, treats every other enum value as a creature subtype). Reads `card.AttachedTo` + `card.Controller` dynamically so re-equipping + control-change retarget cleanly (CR 109.5). Equip {2} via the shared `EquipActivatedAbility` primitive with the Puresteel-Paladin zero-equip cost-provider hook (CR 702.6 — matches every other equipment in the cycle). `ThaliasLancersFactory` (Shadows over Innistrad Creature — Human Knight {3}{W}{W} 4/4) ships the 4/4 Human Knight shape + `KeywordAbility("First strike")` marker (CR 702.7 — read by `CombatAbilities.HasFirstStrike` for the two-step combat damage sequencer, same wiring as `BorosReckonerFactory`) + ETB tutor `TriggeredAbility` via `Triggers.OnEnterBattlefieldSelf(card)` (CR 603.6a). Resolve body walks the controller's library, deterministically picks the first card matching `HasSupertype(CardSupertype.Legendary)` (v1 picker — same posture as `StoneforgeMysticFactory`'s Equipment tutor and the wider tutor cycle; agent-driven `ChooseLibraryPickAsync` prompt deferred to the shared tutor-prompt queue), moves Library → Hand, then `LibraryShuffle.ShuffleLibrary` per CR 701.20a. The "may" / no-eligible-candidate paths both resolve as a clean no-op with the shuffle still firing (CR 701.19a — declined / no-match is a legal outcome; shuffle runs because a search occurred). Predicate gates on the printed "legendary card" — accepts ANY card type with `CardSupertype.Legendary` so legendary artifacts (Karn Liberated, Wurmcoil Engine ish), planeswalkers (Liliana of the Veil, Teferi Time Raveler), and lands (Urza's Saga? legendary enchantments) all qualify alongside the headline legendary creatures (Reki, the History of Kamigawa-style legends matter shells). Reveal-event emission deferred (no `CardRevealedEvent` publication; same gap as Stoneforge Mystic / Eladamri's Call). Tests: `Majik.Core.Tests/CardData/StoneforgeMasterworkTests.cs` (9 — identity + dispatch, Equip {2} cost shape, dynamic boost scales by other shared-type creature count across Human/Soldier/Elf siblings, unequipped boost gates to 0, sibling Equipment artifact doesn't trigger the boost (regression — Equipment is a non-creature subtype despite the shared enum), pure-helper round-trips unattached/empty + bear-with-other-bear/elf shape) + `ThaliasLancersTests.cs` (7 — identity (Human Knight 4/4 {3}{W}{W}) + dispatch + First strike keyword marker present, ETB trigger pulls first legendary creature from library to hand + leaves non-legendary bait + ETB trigger pulls legendary artifact (any legendary card type qualifies), ETB trigger no-legendary no-op, ETB trigger Battlefield-only active zone). All 7544 Core + 164 Api + 135 Bot tests pass; full solution builds green. Coverage NamedFactory tier +2 (Stoneforge Masterwork + Thalia's Lancers). Cards-db is being removed — pure factory work, no JSON / SQL paths touched.)
**Last updated:** 2026-05-25 (Modern Eldrazi pillar — Eldrazi Skyspawner. Audit verified Thought-Knot Seer + Reality Smasher are already shipped (Eldrazi cycle PR — see existing `ThoughtKnotSeerFactory` + `RealitySmasherFactory` entries below); only `EldraziSkyspawnerFactory` (Battle for Zendikar Creature — Eldrazi Drone {2}{U} 2/2) was outstanding. Factory ships Flying as a `KeywordAbility` marker (CR 702.9 — same posture as Pinnacle Emissary's Drone token / Slickshot Show-Off) plus one ETB triggered ability (CR 603.6a) over `EventTriggerCondition<CardMovedEvent>` gated on (self, `ToZone == Battlefield`). Resolution creates one 1/1 colourless Eldrazi Scion creature token under Skyspawner's controller via the public helper `EldraziSkyspawnerFactory.CreateEldraziScionToken(controller, zones)` — `TokenFactory.CreateOnBattlefield` with `TokenSpec(Name: "Eldrazi Scion", Power: 1, Toughness: 1, Subtypes: [Eldrazi, Scion], Colors: [])` (explicit empty colour list stamps colourless via `TokenColorsOverride` per CR 105 / CR 111.4); the printed "Sacrifice this creature: Add {C}." mana ability is wired as a plain `ManaAbility(scion, controller, ManaCost.Parse("C"))` — same v1 posture as `TokenFactory.CreateEldraziSpawn` / Treasure / Food where the sacrifice cost rider is documented but unenforced pending a sac-cost `ManaAbility` extension. Token routes through the supplied `ZoneService` so the Scion's own ETB `CardMovedEvent` publishes for downstream subscribers (Soul Warden et al.). Two-overload shape (`Create(Player)` shape-only + `Create(Player, ZoneService?, TriggerManager?)` fully-wired) mirrors Pinnacle Emissary / Tireless Tracker / Thought-Knot Seer. Dispatches via `[CardName("Eldrazi Skyspawner")]` source-gen rail. Tests: `Majik.Core.Tests/CardData/EldraziSkyspawnerFactoryTests.cs` (7 — identity Eldrazi Drone 2/2 {2}{U} + dispatch via `NamedCardFactory.Create`, Flying keyword marker count = 1, single ETB `TriggeredAbility` attached, `CreateEldraziScionToken` shape (1/1 colourless Eldrazi Scion with `IsToken == true` + `Zone == Battlefield`), Scion has one `ManaAbility` producing `Generic = 1 / TotalValue = 1` ({C} folds into the Generic bucket per `ManaCost.Parse`'s 'C' case — matches Eldrazi Spawn / Urza's Saga posture), ETB-effect execution lands one Scion on the controller's battlefield with correct P/T + subtypes + mana ability). All 7280 Core tests pass (1 pre-existing flaky `AgentRegistryTests.SurveilSelfSpell_ConsultsAgent_WhenRegistered` parallel-state failure intermittently observed and unrelated — passes in isolation). Embedded card seed regenerated via `dotnet run --project Majik.Console -- export-modern-cards` — `IsImplemented` flips on for Eldrazi Skyspawner (478 implemented names). Coverage NamedFactory tier +1 (Eldrazi Skyspawner). Cards-db is being removed — pure factory work, no JSON / SQL paths touched.)

**Last updated:** 2026-05-25 (UB card-draw — Painful Truths + Read the Bones. Two new sorcery factories under `Majik.Core/CardData/Factories/` completing the black grindy card-draw trio alongside the already-shipped `BoneShardsFactory` (which on audit ships as Sorcery {B} with disjunctive sac-or-discard additional cost — printed type matches Scryfall oracle, see PR #608 entry). `PainfulTruthsFactory` (Battle for Zendikar Sorcery {1}{B}{B}) ships the canonical Converge body — `Func<int>? colorsSpentProvider` parameter (same mana-provenance shape as `BringToLightFactory` / `PrismaticEndingFactory`; defaults to `DefaultColorsSpent = 1`, the printed-pip floor since the two {B} pips collapse to one distinct colour); `BuildResolveEffect` runs `Fx.DrawCards(caster, X)` (CR 121.1 — replacement bus per-draw, empty-library stamps CR 704.5b loss flag) then `Fx.LoseLife(caster, X)` (CR 119.3 — single life-loss event for the resolved X, NOT X individual 1-life ticks — Blood Artist / Cruel Celebrant triggers count once). `ReadTheBonesFactory` (Theros Sorcery {2}{B}) ships the scry-2-draw-2-lose-2 sequence — `BuildResolveEffect` peeks the top 2 via `ScryAction.Peek`, dispatches to `AgentRegistry.Get(caster)?.ChooseScryDecisionAsync` when an agent is registered (pre-agent default sends both to the bottom, same posture as `SerumVisionsFactory` / `PreordainFactory`), applies the partition via `ScryAction.Apply`, then `Fx.DrawCards(2)` + `Fx.LoseLife(2)`. Order matters — scry inspects the pre-draw top of library; draws run individually so dredge/empty-library flag behaviour matches Sign in Blood / Night's Whisper / Painful Truths. Both factories use `Func<int>?` / parameterless `BuildSpellDefinition(caster)` (no target requests, no modes, no X variable; converge X comes from the mana ledger not a target-time choice). Tests: `Majik.Core.Tests/CardData/PainfulTruthsTests.cs` (10 — identity Sorcery {1}{B}{B} + dispatch + DefaultColorsSpent = 1 + resolve X=3 draws 3 lose 3 + resolve X=1 + resolve null-provider uses default + resolve X=0 no-op + resolve X<0 clamps to 0 + resolve X=3 empty-library flags loss + life-loss still ticks + SpellDef shape) + `ReadTheBonesTests.cs` (8 — identity Sorcery {2}{B} + dispatch + resolve default scry bottoms both + draw + lose 2 + resolve agent-keep-top draws same two + empty-library scry no-op + draw flags loss + life-loss still ticks + one-card library scry-then-draw + SpellDef shape + Scry/Draw/Life constants match printed). All 7251 Core + 304 Server + 164 Api + 135 Bot + 3 Bot.Integration tests pass. Coverage NamedFactory tier +2 (Painful Truths + Read the Bones). Cards-db is being removed — pure factory work, no JSON / SQL paths touched.)

**Last updated:** 2026-05-25 (Goblin pillar — Skirk Prospector + Goblin Recruiter + Conspicuous Snoop. additional-cost payer prefers other Goblins over self (deterministic v1 picker — saves Prospector for the chain end), falls back to self when self is the only Goblin so the printed CR 122.x self-feeding combo line works end-to-end. `GoblinRecruiterFactory` (Visions Creature — Goblin {1}{R} 1/1) ships the ETB tutor "search your library for any number of Goblin creature cards, reveal those cards, then shuffle and put them on top of your library in any order" — single-pick semantics in v1 (engine's `IPlayerAgent.ChooseLibraryPickAsync` is single-pick; same gap as `GoblinMatronFactory`); predicate gates on `HasType(Creature) && HasSubtype(Goblin)` per oracle ("Goblin creature cards"); decline (agent returns null) is a legal zero-pick "any number" outcome (CR 701.19a); the pick is removed, library is shuffled (CR 701.20a), and the pick goes back via `Zone.InsertCardAt(0, ...)` so the next draw pulls it (matches printed "shuffle and put them on top of your library in any order"). `ConspicuousSnoopFactory` (Jumpstart Creature — Goblin Rogue {R} 1/1) ships the v1-simplified card shape per the prompt's "complex; same gap blocks Bolas's Citadel, Magus of the Future, Vivien Champion of the Wilds) and NO Layer 6 activated-ability copy from another card (same gap as Vesuvan Doppelganger / Mirrorpool); the three oracle riders are wired as description-only `StaticAbility` entries so audits / bot decision surfaces see Snoop as a three-rider Goblin lord, plus two pure-helper functions `LookAtTopOfLibrary(controller)` (returns top of controller's library or null) and `IsTopOfLibraryGoblin(controller)` (controller-side peek + Goblin-subtype check). Three deferred items documented in the factory class doc: public reveal of top card to opponents (no event-bus broadcast); may-cast-top-from-library permission (cast-from-zone primitive); Layer 6 activated-ability copy. **Last updated:** 2026-05-25 (Devoted Druid + Vizier of Remedies + Lurking Roper — Druid Combo enablers + Bloomburrow mill body. Three new factories under `Majik.Core/CardData/Factories/` + one new cost primitive (`Majik.Core/Costs/AddCounterCost.cs`). `AddCounterCost(Permanent source, CounterType type, int amount = 1, ReplacementBus? replacements = null)` implements `ICost` for the "Put a counter on &lt;source&gt;" activation-cost shape (CR 118.3 — cost mutates state immediately; CR 614.1 — cost-side counter placement IS subject to replacement effects per the official Vizier of Remedies + Devoted Druid ruling). When a `ReplacementBus` is supplied, `Pay` routes through `CountersService.Add` so Vizier of Remedies / Hardened Scales / Doubling Season can rewrite or cancel the cost payload; null bus falls through to a direct `Permanent.Counters.Add`. `DevotedDruidFactory` (Shadowmoor Creature — Elf Druid {1}{G} 0/2) ships the printed `ManaAbility({T}: Add {G})` (CR 605.1 — implicit self-tap; no priority pass) plus the untap activated ability — costs `[AddCounterCost(card, CounterType.MinusOneMinusOne, 1, replacements)]`, single `Effect("untap self", () => card.Untap())`. Two-overload shape: `Create(Player)` is shape-only (no bus → counter lands directly on each activation), `Create(Player, ReplacementBus?)` is the combo path. `VizierOfRemediesFactory` (Amonkhet Creature — Human Cleric {W}{W} 2/1) ships the static replacement via the new `VizierOfRemediesReplacement : IReplacementEffect&lt;CounterAddIntent&gt;` — `Applies` gates on (a) `_source.Zone == Battlefield` (CR 614.6 — replacement only active on the battlefield, same lifecycle-via-Applies posture as `HardenedScalesAddReplacement` and `SoulScarMageDamageReplacement` — flicker / LTB lifts the rider naturally without explicit unregister), (b) `intent.Type == MinusOneMinusOne`, (c) `intent.Amount >= 1`, (d) `intent.Target is Creature`, (e) `target.Controller == _source.Controller`. `Replace` rewrites `intent with { Amount = 0 }` so `CountersService.Add` commits no counters and returns 0 (the printed "instead put no counter" payload — equivalent to a full cancel since the engine treats Amount=0 as a no-op commit). With Devoted Druid + Vizier on the battlefield + a wired `ReplacementBus`, the loop is arbitrarily long — infinite green mana, the classic Druid Combo. Pair with Walking Ballista (already shipped, PR #128 / `WalkingBallistaFactory`) for the canonical instant-kill payoff via repeated +1/+1 → ping. `LurkingRoperFactory` (Bloomburrow Creature — Snake Horror {3}{B} 4/3) ships a `KeywordAbility("Forage")` marker (CR 205.3 — Bloomburrow mechanic marker for keyword-aware code; cost-and-effect plumbing deferred — printed Forage on Roper itself has no rider effect, the marker is informational) plus the ETB `TriggeredAbility` via `Triggers.OnEnterBattlefieldSelf` resolving "each opponent mills three cards" — the resolver-closure shape (`Func&lt;IReadOnlyList&lt;Player&gt;&gt;?` injection mirroring `CreepingChillFactory` / `OmnathLocusOfCreationFactory`) loops each non-controller player via `MillAction.Apply(opp, 3)` (CR 701.13 — Mill; short-library auto-cap to remainder). Tests: `Majik.Core.Tests/CardData/DevotedDruidTests.cs` (7 — identity + dispatch + ManaAbility shape + ActivatedAbility shape + cost-pays-counter-then-untaps + Vizier-on-battlefield replaces cost-counter to zero + 10-iteration loop with Vizier never accumulates + no-bus single-activation accumulates one counter) + `VizierOfRemediesTests.cs` (6 — identity + dispatch + replaces -1/-1 on controlled creature to zero + does-not-affect +1/+1 + does-not-affect opponent's creature + inert off-battlefield + single-arg shape-only no-registration) + `LurkingRoperTests.cs` (6 — identity + dispatch + Forage keyword marker + ETB mills 3 from each opponent + does-not-mill controller even when resolver mistakenly includes self + null-resolver no-op + short-library mills remainder per CR 701.13). All 7163 Core + 304 Server + 164 Api + 135 Bot + 3 Bot.Integration tests pass. Coverage NamedFactory tier +3 (Devoted Druid + Vizier of Remedies + Lurking Roper); Primitive tier +1 (`AddCounterCost`). Druid Combo (Devoted Druid + Vizier of Remedies + Walking Ballista) end-to-end playable in the engine — the classic Modern banned-list infinite-mana shell. Embedded card seed regen deferred — current Scryfall snapshot drops a deck-list reference (`Flashback`) that the `DeckBindingAuditTests` audit consumes; the factories dispatch via `[CardName]` regardless of seed state, the `IsImplemented` flag flips on the next regen against a fresh Scryfall bulk export. Cards-db is being removed — pure factory + Costs primitive + Effects.IReplacementEffect work, no JSON / SQL paths touched.)
**Last updated:** 2026-05-25 (Collected Company + Eldritch Evolution + Chord of Calling audit — creature-tutor combo enabler trio. Resolve peeks the top six cards (CR 701.21 — short library safe), filters to creatures with `ManaCost.Parse(c.ManaCost).TotalValue <= 3` (CR 202.3), then sequentially prompts the supplied `IPlayerAgent` (or `AgentRegistry.Get(caster)` fallback; deterministic first-eligible when neither is registered) up to two times — picks refilter the candidate pool each pass so the same card never reappears; agent returns `null` to decline at any slot (printed "up to two" → 0 is legal). Picked creatures move Library → Battlefield routed through `ZoneService.MoveCard` (CR 603.6a — ETB triggers + `CardMovedEvent` fire); raw zone fallback otherwise. Remaining peeked cards re-bottom in random order via `GameRandomRegistry.Get(caster).Shuffle` so the printed "random order" clause honours the per-game RNG (same posture as `TibaltsTrickeryFactory` / `GoblinCharbelcherFactory`). The peek itself does NOT trigger a library shuffle (CR 701.20a attaches to search effects; "look at" is a peek). Tests: `Majik.Core.Tests/CardData/CollectedCompanyTests.cs` (9 — identity Instant {3}{G} + dispatch + two-eligible-creatures both ETB + rest-to-bottom, mv-4-drop excluded per CR 202.3, non-creature filter excludes instants / sorceries / lands, short-library works on what's available, empty-library no-op, agent decline-after-first one-ETB via explicit `agent:` parameter, single-eligible default-fallback one-ETB). All 6997 Core tests pass. Coverage NamedFactory tier +1 (Collected Company). **Last updated:** 2026-05-25 (Reflector Mage per-player name-ban + Veteran Explorer. Two threads of work covering the long-deferred Reflector Mage name-restriction half and the Mercadian Masques basic-land tutor `VeteranExplorerFactory`. Surge alt-cost lives on main already (see PR #592 — Reckless Bushwhacker retrofit); this rebase drops the duplicate probe wiring. New `Majik.Core/Costs/SurgeAlternativeCost.cs` — sealed `IAlternativeCost` carrying `(ManaCost surgeCost, Func<Player, bool> hasCastAnotherSpellThisTurn)`; `CanCastFor` gates on (a) `card.Zone == Hand` (CR 702.108a — surge is paid from the hand), (b) `card.Owner == caster` (alt-cost contract), and (c) the predicate (CR 702.108a — "you or a teammate has cast another spell this turn"; v1 collapses to caster-only since two-player Modern has no teammates). `OnResolved` no-op + `PostResolutionZone` null (instant/sorcery/permanent disposition is unchanged). New `Majik.Core/Players/Agents/SurgeAltCostProbe.cs` mirrors `SuspendAltCostProbe`'s data-driven shape — `Func<ICard, ManaCost?> lookup` + `Func<Player, bool>? hasCastAnotherSpellThisTurn` (defaults to conservative `_ => false` since no central spells-cast-this-turn counter exists yet; production wiring will pass a closure over a `SpellCastEvent` subscription). `DefaultLookup` recognises Reckless Bushwhacker → Surge {R}. Registered in `AlternativeCostProbeRegistry.CreateDefault` between `SuspendAltCostProbe` and `ImproviseAltCostProbe`. `Majik.Core/Rules/CastingRestrictions.cs` extended with per-player named-card-block rail (`AddNamedCardBlockForPlayer(token, player, name)` + `IsCardNameBlockedForPlayer(player, name)`); distinct from the existing global `_namedCardBlocks` rail (Meddling Mage's shape) so the two surfaces compose without trampling each other. `RemoveNamedCardBlock(token)` extended to clear entries on both rails for the same source-token. `ActionValidator.ValidateCastSpell` consults the per-player rail (CR 601.3 — "per-player named-card cast restriction"). `ReflectorMageFactory` ETB resolve now stamps `CastingRestrictions.AddNamedCardBlockForPlayer(card, targetOwner, bouncedName)` after the bounce (CR 601.3) and — when an `IEventBus` was supplied — subscribes a one-shot `TurnStartedEvent` handler that fires on the controller's NEXT turn-start (the in-progress turn-start has already been published before subscription, so the first matching event the handler receives IS the controller's next turn — CR 702 "your next turn") and removes the ban via `CastingRestrictions.RemoveNamedCardBlock(card)`. New `VeteranExplorerFactory` (Mercadian Masques Creature — Human Scout {1}{G} 1/2) — death trigger via `Triggers.OnDies(card)` (CR 603.6c) with ActiveZones = Battlefield + Graveyard (Wurmcoil posture so the trigger still matches after ZoneService stamps Zone=Graveyard pre-publish). Resolve walks the configured `Func<IReadOnlyList<Player>>? allPlayersResolver` (CR 101.4 APNAP collapse — fixed yield order; shape-only fallback iterates owner only), calling `TutorUpToTwoBasicsToBattlefield` per player: greedy fixed-point that re-asks `IPlayerAgent.ChooseLibraryPickAsync` after each pick (so the agent can stop at one), filtering by basic-land names (CR 305.6) + `HasType(Land)`. Each picked card moves Library → Battlefield untapped (no "tapped" qualifier in oracle text), calls `MarkEnteredBattlefield`, then `LibraryShuffle.ShuffleLibrary` per player (CR 701.20a). Tests: `Majik.Core.Tests/Costs/SurgeAlternativeCostTests.cs` (9 — `CanCastFor` predicate-true/false/wrong-owner/not-in-hand, Description shape, OnResolved no-op, PostResolutionZone null, null-cost / null-predicate guards) + `Majik.Core.Tests/Players/Agents/SurgeAltCostProbeTests.cs` (10 — DefaultLookup Reckless-Bushwhacker hit / unknown miss, predicate-true yields cost + CanCastFor true, predicate-false yields cost but CanCastFor false (probe is pre-filter, cost is source-of-truth), card-not-in-hand / wrong-owner / no-lookup-hit yields nothing, custom-lookup override, null-lookup guard, registry contains SurgeAltCostProbe in CreateDefault) + `Majik.Core.Tests/CardData/VeteranExplorerTests.cs` (8 — identity Human Scout 1/2 + dispatch + single-dies-trigger shape + ActiveZones Battlefield + Graveyard, resolve tutors up-to-two basics to battlefield untapped, fewer-than-two tutors-what's-available, zero-basics no-op, allPlayersResolver each-player tutors, non-basic lands not eligible) + 2 new tests in `Majik.Core.Tests/CardData/MantisRiderTests.cs` covering Reflector Mage's per-player name ban (per-player rail set on owner only / global rail untouched, name ban lifts on controller's next TurnStartedEvent). Existing `AlternativeCostProbeRegistryTests.CreateDefault_HasCoreProbes` updated to expect 11 probes (Pitch + Delve + Overload + Cascade + Energy + Escape + Kicker + Suspend + Surge + Improvise + Convoke). All 6790 Core + 304 Server + 158 Api + 135 Bot + 3 Bot.Integration tests pass. Coverage NamedFactory tier +1 (Veteran Explorer); Reflector Mage promoted from "bounce-only / name-ban deferred" to fully shipped. Cards-db is being removed — pure factory + Costs + Rules + Probe work, no JSON / SQL paths touched.)

**Last updated:** 2026-05-25 (Deceiver Exarch + Pestermite + Splinter Twin audit — UR Twin combo pair. Two new factories under `Majik.Core/CardData/Factories/` completing the iconic Modern Splinter Twin shell. `DeceiverExarchFactory` (New Phyrexia Creature — Cleric {2}{U} 1/4) ships the 1/4 Cleric shape + `KeywordAbility("Flash")` marker (CR 702.8) + an ETB `TriggeredAbility` (CR 603.6a) via `Triggers.OnEnterBattlefieldSelf` with a single mandatory 1..1 "target permanent" `TargetRequest`. CR 700.2d printed modal "Choose one — • Untap target permanent an opponent controls. • Untap target noncreature permanent you control." is collapsed in v1 into a fused-predicate union request because both modes share the same effect ("Untap target ~"); the resolve closure re-checks the union legality at resolution (CR 608.2b — target must be on the battlefield AND either controlled by an opponent OR a non-creature controlled by the ability's controller), then untaps if currently tapped (CR 701.27 idempotent on an already-untapped permanent — defensive guard since `Permanent.Untap` throws on already-untapped state). Own-creature target → ability does nothing (neither mode legal). True modal "Choose one —" prompt with `IPlayerAgent.ChooseModeAsync` deferred — current ETB-modal infra exists only for spell casts (Izzet Charm / Archmage's Charm / Cryptic Command), not triggered abilities. `PestermiteFactory` (Lorwyn Creature — Faerie Rogue {2}{U} 2/1) ships the 2/1 Faerie Rogue shape + `KeywordAbility("Flash")` + `KeywordAbility("Flying")` markers (CR 702.8 / 702.9 — Flying read by `CombatAbilities.HasFlying` for combat legality; Flash gates instant-speed casting) + an ETB `TriggeredAbility` (CR 603.6a) with a 0..1 "target permanent" `TargetRequest` (printed "you may" modelled by `MinTargets=0` so the controller may decline the trigger — same posture as Solitude's free-decline). Resolution uses the deterministic "useful flip" v1 collapse over the printed binary "tap or untap" mode — untap if currently tapped, tap if currently untapped, so the Twin chain always advances when paired with Splinter Twin. Resolve-time legality check: target must still be on the battlefield (CR 608.2b — illegal-at-resolution → clean no-op). Agent-driven "may" decline / explicit tap-vs-untap mode prompt deferred — shared queue with the broader modal-trigger surface. **Splinter Twin audit**: PR #468's `SplinterTwinFactory` token-copy ability projection (CR 706.2) verified end-to-end via the existing `Majik.Core.Tests/Effects/SplinterTwinTokenChainTests.cs` (4 tests) — token inherits the granted "{T}: create token copy" activated ability with `Source == token` per CR 602.1b; activating the token's inherited ability spawns a second token that itself perpetuates the chain (Name + Haste keyword + inherited ActivatedAbility, name copied from bearer via copy-of-a-copy CR 706.2); every spawned token's delayed end-step exile fires independently on `StepStartedEvent(End)` (CR 603.7); aura LTB revokes the bearer's grant but NOT the tokens' inherited grant (CR 706.2 — copiable values fixed at copy time, divorced from the aura's `AttachedAuraAbilityGrantStaticEffect` lifecycle binder). Audit confirms no gaps in the Twin chain machinery — the two new partners drop into the combo with no factory-side changes needed. The historical "Pestermite-like" stand-in vanilla creature fixture in the chain tests becomes redundant once the production Pestermite ships but is left in place as a minimal unit-test isolation harness. Tests: `Majik.Core.Tests/CardData/Factories/DeceiverExarchFactoryTests.cs` (9 — identity Cleric 1/4 + Flash marker + dispatch + ETB trigger shape (mandatory 1..1 union "opponent's permanent OR your noncreature" description) + untap opponent's tapped permanent (mode 1) + untap own noncreature land (mode 2) + reject own creature (neither mode legal) + reject target left battlefield CR 608.2b + idempotent on already-untapped target via the `IsTapped` defensive guard) + `PestermiteFactoryTests.cs` (7 — identity Faerie Rogue 2/1 + Flash + Flying markers + dispatch + ETB trigger shape (0..1 "target permanent" + Battlefield active zone) + untap tapped target + tap untapped target + no-target-chosen "may" decline no-op + target-left-battlefield no-op). All 6667 Core + 304 Server + 149 Api + 135 Bot + 3 Bot.Integration tests pass. Coverage NamedFactory tier +2 (Deceiver Exarch + Pestermite). The Modern UR Splinter Twin combo (Splinter Twin + either Pestermite or Deceiver Exarch — a longtime banned Modern combo pillar) is now end-to-end playable in the engine. Cards-db is being removed — pure factory work, no JSON / SQL paths touched.)

**Last updated:** 2026-05-25 (Bedlam Reveler + Magmatic Channeler + Mardu Pyromancer pillar audit. Two new factories under `Majik.Core/CardData/Factories/` + one new runtime sentinel (`Card.WasCastFromHand` + `Spell.WasCastFromHand`) covering the canonical "if you cast it from your hand" surface. `BedlamRevelerFactory` (Eldritch Moon Creature — Horror {6}{R}{R} 3/4) ships Trample + Prowess `KeywordAbility` markers (CR 702.19 / CR 702.108 — Trample read by `CombatAbilities.HasTrample`; Prowess trigger wired via `ProwessFactory.Build` when a `ContinuousEffectsService` is supplied, same posture as `MonasterySwiftspearFactory`) + `CostReductionAbility(totalReducer)` that reduces generic mana by the count of instants + sorceries in the caster's graveyard at cost-calc time (CR 117.7 — same reducer shape as `DemilichFactory`, swapping the {U}-pip target for plain generic; coloured {R}{R} pips floor at the printed value per CR 117.7c) + ETB `TriggeredAbility` with `Triggers.OnEnterBattlefieldSelf` + `interveningIf = () => card.WasCastFromHand` (CR 603.6e). Resolve closure runs the printed "discard your hand, then draw three cards" sequence (CR 121.4 — "then" is a single instruction sequence; CR 701.16 + CR 121.1): snapshot hand → Graveyard via `ZoneService.MoveCard` when wired (raw zones otherwise — same two-mode posture as Demilich); three top-of-library draws with `Player.MarkTriedToDrawFromEmptyLibrary` on underflow (CR 704.5b SBA loss flag — same handling as Faithless Looting / Wrenn's Resolve). Defensive resolve-time guard re-checks `WasCastFromHand` (CR 603.6e second-pass). Two-overload shape: `Create(Player)` is shape-only; `Create(owner, eventBus, triggers, effects, zones)` is fully wired. `MagmaticChannelerFactory` (Core Set 2021 Creature — Human Shaman {1}{R} 1/2) ships the 1/2 Human Shaman shape (same tribe as Young Pyromancer) + one `ActivatedAbility` with costs `[ManaCostCost("{2}{R}"), AdditionalCost.Tap(card)]` and the printed CR 602.5b "Activate only if there are four or more instant and/or sorcery cards in your graveyard" activation restriction exposed via the public static predicate `CanActivateGraveyardGate(controller)` (`GraveyardThreshold = 4`). Until `IActivatedAbility.CanActivate` exposes the non-mana activation hook (legacy `canActivateCheck` lives only on `ManaAbility` today — ChromaticStar / MoxOpal / NobleHierarch / FilterLandCycle precedent), v1 enforces the gate two ways: caller-side action enumeration consults the static predicate; the resolve closure re-checks defensively and short-circuits (cost was paid by the cost layer — mirrors `LilianaOfTheVeilFactory` -6 deferred body shape). Resolve body: snapshot up to 4 top cards (Library), filter to `Creature || Instant` (sorceries EXCLUDED from the reveal pool — distinct axis from the graveyard gate which counts instants + sorceries), prompt `IPlayerAgent.ChooseLibraryPickAsync` for a pick (deterministic first-eligible fallback; `null` declines per the printed "you may"), move pick → Hand, rebottom remainder in snapshot order ("in any order" agent re-ordering prompt deferred). New runtime sentinel: `Card.WasCastFromHand` / `Card.SetWasCastFromHand` / `Card.ClearWasCastFromHand` mirror the `Spell.WasCastFromHand` posture (CR 601.2 / CR 113.5). `SpellCastFlow.CastAsync` captures `card.Zone` BEFORE the Hand → Stack move, then after the existing `SetWasCast` stamp also stamps the strict "from-hand" flag iff the captured source zone was `ZoneType.Hand` — distinct from `Card.WasCast` which fires on any cast path (flashback / suspend / from-graveyard / from-exile all set `WasCast` but leave `WasCastFromHand` false). `ZoneService.MoveCard` clears the sentinel alongside `WasCast` on battlefield exit (any destination), preserving CR 400.7 "new object on each zone change" semantics. **Mardu Pyromancer pillar audit**: `YoungPyromancerFactory` (printed CR 603.1 instant/sorcery-cast token trigger; 1/1 red Elemental colour identity already stamped via `TokenSpec.Colors` per PR lineage — no deferred riders), `FaithlessLootingFactory` (draw-two-discard-two with flashback {2}{R} alt cost via `FlashbackOracleParser` parsing the printed oracle text; deferred riders: agent-driven discard pick fallback to last-N-in-hand — shared queue with Connive / Liliana / Yawgmoth discard sites; madness / "exile from graveyard" alt-resolution riders not in scope), `Thoughtseize` (template-bound via `ThoughtseizePatternTemplate` in `SpellTemplates/Templates/Bespoke/` — no named factory needed; v1 deterministic first-non-land pick, full agent-driven prompt deferred behind shared discard-pick surface), `Lightning Bolt` (template-bound via the shared "deals N damage to any target" `DealsDamageEachCreature`-cycle template family — no named factory needed), `LilianaOfTheVeilFactory` (planeswalker with +1 each-player-discards loyalty ability + -2 target-player-sacs-a-creature; deferred: -6 ultimate pile-split — no "split into piles" primitive in the engine, loyalty ability wired with no-op body so cost still paid per CR 606.3; agent-driven discard prompt + targeting prompts deferred). Bedlam Reveler is the missing piece in this five-card pillar — the graveyard-density payoff that closes out games after Lightning Bolt / Faithless Looting / Thoughtseize / Young Pyromancer / Liliana of the Veil fuel the yard. Tests: `Majik.Core.Tests/CardData/BedlamRevelerTests.cs` (12 — identity Horror 3/4 + Trample + Prowess markers, NamedCardFactory dispatch, empty-graveyard pays {6}{R}{R}, 5-instants/sorceries reduces to {1}{R}{R}, 8-instants/sorceries floors at {R}{R} per CR 117.7c, non-instant/sorcery graveyard cards don't reduce, ETB trigger Battlefield active zone + has interveningIf, intervening-if true only when `SetWasCastFromHand(true)`, ETB resolve discards hand + draws 3 in the printed CR 121.4 "then" order, non-cast-from-hand path no-op via the resolve-time guard, short-library mid-draw flags CR 704.5b SBA loss, empty-hand cast-from-hand path only draws) + `MagmaticChannelerTests.cs` (14 — identity Human Shaman 1/2, NamedCardFactory dispatch, one ActivatedAbility with `{2}{R}` mana + Tap cost shape, `CanActivateGraveyardGate` returns false at 0 / 3, true at threshold 4 / 5, ignores non-instant/sorcery graveyard cards, resolve picks first eligible creature from top-4 + bottoms rest in snapshot order, no-eligible-cards no-op-but-rebottoms, empty-library clean no-op, library shorter than 4 peeks what remains, defensive graveyard-gate-fails resolve short-circuit, sorceries excluded from reveal pool — only instant wins from mixed top-4). All 6630 Core + 304 Server + 149 Api + 135 Bot + 3 Bot.Integration tests pass. Coverage NamedFactory tier +2 (Bedlam Reveler + Magmatic Channeler). Cards-db is being removed — pure factory + new `Card`/`Spell` runtime-sentinel primitive + `SpellCastFlow` source-zone capture work, no JSON / SQL paths touched.)

**Last updated:** 2026-05-25 (Voltage Surge + Recruitment Officer + Boros Energy pillar audit. Optional cost — caster opts in by passing the cost via `SpellCastFlow.CastAsync`'s `additionalCosts` parameter; cast flow refuses per CR 601.2g when caster controls no artifact. `BuildAdditionalCost()` + `ReadSacrificeOutcome(cost)` round out the opt-in / inspection surface. `RecruitmentOfficerFactory` (Modern Horizons 3 Creature — Human Soldier {W} 1/1) ships the 1/1 Human Soldier shape with (a) "can block as if it had reach" rider wired as a `KeywordAbility("Reach")` marker — `CombatAbilities.CanBlockFlying` honours it so blocker-legality matches printed Reach exactly (strict CR 702.9b "as if" distinction structurally invisible to the v1 combat surface — only consumer `CanBlockFlying` gets the right answer); and (b) `{2}{W}: Look at top 6, may reveal a creature card with mana value ≤ 2 to hand, rest to bottom in random order` wired as a single `ActivatedAbility` (CR 602) — resolution mirrors `AncientStirringsFactory.BuildResolveEffect` with eligibility predicate `HasType(Creature) && ManaCost.Parse(card.ManaCost).TotalValue ≤ 2` (CR 202.3), `PickSelector` delegate seam for tests, Fisher-Yates bottom (CR 701.20a). **Boros Energy pillar audit**: all 7 audit-list cards (Ajani Pariah, Guide of Souls, Ocelot Pride, Amped Raptor, Wrath of the Skies, Static Prison, Phlage) verified end-to-end at the named-factory level. Every dispatch test passes; every resolve body executes against the live energy ledger; no missing wiring discovered. Deferred riders documented per-card are uniformly cross-cutting infra gaps that gate broader card classes (back-face DFC characteristic-replacement on Ajani's transform, Ascend / city's blessing primitive on Ocelot Pride's 2-token branch, agent-driven "may" / target prompts on every tutor / opt-in path, deterministic RNG seam on every "random order" rider, reveal-event emission on every tutor) — not Boros-Energy-specific. Tests: `Majik.Core.Tests/CardData/VoltageSurgeTests.cs` (5 — identity + dispatch, no-sacrifice 2-damage + 2-energy via `SpellCastFlow.CastAsync`, with-sacrifice 4-damage + 2-energy, energy unconditional via direct `EffectFactory` invocation, `CanPay` legality gate) + `RecruitmentOfficerTests.cs` (15 — identity + dispatch, `CombatAbilities.CanBlockFlying` via Reach rider, activated ability shape, mv ≤ 2 eligibility theory across {W} / {1}{W} / {2}{W} / {W}{W}{W} + non-creature, first-eligible pick + bottom partition, no-eligible all-six-bottom, short library, empty library no-op, `DefaultPickSelector` first-eligible unit test). All 6577 Core + 304 Server + 149 Api + 135 Bot + 3 Bot.Integration tests pass. Coverage NamedFactory tier +2 (Voltage Surge + Recruitment Officer); Boros Energy pillar share lifts ~40% → ~80%. **Last updated:** 2026-05-25 (Splice onto Arcane primitive (CR 702.46) + Through the Breach / Goryo's Vengeance / Desperate Ritual retrofit. New `Majik.Core/Costs/SpliceOntoArcaneCost.cs` — sealed `IAdditionalCost` primitive carrying `(ICard arcaneTarget, ICard splicedCard, ManaCost spliceCost, Func<Player, IReadOnlyList<IEffect>> effectBuilder)`. `CanPay` / `Pay` gate on (a) `arcaneTarget.HasSubtype(CardSubtype.Arcane)` (CR 702.46 — splice only attaches to Arcane spells), (b) `splicedCard.Zone == ZoneType.Hand` (CR 702.46a — splice card revealed from hand), and (c) `caster.ManaPool.Pay(spliceCost).Success` for the splice mana. `Pay` drains splice mana via `caster.PayMana` and does NOT move the spliced card out of hand (CR 702.46a — "the card stays in your hand"). `BuildSplicedEffects(controller)` lazy-builds the rider's effect list at cast-flow injection time. New `CardSubtype.Arcane` enum slot (CR 205.3k). `SpellCastFlow.CastAsync` integration: right after `effects = definition.EffectFactory(chosen)` the cast flow scans `mergedAdditional.OfType<SpliceOntoArcaneCost>()` and appends each rider's `BuildSplicedEffects(caster)` output to the effect chain in announcement order (CR 702.46b — multi-rider splice concatenates in announcement order). Splice mana was already paid in the standard CR 601.2f additional-cost loop; the splice-effect injection is purely an effect-chain mutation. Existing CR 601.2g pre-check (`foreach (var pre in mergedAdditional) if (!pre.CanPay(caster)) throw`) gives clean rejection on non-Arcane target / non-hand-residence / under-funded splice cost — no zone movement, no partial mana drain. Retrofits: `DesperateRitualFactory` gains `WithSubtype(CardSubtype.Arcane)` on its `CardDef.Define()` + `BuildSpliceCost(arcaneTarget, splicedCard)` returning `SpliceOntoArcaneCost(target, this, ManaCost.Parse("{1}{R}"), BuildResolveEffect)`. `GoryosVengeanceFactory` gains `subtypes: [CardSubtype.Arcane]` on its `Instant` ctor + `BuildSpliceCost(target, this, ZoneService?, TriggerManager?)` returning `SpliceOntoArcaneCost(target, this, ManaCost.Parse("{2}{B}"), BuildResolveEffect)`. `ThroughTheBreachFactory` gains `subtypes: [CardSubtype.Arcane]` + new `SpliceOntoArcaneManaCost = "{1}{R}{R}{R}"` const + `BuildSpliceCost(target, this, ZoneService?, TriggerManager?, IPlayerAgent?)` returning `SpliceOntoArcaneCost(target, this, ManaCost.Parse("{1}{R}{R}{R}"), BuildResolveEffect)`. Pre-existing `KeywordAbility("Splice onto Arcane")` markers on these factories left in place — they're observational metadata for oracle audits / bot probes; the new primitive does NOT consult them (uses the typed `SpliceOntoArcaneCost` instance instead). New tests: `Majik.Core.Tests/Costs/SpliceOntoArcaneTests.cs` (18 — null-arg construction guards × 4, Arcane subtype assertions across the three retrofitted factories × 3, `CanPay` happy path, `CanPay` rejects non-Arcane target / non-hand-residence / insufficient mana × 3, `Pay` drains splice mana + leaves spliced card in hand, `Description` shape, `BuildSplicedEffects` returns rider effects, end-to-end cast of Goryo's Vengeance + Desperate Ritual splice resolves both effects, decline-splice path only runs base spell, non-Arcane target spell rejection short-circuits before mana drain). All 6551 Core + 304 Server + 149 Api + 135 Bot + 3 Bot.Integration tests pass. Coverage Primitive tier: Splice onto Arcane (CR 702.46) lands. CR 702.46 primitive unblocks every printed Splice-onto-Arcane card — the current ship list (Through the Breach, Goryo's Vengeance, Desperate Ritual) is retrofitted in this PR; future Splice cards (Glacial Ray, Reach Through Mists, Sosuke's Summons, etc.) slot in via a single `BuildSpliceCost(target, this)` line per factory. Cards-db is being removed — pure cost / cast-flow / factory + CardSubtype enum work, no JSON / SQL paths touched.)
**Last updated:** 2026-05-25 (Endbringer + Conduit of Ruin — Eldrazi Tron big-mana finishers. Two new factories under `Majik.Core/CardData/Factories/`. `EndbringerFactory` (Oath of the Gatewatch Creature — Eldrazi {5}{C} 5/5) ships Vigilance + Reach `KeywordAbility` markers (CR 702.20 / 702.17) plus three `ActivatedAbility` instances: (a) `{T}: 1 damage to any target` (`AdditionalCost.Tap` only, 1..1 "any target" `TargetRequest` with `BotIntent.Burn`, resolves via `Fx.DealDamageAny` so Planeswalker targets convert to loyalty removal per CR 306.7), (b) `{C}, {T}: target player draws a card` (`ManaCostCost("{C}") + AdditionalCost.Tap`, 1..1 "target player" with `BotIntent.Draw`, resolves via `Fx.DrawCards` so DrawCardIntent replacements participate; controller-fallback when no target chosen — Nihil Spellbomb posture), (c) `{C}, {T}: tap target creature` (same cost stack, 1..1 "target creature" with `BotIntent.Removal`, CR 608.2b recheck creature + battlefield then `Fx.Tap`; CR 701.21b — idempotent on already-tapped). The {C} pip is bucketed into generic by `ManaCost.Parse` today — same engine-wide colourless-payment gap as Eldrazi Temple (deferred). `ConduitOfRuinFactory` (Battle for Zendikar Creature — Eldrazi {6} 5/5) ships a cast trigger over `SpellCastEvent` filtered to `ReferenceEquals(e.Spell.Card, card)` with `activeZones = {Stack}` (same self-cast posture as Emrakul / Ulamog / Demilich) — resolution captures the actual caster from the event, filters the caster's library via pure helper `IsTutorCandidate` (`HasType(Creature) && CardColors.GetColors(c).Count == 0 && ManaCost.Parse(c.ManaCost).TotalValue >= 7` — CR 105.2 + CR 202.3b), prompts `IPlayerAgent.ChooseLibraryPickAsync` with deterministic-first-match fallback (Worldly Tutor posture), then `Library.RemoveCard` → `LibraryShuffle.ShuffleLibrary("conduit-of-ruin")` → `Library.InsertCardAt(0, pick)` so the tutored card lands on top of an otherwise-randomised library (CR 701.20a — Worldly Tutor / Mystical Tutor sequencing). Null pick / empty candidate list = no-op (CR 701.19a "may" — declining is permitted). Cost-reduction half ("first colorless creature spell you cast each turn costs {2} less") shipped as ALWAYS-ON `SpellCostReductionAbility` (predicate: colourless Creature spell; reduction: 2 unconditional) — per the factory brief, the per-turn-once primitive isn't built (TurnState has no by-type first-cast tracker; SpellCostReductionAbility has no usage counter), so v1 over-reduces for second/third casts in the same turn. CR 117.7c — coloured pips untouched (Reality Smasher {4}{C} → 3 generic remaining; bears {1}{G} unchanged); Conduit's own cast pays full {6} because reducers must be on the battlefield (CR 117.7 — casting copy is on the stack). `Create(Player)` shape-only + `Create(Player, TriggerManager?)` for bus-driven trigger registration. Pure helper `IsTutorCandidate(ICard)` exposed for tests + bot policies. Tests: `Majik.Core.Tests/CardData/EndbringerFactoryTests.cs` (11 — identity, dispatch, Vigilance/Reach markers, three activated abilities, tap-only damage ability cost shape, {C}+tap colourless cost shape on draw+tap abilities, damage to creature target, damage to player target, draw target player from library to hand, tap target creature, no-op on non-battlefield target) + `Majik.Core.Tests/CardData/ConduitOfRuinFactoryTests.cs` (15 — identity (colourless), dispatch, cast trigger ActiveZones=Stack, self-cast match, other-spell-cast non-match, IsTutorCandidate accepts colourless creature mv 7 + rejects mv 6 / coloured / artifact, cast trigger places qualifying Ulamog on top of library, no-qualifying-card no-op, cost reduction applies to colourless creature spells, cost reduction does NOT apply to coloured creature spells, cost reduction does NOT apply to colourless non-creature spells, cost reduction off-battlefield silent). All 7340 Core tests pass. Coverage NamedFactory tier +2 (Endbringer + Conduit of Ruin). Cards-db is being removed — pure factory work, no JSON / SQL paths touched. World Breaker is already shipped (Battle for Zendikar oracle text — Reach + ETB exile nonbasic land + attack-trigger exile coloured permanent + graveyard activation, see `WorldBreakerFactory` / coverage row).)

**Last updated:** 2026-05-25 (Mortivore + Nimbus Maze + Sea Gate Wreckage — Lhurgoyf-cycle Tarmogoyf sibling + Future Sight conditional dual + BFZ "no-hand" draw land. Three new factories under `Majik.Core/CardData/Factories/`. `MortivoreFactory` (Odyssey Creature — Lhurgoyf {3}{B}{B}) ships the */* Lhurgoyf shape mirroring `TarmogoyfFactory`'s cross-game graveyard CDA scope: Layer 7a `CdaPowerToughnessEffect` (CR 604.3 / 613.2) whose power AND toughness evaluators both return `MortivoreFactory.CountCreatureCards(graveyardSource())` — a linear `HasType(CardType.Creature)` tally across every graveyard in the game (callers supply `() => players.SelectMany(p => p.Zones.Graveyard.GetCards())`). Lifecycle (`MortivoreCdaLifecycle`) subscribes to `CardMovedEvent` and registers/unregisters the CDA on ETB / LTB — same shape as `TarmogoyfCdaLifecycle`. `{B}: Regenerate Mortivore` (CR 701.18 / 701.15a) wired as a proper `ActivatedAbility` with sole cost `ManaCostCost("{B}")`; resolution calls `card.AddRegenerationShield()` so shields stack across multiple activations (CR 701.15a — each regeneration creates a separate shield) and clear during cleanup (CR 514.2). Printed P/T seeded `0/0` since the CDA overwrites on every `Compute`. Pure helper `CountCreatureCards(IEnumerable<ICard>)` exposed for tests + bot policies. `NimbusMazeFactory` (Future Sight Land) ships the plain Land shape (no subtype, no supertype — Nimbus Maze never satisfies its own Plains/Island gate) + three `ManaAbility` instances: `{T}: Add {C}` vanilla (CR 605.1), `{T}: Add {W}` gated on `canActivateCheck: () => !land.IsTapped && ControllerControlsSubtype(land, Plains)`, and `{T}: Add {U}` gated on the Island variant. Subtype scan reads `land.Controller` live so control-change effects re-point the gate (CR 109.5). "A Plains" / "an Island" matches any permanent with that subtype on the controller's battlefield — basics, dual lands (Tundra), shocks (Hallowed Fountain), or any Plains/Island-typed permanent qualifies. Pure helper `ControllerControlsSubtype(Land, CardSubtype)` exposed. `SeaGateWreckageFactory` (Battle for Zendikar Land) ships the plain Land shape + unconditional ETB-tapped via `EntersTappedReplacement` on a supplied `ReplacementBus` (CR 614.1c — single-arg path omits) + `{T}: Add {C}` vanilla `ManaAbility` + `{2}, {T}: Draw a card` `ActivatedAbility` with cost stack `[ManaCostCost("{2}"), AdditionalCost.Tap(self)]` — resolution routes a single draw through `Fx.DrawCards` so any future `DrawCardIntent` replacements (Dredge, etc.) participate. The printed "Activate only if you have no cards in hand" restriction is exposed via `SeaGateWreckageFactory.HasNoCardsInHand(land)` predicate for activator / bot probing; not yet wired into `ActivatedAbility.CanActivate` because the engine doesn't yet expose a generic activation-legality closure on `ActivatedAbility` (same posture as `MagmaticChannelerFactory`'s delirium gate which surfaces the predicate for bot consumers pending the activation-legality surface landing). Tests: `MortivoreTests.cs` (10 — identity (Lhurgoyf {3}{B}{B}), dispatch, empty-graveyard 0/0, counts in controller's graveyard, cross-graveyard scan distinguishes Mortivore from Nethergoyf, pure-helper counts, regenerate-ability cost shape `{B}`, regenerate-ability adds shield on resolve, regenerate-shield-stacking via direct `AddRegenerationShield`) + `NimbusMazeTests.cs` (10 — identity (Land, no Plains/Island self-subtype), owner / controller, dispatch, three mana abilities, {C} always activatable when untapped, {W} gated off no-Plains / on-Plains, {U} gated off no-Island / on-Island, tap-cost belt-and-braces gate, pure-helper, opponent's Plains doesn't open Alice's gate) + `SeaGateWreckageTests.cs` (9 — identity, owner / controller, dispatch, ETB tapped, one {C} mana ability, one activated draw ability, cost stack {2} + tap-self, empty-hand pure-helper, draw resolves +1 card / -1 library). All 6679 Core tests + 304 Server pass. Coverage NamedFactory tier +3. Cards-db is being removed — pure factory work, no JSON / SQL paths touched.) **Prior:** (Field of the Dead + Ramunap Excavator + Wrenn and Six audit — two new factories under `Majik.Core/CardData/Factories/` extending the Loam / lands-matter pillar from PR #553 (Crucible of Worlds + Knight of the Reliquary). `FieldOfTheDeadFactory` (M20 Land) ships the plain non-basic Land shape + unconditional ETB-tapped via `EntersTappedReplacement` on a supplied `ReplacementBus` (CR 614.1c — same posture as Bojuka Bog / Sunscorched Desert) + `{T}: Add {C}` vanilla `ManaAbility` (CR 605.1) + land-ETB-under-controller `TriggeredAbility` (CR 603.1 / 603.6a) routed via `Triggers.OnLandEntersUnderControl(owner)` so EVERY land entering under Field's controller — including Field itself per printed "this land or another land" — qualifies; `interveningIf` (CR 603.4) gates on `CountDistinctlyNamedLands(controller) >= 7` (printed `ICard.Name` distinct count over `Battlefield.GetCards().Where(HasType(Land))`; CR 205.1 / no Layer 3 name-change effect surface today, same posture as Valakut's Mountain count). Resolve body re-checks the intervening-if (CR 603.4 second-pass), then creates a 2/2 black Zombie creature token via `TokenFactory.CreateOnBattlefield(new TokenSpec("Zombie", 2, 2, [Zombie], Colors: [Black]), controller, zones)` (CR 111.4 — explicit black colour identity stamped on `TokenColorsOverride`; ZoneService-routed when supplied so the token's own ETB `CardMovedEvent` fires for downstream listeners — Soul Warden / Amalia — but the Zombie is NOT a Land so it can't recursively re-fire Field's trigger). Pure helper `CountDistinctlyNamedLands(player)` exposed for tests + the gate. `RamunapExcavatorFactory` (Hour of Devastation Creature — Naga Cleric {1}{G}{G} 2/3) ships the creature analogue of Crucible of Worlds with identical permission-stamp semantics: `StaticAbility` marker "You may play lands from your graveyard." gated on `card.Zone == ZoneType.Battlefield` (CR 113.6e / 603.6e) + per-Land `Card.GrantPlayLandFromGraveyard` stamp on every Land in the controller's graveyard at construction time (snapshot path; CR 305.9 / 113.6c) + `(owner, eventBus)` overload subscribing to `CardMovedEvent` for live updates while Excavator is on the battlefield. Multiple Crucible / Ramunap Excavator / Conduit of Worlds permission sources are idempotent per CR 616 — the flag is per-LAND-card, not per-player. Engine change: new `CardSubtype.Naga` enum slot (Amonkhet snake-people lineage — CR 205.3m). Wrenn and Six audit pass: factory was previously shipped (`WrennAndSixFactory.cs` + `WrennAndSixTests.cs` from PR #178) — re-verified +1 (return-land-from-graveyard with deterministic auto-pick + "up to one" no-op tolerance + Land filter over mixed graveyard), -1 (loyalty cost wires legally, body is documented no-op pending engine's until-EOT batch-grant primitive for the reach + ping rider — CR 613.4), -7 (structural `Emblem` in the controller's command zone with retrace cost-replacement still deferred — emblem is shape-only). Coverage table row promoted from "+1 land return, -1 ping" placeholder to the full audit description. **Last updated:** 2026-05-25 (Underworld Breach + Mizzix's Mastery. Two new factories under `Majik.Core/CardData/Factories/`. `UnderworldBreachFactory` ships the Theros Beyond Death Enchantment {1}{R}; on ETB (via `CardMovedEvent` subscription) stamps `Card.GrantRuntimeEscape(card.ManaCostValue, 3)` on every nonland card in the controller's graveyard (CR 702.143). New `Card.RuntimeEscapeCost` / `Card.RuntimeEscapeExileCount` runtime-grant pair (parallel to `RuntimeFlashbackCost` / `RuntimeGraveyardCastCost`) — `GrantRuntimeEscape(cost, count)` / `ClearRuntimeEscape()`. `EscapeAltCostProbe.DefaultLookup` extended to consult the runtime stamp FIRST (precedence over the printed ship list) so granted escapes surface to the bot's alt-cost enumeration alongside Uro / Phlage / Phoenix of Ash / Cling to Dust. The actual cast reuses the existing `EscapeAlternativeCost` primitive + `SpellCastFlow` escape path — no new cast plumbing. LTB clears the stamps via a second `CardMovedEvent` handler so the grant is bounded to Breach's battlefield lifetime; both handlers self-unsubscribe on first matching fire. End-step sacrifice trigger (CR 500.4 / CR 603.1 / CR 701.16) wired via `Triggers.OnStepBegin(owner, End)`; resolution = `OracleSpellBinder.MoveToGraveyard(card, Sacrifice)`. `MizzixsMasteryFactory` ships the Commander 2015 Sorcery {3}{R}{R}; `BuildDefinition(caster, targetResolver, spellDefinitionLookup?, zoneService?)` returns a `SpellDefinition` with a 1..1 `TargetRequest` over instant/sorcery cards in the controller's graveyard. On resolve: the chosen card's bound `SpellDefinition` is looked up via the caller-supplied `spellDefinitionLookup` (production wires `ScryfallCardFactory.LookupSpellDefinition`); its `EffectFactory` runs with default `ChosenSpellParams` (no mode / X, empty targets, `ManaPayment.Empty`) — CR 707.10 lossy-v1 copy semantics inherited from `SpellCopier` (no real `IStackObject` push, no retarget). Original card moves Graveyard → Exile (routed through `ZoneService.MoveCard` when supplied; raw zone fallback otherwise). Tests: `UnderworldBreachTests` (8 — identity + dispatch, `ApplyGraveyardGrants` stamps nonland + skips land, `EscapeAltCostProbe.DefaultLookup` surfaces granted escape on non-ship-list card, ETB + LTB lifecycle via `ZoneService.MoveCard`, end-step sacrifice fires on controller's End step + skips opponent's End step, opponent-graveyard cards not stamped) + `MizzixsMasteryTests` (7 — identity + dispatch, `BuildDefinition` 1..1 target shape filters to instant/sorcery in controller's graveyard rejects creatures, resolve copies sentinel-effect + exiles original, empty-graveyard no-op, no-lookup still exiles, opponent's graveyard card fizzles). All 6393 Core + 304 Server + 149 Api + 135 Bot + 3 Bot.Integration tests pass. Coverage NamedFactory tier +2. Underworld Breach completes the storm-revival shell (cast → mill → escape combo); Mizzix's Mastery clears a Modern combo-piece staple. **Last updated:** 2026-05-25 (Narcomoeba + Prized Amalgam + Creeping Chill — Modern Dredge graveyard-recursion trio. Three new factories under `Majik.Core/CardData/Factories/`. `NarcomoebaFactory` (Future Sight Creature — Illusion {1}{U} 1/1) ships the 1/1 Illusion shape + `KeywordAbility("Flying")` marker (CR 702.9) + a graveyard-resident self-mill trigger (CR 603.6c) over `EventTriggerCondition<CardMovedEvent>` gated on `ReferenceEquals(e.Card, card) && e.FromZone == Library && e.ToZone == Graveyard` with `ActiveZones = {Graveyard}`. On resolve, "you may put it onto the battlefield" — when an `IPlayerAgent` is supplied, consult `ChooseYesNoAsync(BotIntent.Reanimate)`; null agent auto-accepts (Bloodghast / Arclight Phoenix posture). `ZoneService.MoveCard` routes Graveyard → Battlefield so ETB triggers + replacements fire (CR 603.6a) with raw-zone-mutation fallback. `PrizedAmalgamFactory` (Shadows over Innistrad Creature — Zombie Horror {3}{U/B} 3/3) ships the 3/3 Zombie Horror shape (printed `{3}{U/B}` hybrid pip stored as raw string — same posture as every Modern hybrid card) + graveyard-resident ETB trigger (CR 603.6d) over `CardMovedEvent` gated on `ToZone == Battlefield && e.Card.HasType(Creature) && !ReferenceEquals(e.Card, card) && ReferenceEquals(e.Card.Controller, owner)` with `ActiveZones = {Graveyard}` AND `interveningIf` re-checking Amalgam still in owner's graveyard (CR 603.4 belt-and-braces — same posture as Bridge from Below). On resolve, registers a `DelayedTriggeredAbility` (CR 603.7) on the supplied `TriggerManager` that fires on the next `StepStartedEvent` with `StepType == PhaseStateType.End && e.Timestamp > registeredAt` (Goryo's Vengeance timestamp-guard posture); delayed effect re-checks Amalgam's graveyard residence at resolve time (CR 608.2b — short-circuits duplicate registrations from multiple ETBs in the same turn), then routes Graveyard → Battlefield via `ZoneService.MoveCard` with a post-move `Permanent.Tap()` for the printed "tapped" rider. `CreepingChillFactory` (Guilds of Ravnica Sorcery {3}{B}) ships the Sorcery shape + `BuildResolveEffect(controller, opponents)` exposing the cast resolution body ("deals 3 damage to each opponent and you gain 3 life" — iterates the supplied opponent list, skips controller defensively, routes damage via `Fx.DealDamageAny` so Player / Planeswalker shape consistent; life gain via `Fx.GainLife`) + a graveyard-resident self-mill trigger (CR 603.6c) over the same Library→Graveyard reference-self condition shape as Narcomoeba, with `ActiveZones = {Graveyard}`. Mill-trigger resolution: `IPlayerAgent.ChooseYesNoAsync(BotIntent.LoseLife | BotIntent.Heal)` to "exile it" (auto-accept on null agent), `ZoneService.MoveCard` Graveyard → Exile, then re-runs the burn-and-life-gain body via shared `ApplyBurnAndLifeGain(controller, opponentResolver)` helper (CR 117.2 — equivalent text means equivalent effect). All three factories follow the standard two-overload shape: single-arg `Create(Player)` is shape-only (no service wiring), full-arg overload threads `ZoneService` / `TriggerManager` / `IPlayerAgent` / `opponentResolver` for end-to-end behaviour. Tests: `Majik.Core.Tests/CardData/NarcomoebaTests.cs` (5 — identity / Flying marker / dispatch / trigger shape / Library→Graveyard returns to battlefield / Hand→Graveyard rejected) + `PrizedAmalgamTests.cs` (7 — identity / dispatch / trigger shape / another-creature-ETB queues delayed return + end-step return tapped / opponent's creature ignored / self-ETB ignored / battlefield-zone gating) + `CreepingChillTests.cs` (8 — identity / dispatch / trigger shape / BuildResolveEffect dishes 3-each-opp + 3-life / controller-skip defensive / Library→Graveyard exiles + dishes + lifegains / Hand→Graveyard rejected). All 6473 Core + 304 Server + 149 Api + 135 Bot + 3 Bot.Integration tests pass. Coverage NamedFactory tier +3. Cards-db is being removed — pure factory work, no JSON / SQL paths touched. The three cards form the Modern Dredge / mill-reanimator core: Narcomoeba self-revives off Stinkweed Imp / Golgari Grave-Troll dredge mills, Creeping Chill drains 3 each-opp per mill (4 copies = 12 lifeloss + 12 life-gain without ever casting), Prized Amalgam piggybacks on every other zombie ETB (Bloodghast landfall returns, Narcomoeba mill returns, Bloodghast → graveyard → re-return loops) for end-step 3/3 attackers.)
