**Last updated:** 2026-05-25 (Dredge primitive (CR 702.52) + Life from the Loam + Stinkweed Imp + Golgari Grave-Troll. New `Majik.Core/Keywords/DredgeFactory.cs` — shared graveyard-anchored draw replacement builder. `Build(source, n, replacementBus?, eventBus?)` attaches a `KeywordAbility "Dredge"` marker carrying `Arg = N` and registers a `LambdaReplacement<DrawCardIntent>` on the supplied `ReplacementBus`. The replacement gates on (a) would-draw player matches `source.Owner` (CR 702.52 — "you" = source controller; uses owner since the card is in a graveyard / no separate controller), (b) `source.Zone == Graveyard` (CR 702.52a — Dredge functions only from the graveyard), (c) `owner.Library.Count >= N` (CR 702.52b — can't dredge with fewer cards than N), (d) agent yes via `IPlayerAgent.ChooseYesNoAsync` with `BotIntent.CardAdvantage` (the default heuristic accepts). On yes: mill N via `MillAction.Apply` (CR 701.13 — clean halt on mid-mill empty library; CR 704.5b empty-library loss flag NOT set because milling an empty library is not a draw from empty), return source Graveyard → Hand, cancel the underlying draw (return null). On no: pass intent through, underlying draw resolves. No-agent default = pass-through (conservative posture). The source-anchored gate means zone changes (return to hand, exile, reanimate, shuffle into library) silently disable the replacement without unregistering — re-entering the graveyard re-enables it (CR 614.6). New `Effects/DrawCardIntent.cs` — sealed record `DrawCardIntent(Player Player)` routed through `Fx.DrawCards` per individual draw when `player.Replacements` is non-null (per-draw routing so a "draw 3" with a single Dredge offer dredges once, draws twice). `LifeFromTheLoamFactory` (Ravnica Sorcery {1}{G}): Sorcery shape + Dredge 3 marker + `BuildResolveEffect(caster, landSelector?, zoneService?)` exposing the "return up to three target land cards from your graveyard to your hand" body (deterministic first-three-lands fallback, CR 608.2b illegal-on-resolution recheck filters non-lands + library-absent picks, ZoneService-routed when supplied). `StinkweedImpFactory` (Ravnica Creature — Imp {1}{B} 1/2): Flying marker + combat-damage-to-a-creature trigger (CR 510 / CR 603.1) over `CombatDamageDealtEvent` filtered to `e.Source == card && e.Target is Creature` (player + planeswalker targets gated out) destroying the victim via `Fx.MoveToGraveyard(victim, ZoneMoveReason.Destroy)` (Indestructible + Regeneration shields apply, CR 608.2b illegal-on-resolution clean no-op) + Dredge 5 marker. `GolgariGraveTrollFactory` (Ravnica Creature — Zombie Troll {3}{B}{G} base 0/0): ETB trigger (CR 603.6a / CR 614.1d retrofit) over `CardMovedEvent` `FromZone != Battlefield && ToZone == Battlefield` for this card, resolution counts Creature cards in controller's graveyard at resolve time and adds that many `CounterType.PlusOnePlusOne` counters via direct `Permanent.Counters.Add` (same shape as Murktide Regent's delve-counter retrofit; empty-graveyard branch dies to SBA after trigger per CR 704.5f) + Dredge 6 marker. Introduces `CardSubtype.Troll`. New `Fx.DrawCards` wiring: each individual draw constructs a `DrawCardIntent` and routes through `player.Replacements.Apply` when attached; null result skips the library → hand move for THIS draw only (the remaining draws in a "draw N" loop continue). New tests: `Majik.Core.Tests/Keywords/DredgeFactoryTests.cs` (10 — keyword + arg marker, argument validation (null source / non-positive N / owner-not-wired), shape-only no-bus path, agent yes mills N + source → hand + cancels draw, agent no straight draw + source stays in graveyard, library < N gates offer (agent NOT prompted), source not in graveyard skips, mid-mill empty library halts cleanly without empty-library loss flag, no-agent default = straight draw) + `Majik.Core.Tests/CardData/StinkweedImpTests.cs` (7 — identity (Imp 1/2 at {1}{B}), dispatch, Flying marker, Dredge 5 marker, combat trigger shape, combat-damage-to-creature destroys victim end-to-end, combat-damage-to-player does NOT fire) + `Majik.Core.Tests/CardData/GolgariGraveTrollTests.cs` (6 — identity (Zombie Troll 0/0 at {3}{B}{G}), dispatch, Dredge 6 marker, ETB trigger shape, ETB-counters one-per-Creature-card (3 creatures + 1 sorcery → 3 counters), empty-graveyard ETB places 0 counters) + `Majik.Core.Tests/CardData/LifeFromTheLoamTests.cs` (6 — identity (Sorcery at {1}{G}), dispatch, Dredge 3 marker, default selector picks first three lands + filters non-lands, empty-graveyard clean no-op, custom selector picks supplied lands). All 6408 Core + 304 Server + 149 Api + 135 Bot + 3 Bot.Integration tests pass. Coverage NamedFactory tier +3 (Life from the Loam / Stinkweed Imp / Golgari Grave-Troll). Cards-db is being removed — pure factory + Effects work, no JSON / SQL paths touched. CR 702.52 primitive unblocks every printed Dredge card (Stinkweed Imp / Golgari Grave-Troll / Life from the Loam ship today; future Dredge cards — Grave-Shell Scarab, Putrid Imp, Sprouting Thrinax wing, Greater Mossdog, Darkblast, Smolder Initiate, etc. — slot in via a single `DredgeFactory.Build(card, N, bus)` line).)

**Last updated:** 2026-05-25 (Talisman cycle — 10 artifacts (Mirrodin allied: Talisman of Progress / Dominance / Indulgence / Impulse / Unity. Mirrodin Besieged enemy: Talisman of Hierarchy / Curiosity / Resilience / Conviction / Creativity). New `Majik.Core/CardData/Factories/TalismanCycleFactory.cs` — parametric named-card factory (`[CardName(name, "A", "B")]`) covering all 10 with one class (PR pattern, mirrors `PainLandCycleFactory` / `HorizonLandCycleFactory` / `BounceLandCycleFactory` / `ChannelLandCycleFactory` / `FetchLandCycleFactory` / `FilterLandCycleFactory`). Each member ships: (1) `Artifact` identity with `PrintedManaCost = "{2}"` + owner/controller wiring, (2) `{T}: Add {C}` vanilla `ManaAbility` (no pain rider — printed oracle only attaches the damage clause to the coloured "Add A or B" mode; CR 605.1 + {C} folds into the generic bucket via `ManaCost.Parse` per CR 107.4c), (3) `{T}: Add {A}. <Name> deals 1 damage to you.` + `{T}: Add {B}. <Name> deals 1 damage to you.` — two separate `ManaAbility` instances per the same dual-modal split `PainLandCycleFactory`'s coloured modes use. Each coloured ability built via the additional-cost overload of `ManaAbility`: `canActivateCheck = !talisman.IsTapped`; `additionalCostPayer = controller.LoseLife(1)` running after the {T} tap (CR 120.3 — damage to a player causes loss of life equal to that damage, same `Player.LoseLife` route the painland cycle takes). v1 collapse: CR 605.1b "deals 1 damage to you" triggered-side-effect modelled as a non-mana activation cost since the engine's mana-source picker only consults activation legality, not in-flight triggers — keeps the bot's source picker honest about the cost without growing trigger awareness. CRITICALLY no life-floor gate (distinct from `HorizonLandBinder.AttachPayLifeMana`'s `LifeTotal > 1` check) — CR 119.4 only blocks "Pay X life" costs (you can't pay life you don't have), not damage, so the pain rider activates legally at 1 life and can drop you to 0; SBAs then handle loss (CR 704.5a). Same documented-deferred gap Mana Crypt / Manabarbs / Dark Confidant take: damage routes through `Player.LoseLife` not a `DamageDealtEvent`, so damage-prevention subscribers (Worship-style shields) don't intercept the 1. **Refactor**: the prior per-card `TalismanOfProgressFactory` (`Talisman of Progress` only) is removed in favour of the parametric `TalismanCycleFactory` — Progress's WU pair now lives as the cycle's first `[CardName]` attribute, and the old factory's tests (`TalismanOfProgressTests.cs`) are replaced by the new theory-driven `TalismanCycleFactoryTests.cs` (110 tests over `[Theory] [MemberData(nameof(AllTalismans))]` — identity + Artifact type + {2} mana cost + owner/controller wiring + dispatch via `NamedCardFactory` + 3-mana-ability shape + no-activated-or-triggered + colourless ability shape + per-coloured pain rider fires (loses 1 life and taps) + colourless mode does NOT lose life + tapped talisman cannot activate second ability + args validation + null-owner guard + Talisman-of-Progress-fallback overload + "can activate coloured at 1 life and drop to 0" gate sanity). All 6867 Core + 304 Server + 158 Api + 135 Bot + 3 Bot.Integration pass. Coverage NamedFactory tier +9 (Progress already counted; the 9 new talismans are net-new factories). Cards-db is being removed — pure factory work, no JSON / SQL paths touched. Talisman cycle is the artifact analogue of the Ice Age / Apocalypse painland family — Modern Tron / Eldrazi / mid-range / Goblin Charbelcher / Affinity mana base staple, and the standard 2-colour-pair {2}-cost rock alongside Mind Stone (mono-coloured), the Signets, and the Diamonds.)

**Last updated:** 2026-05-25 (Typed-cycling primitive (CR 702.32d) + Generous Ent retrofit + Krosan Tusker. New `Majik.Core/Keywords/TypedCyclingFactory.cs` — shared activated-from-hand builder for the typecycling family (Forestcycling / Slivercycling / Landcycling / etc.), parallel to `CyclingFactory` but resolves to a typed library tutor instead of a draw. `Build(source, cycleCost, predicate, typedKeyword, kindLabel?, eventBus?)` attaches both the typed `KeywordAbility` marker (e.g. "Forestcycling") AND the generic "Cycling" marker (CR 702.32d — typecycling IS Cycling, so CR 702.32d subscribers and oracle audits both surface) plus a single `ActivatedAbility` with cost stack `[cycleCost, DiscardSelfCost]` (CR 702.32a hand-zone gate). Resolve body: filter the controller's library by `predicate`, consult `IPlayerAgent.ChooseLibraryPickAsync` with deterministic first-match fallback (CR 701.19a — same posture as Stoneforge Mystic / Expedition Map / Sylvan Scrying), zone-move Library → Hand, shuffle via `LibraryShuffle.ShuffleLibrary` (CR 701.20a — shuffle still runs on empty candidate pool since the search occurred), then publish `CardCycledEvent` for CR 702.32d "Whenever a player cycles" subscribers (Lightning Rift / Astral Slide / Astral Drift / Decree of Justice fire uniformly across generic Cycling and every typecycling variant). The factory also exposes a public `TutorTypedCard(owner, predicate, kindLabel, shuffleReason)` helper — same tutor-and-shuffle body — for non-cycling-activated cards that share the typed-tutor primitive (Krosan Tusker's on-cycle trigger uses it for the basic-land lookup). `GenerousEntFactory` retrofit: replaced the v1 generic `CyclingFactory.Build(card, ManaCostCost({G}))` + shape-only `KeywordAbility("Forestcycling")` marker with a proper `TypedCyclingFactory.Build(card, ManaCostCost({G}), c => c.HasSubtype(CardSubtype.Forest), "Forestcycling", kindLabel: "Forest card", eventBus)` call — the "Forestcycling deferred" rider documented in PR #548 is now fully wired. New `KrosanTuskerFactory` (Onslaught Creature — Beast `{5}{G}{G}` 6/5): standard Cycling {2} via `CyclingFactory.Build` (the printed cycle is generic Cycling, not typed) plus a separate on-cycle `TriggeredAbility` over `EventTriggerCondition<CardCycledEvent>` gated to `ReferenceEquals(e.Card, card) && ReferenceEquals(e.Player, owner)` (self-cycle gate — same posture as Decree of Pain's self-cycle rider, distinct from Curator of Mysteries' "another card" gate) with `ActiveZones = {Graveyard}` (post-discard zone; cycling resolve has already moved hand → graveyard by the time the event publishes). Trigger-resolve invokes `TypedCyclingFactory.TutorTypedCard(owner, c => c.HasType(CardType.Land) && c.HasSupertype(CardSupertype.Basic), "basic land card", "krosan-tusker-cycle")` for the "search your library for a basic land card" body — same primitive Forestcycling uses, just with a different predicate. Beast subtype (already in `CardSubtype` enum). New tests: `Majik.Core.Tests/Keywords/TypedCyclingFactoryTests.cs` (15 tests — typed + generic keyword markers, cost-stack shape ({G} + DiscardSelfCost), Forestcycling end-to-end tutors a Forest + skips non-Forest noise, empty-candidate-pool clean no-op, `CardCycledEvent` publication when bus supplied + no-bus shape path, `TutorTypedCard` helper returns first match / null on empty / null on no-match, argument validation (null source / null cost / null predicate / empty typedKeyword / owner-not-wired)) + `Majik.Core.Tests/CardData/Factories/KrosanTuskerFactoryTests.cs` (8 tests — identity (Beast 6/5), dispatch, Cycling cost shape ({2} + DiscardSelfCost), on-cycle trigger subscribes to `CardCycledEvent` from graveyard, self-cycle gate fires only on this Krosan Tusker + rejects other-card and wrong-player cycles, tutor-body resolve picks the basic Forest and leaves the non-basic Stomping Ground + non-land Lightning Bolt in library, no-basic-land clean no-op, end-to-end cycle pays {2} + discards + draws + publishes `CardCycledEvent`) + retrofit `GenerousEntFactoryTests` end-to-end now asserts Forestcycling tutors the Forest and rejects non-Forest noise. All 6147 Core + 304 Server + 149 Api + 135 Bot + 3 Bot.Integration pass. Coverage NamedFactory tier +1 (Krosan Tusker — Generous Ent retrofitted, not net-new). Cards-db is being removed — pure factory work, no JSON / SQL paths touched. CR 702.32d primitive unblocks every printed typecycling card — Forestcycling / Plainscycling / Islandcycling / Swampcycling / Mountaincycling, Wizardcycling, Slivercycling, Landcycling — through one shared builder.)

**Last updated:** 2026-05-25 (Eldrazi creature pile — Eldrazi Mimic + Matter Reshaper + Endless One. Three new factories under `Majik.Core/CardData/Factories/`. `EldraziMimicFactory` ships the OGW Creature — Eldrazi {2} 2/1 with an ETB-other trigger (CR 603.1) over `EventTriggerCondition<CardMovedEvent>` gated on `ToZone == Battlefield` + entering card is a `CardType.Creature` + colourless via `CardColors.GetColors(e.Card).Count == 0` (CR 105 — honours `TokenColorsOverride` for explicit-colourless tokens) + printed mv via `ManaCost.TotalValue + PendingCastX` >= 4 (CR 202.3b — X variants honoured) + controller-match (`ReferenceEquals(e.Card.Controller, card.Controller)`) + `!ReferenceEquals(e.Card, card)` ("another" per CR 603.1). The predicate snapshots the entering creature into a per-trigger slot (`lastEntered`) consumed at resolve time; the resolve effect registers a new `BecomesPTUntilEndOfTurnEffect` (sibling of `BecomesPTEffect` with `ExpiresAtEndOfTurn = true` — `Majik.Core/Effects/BecomesPTUntilEndOfTurnEffect.cs`, Layer 7b set-base P/T per CR 613.7b, EOT-expirable per CR 514.2) carrying the source's CURRENT power and toughness (printed text reads "power and toughness", not base — so the snapshot honours buffs on the source). Mimic's own +1/+1 counters and Layer 7c pumps stack on top of the new base per the layer system. "You may" auto-accepts (same posture as Wurmcoil's mandatory tail). `MatterReshaperFactory` ships the OGW Creature — Eldrazi Drone {3}{C} 3/2 with a dies trigger (CR 603.6c / CR 700.4) via `Triggers.OnDies(card)` with `ActiveZones = {Battlefield, Graveyard}` (Wurmcoil posture — gate matches after `ZoneService` has already moved the card to the graveyard before the `CardMovedEvent` publish). Resolution peeks the controller's top library card (`Library.GetCards().FirstOrDefault()`), publishes `CardRevealedEvent` when an `IEventBus` is supplied (CR 701.16), then branches on type + mv: permanent card (CR 110.4a — `HasType` Artifact / Creature / Enchantment / Land / Planeswalker — excludes Instant + Sorcery) AND mv via `ManaCost.Parse(top.ManaCost ?? string.Empty).TotalValue <= 3` → Library → Battlefield (under Matter Reshaper's controller per CR 110.2a; `ZoneService.MoveCard` threads the controller arg so downstream ETB triggers fire), otherwise Library → Hand. Empty library → clean no-op. `EndlessOneFactory` ships the BFZ Creature — Eldrazi {X} 0/0 with an ETB self-trigger (CR 603.6a) via `Triggers.OnEnterBattlefieldSelf(card)` whose resolve reads `card.PendingCastX ?? 0` (stamped by `SpellCastFlow` right after `ChooseXAsync` — Chalice of the Void's PendingCastX-consume posture) and routes the placement through `CountersService.Add(card, CounterType.PlusOnePlusOne, x, replacements)` so Hardened Scales / Doubling Season replacements bump the amount before commit (CR 614 / CR 121.2); `ClearPendingCastX` after the read so re-entries (blink, copy) don't double-count — non-cast entry leaves Endless One as a 0/0 with no counters, SBAs (CR 704.5f) immediately put it in the graveyard, matching the printed behaviour. All three two-overload shape (shape-only `Create(Player)` + fully-wired with ContinuousEffectsService / ZoneService+IEventBus+TriggerManager / TriggerManager+ReplacementBus respectively) mirror Wurmcoil / Thought-Knot Seer / Chalice of the Void posture. All three dispatch via the `[CardName]` source-gen rail. Tests: `Majik.Core.Tests/CardData/EldraziMimicFactoryTests.cs` (10 — identity + colourless self-check via `CardColors.GetColors`, dispatch, trigger shape, predicate accepts mv-4 colourless under control, rejects coloured / mv-3 / opponent-controlled / self-entering, resolve copies source P/T and EOT sweep restores base, shape-only no-effects-service no-throw), `Majik.Core.Tests/CardData/MatterReshaperFactoryTests.cs` (9 — identity + Drone subtype, dispatch, active-zones includes Graveyard, condition matches BF→GY rejects BF→Hand, mv-3 permanent → battlefield, mv-4 permanent → hand, Instant any-mv → hand, Land mv-0 → battlefield, empty library no-op), `Majik.Core.Tests/CardData/EndlessOneFactoryTests.cs` (7 — identity + `HasX`, dispatch, ETB trigger count, X=3 → 3 counters + PendingCastX cleared, X=0 → 0 counters + still cleared + base 0/0, non-cast null PendingCastX no-op, ReplacementBus +1 bump bumps X=2 placement to 3 via CountersService.Add). All 6193 Core + 304 Server + 149 Api + 135 Bot + 3 Bot.Integration tests pass. Coverage NamedFactory tier +3 (Eldrazi Mimic / Matter Reshaper / Endless One). New `BecomesPTUntilEndOfTurnEffect` primitive unblocks every other "becomes a P/T until end of turn" rider whose source already has the Creature row (distinct from manlands which pair P/T set-base with Layer 4 type-add via per-factory `*BecomesPTEffect` shims). Cards-db is being removed — pure factory + Effects work, no JSON / SQL paths touched.)
**Last updated:** 2026-05-25 (Furnace of Rath + Angrath's Marauders + Gisela, Blade of Goldnight — damage-doubling cycle. Three new factories under `Majik.Core/CardData/Factories/`. `FurnaceOfRathFactory` (Tempest Enchantment {2}{R}{R}) ships the printed symmetric all-damage doubling clause — single `DamageDoubleReplacement` registration on the supplied `ReplacementBus` with an always-true predicate (gated only on the card being on the battlefield via `card.Zone == ZoneType.Battlefield`). `AngrathsMaraudersFactory` (Ixalan Creature — Human Pirate {4}{R}{R} 4/4) ships the 4/4 Human Pirate shape + asymmetric doubling clause — single `DamageDoubleReplacement` gated on `card.Zone == ZoneType.Battlefield` AND source-controlled-by-Marauders-controller (Card via `Card.Controller`, Player by reference-equality) AND target-is-opponent-or-opponent's-permanent (the same target predicate Gisela reuses). `GiselaBladeOfGoldnightFactory` (Avacyn Restored Legendary Creature — Angel {4}{R}{W}{W} 5/5) ships Flying + First Strike + Lifelink `KeywordAbility` markers + two replacement clauses: (a) doubling — `DamageDoubleReplacement` gated on opponent-or-opponent's-permanent target (no source-controller restriction in Gisela's printed text, distinct from Angrath's); (b) halving — single new `DamageHalveRoundedUpReplacement` primitive (`Majik.Core/Effects/DamageHalveRoundedUpReplacement.cs`) gated on controller-or-controller's-permanent target, rewrites `Amount` to `(amount + 1) / 2` modelling "prevent half that damage, rounded up" (CR 615). Engine refactor: `DamageDoubleReplacement` extracted from `InquisitorsFlailFactory.cs` to its own file under `Majik.Core/Effects/` as a generic predicate-based primitive (`new DamageDoubleReplacement(Func<DamageIntent, bool> predicate)`); Flail's two registrations rewritten as lambda-predicate composers — identical bus-side behaviour. Per-effect dedup on the bus (CR 616.1c) lets the new doublers stack: two Furnaces quadruple; Furnace + Angrath's quadruple damage you deal to opponents; Furnace + Gisela quadruple opponent-side damage and net ~1x on controller-side (Furnace 2x → Gisela halve rounded up). CR 616 player-choice ordering deferred (registration order wins). Two-overload shape across all three: single-arg `Create(Player)` is shape-only (no bus → no replacement registration); `Create(owner, ReplacementBus?)` for fully-wired behaviour. Cards-db is being removed — pure factory + Effects work, no JSON / SQL paths touched. Tests: `Majik.Core.Tests/CardData/Factories/FurnaceOfRathFactoryTests.cs` (9) + `AngrathsMaraudersFactoryTests.cs` (12) + `GiselaBladeOfGoldnightFactoryTests.cs` (19 — including a `[Theory]` table over 7 rounding inputs). All 6148 Core + 304 Server + 149 Api + 135 Bot + 3 Bot.Integration pass. Coverage NamedFactory tier +3.)

**Last updated:** 2026-05-25 (Filter Land cycle — 10 lands (Shadowmoor allied: Mystic Gate / Sunken Ruins / Graven Cairns / Fire-Lit Thicket / Wooded Bastion. Eventide enemy: Cascade Bluffs / Twilight Mire / Fetid Heath / Rugged Prairie / Flooded Grove). New `Majik.Core/CardData/Factories/FilterLandCycleFactory.cs` — parametric named-card factory (`[CardName(name, "A", "B")]`) covering all 10 with one class (PR pattern, mirrors `PainLandCycleFactory` / `HorizonLandCycleFactory` / `BounceLandCycleFactory` / `ChannelLandCycleFactory` / `FetchLandCycleFactory`). Each member ships: (1) Land identity (non-Basic, no subtype), (2) `{T}: Add {C}` vanilla `ManaAbility` (no extra cost — the {1} rider applies only to the filter modes), (3) THREE separate `ManaAbility` instances for the printed filter mode `{1}, {T}: Add {A}{A}, {A}{B}, or {B}{B}` — one slot each for `{A}{A}`, `{A}{B}`, `{B}{B}` (mirrors Aether Hub / Cavern of Souls / Springleaf Drum's per-colour fan-out for "Add one mana of any colour" abilities — bots/agents pick the slot whose produced mana matches the cost they're paying). Each filter mode built via the additional-cost overload of `ManaAbility`: `canActivateCheck = !land.IsTapped && controller.ManaPool.CanPay({1})`, `additionalCostPayer = controller.PayMana({1})` running after the {T} tap. CR 605.1 — still mana abilities (don't use the stack); the {1} extra cost is paid as part of activation, atomically with the {T} tap. Activation requires {1} to already be in the mana pool (no auto-tap "mana-fixer" planner — same posture every other additional-mana-cost activated ability takes: Mind Stone's draw cost, Springleaf Drum, etc.). Net mana per filter activation: -1 generic + 2 coloured pips = +1 mana plus a generic→coloured conversion (the signature filter-land tempo curve, e.g. 1G → WW for Wooded Bastion). New tests: `Majik.Core.Tests/CardData/Factories/FilterLandCycleFactoryTests.cs` (153 tests over `[Theory] [MemberData(nameof(AllFilterLands))]` — identity + dispatch via `NamedCardFactory` + 4-mana-ability shape (one {C} + three filter modes) + per-mode discovery (`{A}{A}`, `{A}{B}`, `{B}{B}`) + filter modes can't activate without {1} in pool + filter modes activate cleanly with seeded {1} + per-mode activation pays {1} and adds the two-pip output via `ManaAbilityActivator` (separate AA / AB / BB cases) + colourless mode does NOT require {1} + tapped land can't activate any filter mode + args validation + null-owner guard + Mystic-Gate-fallback overload). All 6261 Core + 304 Server + 149 Api + 135 Bot + 3 Bot.Integration pass. Coverage NamedFactory tier +10. Cards-db is being removed — pure factory work, no JSON / SQL paths touched. Modal-mana-ability primitive (collapsing the three slots back into one printed modal ability) deferred — functionally equivalent for payment today.)

**Last updated (prior):** 2026-05-25 (`DamageIntent.IsCombatDamage` flag + Inquisitor's Flail damage-doubling retrofit. Engine change: `Majik.Core/Effects/DamageIntent.cs` grows a `bool IsCombatDamage { get; init; }` init-prop (default `false`) discriminating combat damage (CR 510.1) from every other damage-dealing path (spell resolution, activated / triggered abilities, ping effects). `Majik.Core/Combat/CombatFlow.cs` stamps `IsCombatDamage = true` on every intent it pumps through `ReplacementBus` — all three `DealDamageTo{Creature,Player,Planeswalker}` routes — so combat-damage-only replacements can gate cleanly without guessing from source / target shape. Non-combat construction sites (`DamageSpellFactory`, Soul-Scar Mage's existing rider, the `PreventNextN*` shield family) leave the flag at its default `false`. `InquisitorsFlailFactory` retrofit: PR #543 shipped Equip {2} + structural shape only with the damage-doubling clauses deferred. This PR closes the gap — `Create(Player, ReplacementBus?)` overload registers two new `DamageDoubleReplacement` instances against the supplied bus: (1) source-side ("if equipped creature would deal combat damage, it deals double that damage instead" — gates on `intent.Source == AttachedTo` AND `IsCombatDamage`) and (2) target-side ("if a source would deal combat damage to equipped creature, it deals double that damage instead" — gates on `intent.TargetCreature == AttachedTo` AND `IsCombatDamage`). Both replacements gate on the Flail being on the battlefield AND currently attached, so detach / blink / bounce auto-suspends doubling without explicit deregistration. The single-arg `Create(Player)` dispatcher path stays shape-only (no bus → no replacements). New `DamageDoubleReplacement` (alongside the factory in `Majik.Core/CardData/Factories/InquisitorsFlailFactory.cs`) is the generic "double a combat-damage intent matching a per-side predicate" replacement primitive; future retrofits (Furnace of Rath, Gisela the Broken Blade, Boros Charm's third mode) can lean on the same shape with their own gates. Tests: `Majik.Core.Tests/CardData/Factories/InquisitorsFlailFactoryTests.cs` (10 — identity + dispatch + Equip {2} cost shape + shape-only path no-replacements + source-side combat damage to player doubled + source-side combat damage to creature doubled + source-side non-combat ping not doubled + target-side combat damage doubled + target-side non-combat Lightning Bolt not doubled + detach suspends + unequipped no-op) + new `CombatFlowTests.CombatDamageIntent_IsStampedWithIsCombatDamage` (captures every intent CombatFlow pushes through a probe `ReplacementBus`; asserts every intent has `IsCombatDamage = true`). All 6106 Core + 304 Server + 149 Api + 135 Bot + 3 Bot.Integration pass. Coverage NamedFactory tier unchanged (retrofit-only on Inquisitor's Flail; engine surface +1 init-prop). Cards-db is being removed — pure engine work, no JSON / SQL paths touched.)

**Last updated:** 2026-05-25 (Inquisitor's Flail + Shadowspear + Hammer Time pillar audit. Two new factories under `Majik.Core/CardData/Factories/`. `InquisitorsFlailFactory` (Innistrad Artifact — Equipment {2}) ships the structural shape + Equip {2} via the shared `EquipActivatedAbility` primitive with the Puresteel zero-equip cost-provider hook (same wiring as Bone Saw / Colossus Hammer / Skullclamp / Sword of Fire and Ice). The two printed damage-doubling replacements ("If equipped creature would deal combat damage, it deals double that damage instead" + the symmetric incoming clause) are deferred: `ReplacementBus` + `DamageIntent` are the right entry point and combat damage already flows through `CombatFlow.Apply{ToCreature|ToPlaneswalker|ToPlayer}`, but `DamageIntent` carries no `IsCombatDamage` discriminator today (CR 510.1c) — a clean "combat-damage only" filter either misfires on non-combat damage from creature sources or requires widening the intent record. `ShadowspearFactory` (Theros Beyond Death Legendary Artifact — Equipment {1}) ships the full shape: static "+1/+1 + Trample + Lifelink" via paired `AttachedBoostEffect` registrations — `(card, +1, +1)` at Layer 7c (CR 613.7c) + parallel `AttachedBoostEffect(card, 0, 0, grantedKeywords: ["Trample", "Lifelink"], layer: Layer.Abilities)` at Layer 6 (CR 613.1c), same paired-effect shape as `CoriSteelCutterFactory`'s "+1/+1 + Trample + Haste"; activated `{1}, {T}: Target creature loses indestructible and hexproof until end of turn` wired as `ActivatedAbility` with `ManaCostCost("{1}") + AdditionalCost.Tap(card)` + 1..1 `TargetRequest("target creature")` — resolve closure registers TWO new `LoseKeywordUntilEndOfTurnEffect` instances (one per stripped keyword) against the supplied `ContinuousEffectsService` (CR 514.2 cleanup recovery via base `ExpiresAtEndOfTurn = true`); Equip {1} via the same `EquipActivatedAbility` primitive. New `LoseKeywordUntilEndOfTurnEffect` (sibling of `GrantKeywordUntilEndOfTurnEffect` in `Effects/UntilEndOfTurnEffects.cs`) is the generic "strip one named keyword from one creature EOT" Layer 6 ability-removing effect — distinct from `LoseKeywordEffect` which gates on a source permanent's `AttachedTo` dynamically (equipment-style "equipped creature loses X"). Hammer Time audit: shipped Stoneforge Mystic + Colossus Hammer (+10/+0 + lose-flying) + Sigarda's Aid (flash-grant + ETB-attach) + Puresteel Paladin (Equipment-ETB draw + zero-equip on ≥3 artifacts) + Sword of Fire and Ice (markers + combat draw/burn) + Sword of Feast and Famine (protection grants + combat untap-lands/discard) + Skullclamp + Umezawa's Jitte + Cori-Steel Cutter + Hammer of Nazahn + Bone Saw + Memnite + Ornithopter + Phyrexian Walker + Esper Sentinel; deferred riders are the Equip attach-target prompt (every equipment uses deterministic first-creature picker), Sword of Fire and Ice's full protection-grant scoping (markers-on-the-card path; DEBT-A grant deferred for SFAI specifically), Sword of Feast and Famine's discard prompt, and now Inquisitor's Flail's combat-damage doubling pending an `IsCombat` flag on `DamageIntent`. Tests: `Majik.Core.Tests/CardData/Factories/InquisitorsFlailFactoryTests.cs` (4 — identity + dispatch + Equip {2} cost shape + abilities-list shape) + `Majik.Core.Tests/CardData/Factories/ShadowspearFactoryTests.cs` (7 — identity + dispatch + Equip {1} cost + equipped Bear +1/+1+Trample+Lifelink + detach revoke + activated keyword-strip removes Indestructible+Hexproof + EOT expiry restores + strip activated cost shape). **Last updated:** 2026-05-25 (Caustic Caterpillar + Bone Splinters + Cabal Therapy audit — sacrifice-themed removal batch. Standard / Modern allied + enemy dual-colour mana base pillar — alongside the Ravnica shocklands and Innistrad fastlands, check lands are the canonical 2-colour-pair midrange land for Modern Jund / 4-Color Death's Shadow / G/W toolbox shells.)

Empty-pile clean no-op (CR 701.19a — decline-to-find legal); shuffle still fires. `ChromaticStarFactory` (Lorwyn / TSR Artifact {1}) ships five `ManaAbility` instances (one per WUBRG) for `{T}, Sacrifice: Add one mana of any color` — same shape as Lotus Petal / Mox Opal; `canActivateCheck = !IsTapped && Zone == Battlefield`, `additionalCostPayer` sacrifices inline (CR 701.16). LTB draw trigger via `Triggers.OnDies(card)` (permanent-agnostic despite the name — battlefield → graveyard CardMovedEvent self-move; `activeZones = {Battlefield, Graveyard}` so the gate matches pre- or post-move, mirrors Wurmcoil Engine); resolution `Fx.DrawCards(controller, 1)`. Two overloads: shape-only `Create(Player)` + `(Player, TriggerManager?)` for bus-driven LTB firing. `TalismanOfProgressFactory` (Mirrodin Artifact {2}) ships three `ManaAbility` instances: painless `{T}: Add {C}` plus painful `{T}: Add {W}` and `{T}: Add {U}` (each with `additionalCostPayer = p.LoseLife(1)` — same painland additional-cost shape as `HorizonLandBinder.AttachPayLifeMana`). v1 collapse: CR 605.1b "deals 1 damage to you" triggered-side-effect modelled as a non-mana activation cost since the engine's mana-source picker only consults activation legality — keeps the bot's source picker honest about the cost without growing trigger awareness. No `LifeTotal > 1` gate (distinct from Horizon Canopy) — damage may take controller below 0, SBAs handle the loss. Single-modal-colour ability collapse + true CR 605.1b trigger surface + the other four Mirrodin talismans deferred. New tests: `Majik.Core.Tests/CardData/{MindStone,ExpeditionMap,ChromaticStar,TalismanOfProgress}Tests.cs` (28 tests — identity + `NamedCardFactory` dispatch per card, ability-shape counts, cost-shape assertions, resolve behaviours including: Mind Stone cantrip draws + sacrifices + empty-library loss flag, Expedition Map tutors first land + non-lands stay in library + empty-library still sacrifices + finds nonbasic Urza-style lands, Chromatic Star activates per-colour + sacrifices + sibling abilities lock out + LTB condition matches BF→GY only / not bounce / not exile + resolve draws, Talisman painless {C} no life loss + {W}/{U} cost 1 life each + tapped can't reactivate). **Last updated:** 2026-05-25 (MH2 Incarnation cycle audit — Solitude / Endurance / Grief / Fury / Subtlety. Pure audit, no behavioural code changes; all five factories already ship end-to-end with the shared evoke wiring. Per-card identity + wiring confirmation: (1) **Solitude** `{3}{W}{W}` 3/2 Elemental Incarnation — Flash + Lifelink + Evoke keyword markers (CR 702.8 / 702.15 / 702.74), `EvokeFactory.Build` self-sacrifice trigger (CR 702.74b) gated on `Creature.EvokeWasPaid` (set by `EvokeAlternativeCost.OnResolved`), ETB exile-one-target-creature trigger with a 0..1 `TargetRequest` ("up to one") whose `CandidateGatherer` enumerates the live battlefield minus Solitude itself (`BotIntent.Removal`), lifegain to the exiled creature's controller equal to its snapshot power (CR 112.7a last-known-information after the move). (2) **Endurance** `{1}{G}{G}` 3/4 Elemental Incarnation — Flash + Reach + Evoke keyword markers (CR 702.8 / 702.9 / 702.74), shared evoke sacrifice trigger, ETB graveyard → library shuffle trigger with a 1..1 "target player" request (any player including controller for self-recycle); local Fisher-Yates RNG mirroring Surgical Extraction's deterministic shuffle deferral (`IZone` shuffle hook still pending engine-wide). (3) **Grief** `{2}{B}` 3/2 Elemental Incarnation — Menace + Evoke keyword markers (CR 702.110 / 702.74), shared evoke sacrifice trigger, ETB target-opponent reveal-and-discard trigger; v1 deterministically picks the first nonland card in the revealed hand (mirrors `ThoughtseizePatternTemplate`'s caster-choice deferral). (4) **Fury** `{3}{R}` 3/3 Elemental Incarnation — Double strike + Evoke keyword markers (CR 702.4 / 702.74), shared evoke sacrifice trigger, ETB damage-distribution trigger with X = cards in controller's hand at resolution (Fury itself already left the hand on the stack so no self-count pitfall); `distribute: Func<Player, int, IReadOnlyDictionary<Permanent, int>>` parameter for caller-supplied allocation + "all X to first target" default fallback (CR 119.4 real distribute-damage prompt deferred until the agent surface ships). (5) **Subtlety** `{3}{U}` 3/3 Elemental Incarnation — Flash + Evoke keyword markers (CR 702.8 / 702.74), shared evoke sacrifice trigger, ETB bounce-target-opponent-creature/planeswalker-and-look trigger with 1..1 `TargetRequest`; bounced permanent's owner runs `ScryAction.Peek(target, 1)` + `ScryAction.ScryDecision` via `AgentRegistry.Get` (`may put on bottom` — defaults to top when no agent registered). **Evoke alt-cost wiring**: all five route through `EvokeAlternativeCost` (CR 702.74 + CR 117.11) at cast time — `KeywordBinder` does NOT parse the evoke cost generically (Scryfall encoding limitation, see `Majik.Core/CardData/KeywordBinder.cs` line 39-43); the alt-cost is constructed by the cast pipeline with the pitched card and routes the flag-set on `OnResolved`. `EvokeFactory` is generic and DOES attach via `KeywordBinder` when the data path runs (`ActiveZones: [Battlefield, Graveyard]` so the trigger survives the sacrifice's zone change). **Audit conclusion**: no gaps to close — every documented deferred rider blocks on an engine-wide feature (opponent-prompt during another player's spell resolution for Solitude/Grief pitch-back, real distribute-damage prompt for Fury, `IZone.Shuffle` hook for Endurance, private-info "look at" channel for Subtlety) and is appropriate to land as a separate engine pass rather than per-card hack. All 45 existing tests across `SolitudeFactoryTests` (8) / `EnduranceTests` (8) / `GriefTests` (6) / `FuryFactoryTests` (7) / `SubtletyFactoryTests` (7) + `EvokeAlternativeCostTests` (9) pass on full-solution build. Coverage NamedFactory tier unchanged (audit-only).)

**Last updated:** 2026-05-25 (Tutor / fetch routing through `ZoneService` — engine-wide ETB-trigger / replacement-effect fix. Closes the gap flagged in the prior Sakura-Tribe Scout + Explore + Amulet Titan engine audit: tutor-then-place-onto-battlefield factories (`PrimevalTitanFactory`, `ScapeshiftFactory`, `FetchLandCycleFactory`, `SearchSpellFactory.SearchLandToBattlefieldSpell` / `GreenSunsZenithSpell` — covering `SearchForTomorrowFactory`, every `[CardName]` fetchland, and any `SearchLandToBattlefield*Template`-bound spell) previously did raw `Zones.Library.RemoveCard` + `Zones.Battlefield.AddCard` mutation, bypassing `ZoneService.MoveCard`'s `CardMovedEvent` publication + `ReplacementBus` hook. Consequences pre-fix: bounce-land ETB self-bounce did NOT fire on Titan / Scapeshift / Fetchland-tutored Karoos; Amulet of Vigor's "Whenever a permanent enters tapped under your control, untap it" trigger did NOT fire on Titan-fetched tapped lands; ETB-tapped replacement effects on shock lands / bounce lands never applied to tutored arrivals — the printed Amulet Titan combo line only worked through cast-side land drops, never through the Titan's own fetch. Fix: new `Majik.Core/Services/ZoneServiceRegistry.cs` (parallel to `AgentRegistry` / `GameRandomRegistry` / `EventBusRegistry` — process-wide per-player lookup populated by `GameDriver` at game-start, cleared at test teardown) lets the tutor closure (built at card-construction time, executed at spell resolution) look up the live `ZoneService` without forcing a parameter-threading rewrite through `SpellDefinition.EffectFactory`. Retrofitted factories now consult `ZoneServiceRegistry.Get(caster)` — when a live service is registered the Library → Battlefield move routes through `ZoneService.MoveCard` so `CardMovedEvent` publishes + the `ReplacementBus` runs (CR 603.6a / CR 614). When nothing is registered (single-arg dispatcher / shape-test path) the closure falls back to raw zone mutation, so existing shape tests continue to pass unchanged. `PrimevalTitanFactory.MoveToBattlefieldTapped` taps the land AFTER the `ZoneService` move so the bounce-land / shock-land ETB-tapped replacement has already applied — double-tapping is a no-op (`Permanent.Tap` throws if already tapped; the new code guards `!perm.IsTapped` before tapping). Amulet of Vigor's untap trigger queues off the same `CardMovedEvent` because `ZoneService.MoveCard` calls `Permanent.Tap` BEFORE publishing the event, so the trigger's `IsTapped` predicate evaluates true at event-fire time (CR 614.6 — replacements fully resolve before triggers see the event). `ChordOfCallingFactory` / `GreenSunsZenithFactory` / `EldritchEvolutionFactory` / `UroTitanFactory` already had optional `ZoneService?` params on their `BuildSpellDefinition` entry points; updated to fall back to `ZoneServiceRegistry.Get(caster)` when the caller-supplied service is null — closes the cast-flow path where bots / `OracleSpellBinder` invoke `BuildSpellDefinition` without an explicit service ref. `GameDriver` deliberately does NOT call `ZoneServiceRegistry.SetDefault` (only per-player `Set(p, _zoneService)`) — keeps unrelated tests (which create a fresh `Player` and don't seed the registry) from inadvertently inheriting a stale `ZoneService` from a concurrently-running test collection. Tests: new `Majik.Core.Tests/CardData/Factories/PrimevalTitanZoneServiceTests.cs` (4 tests — Primeval Titan tutors Simic Growth Chamber via `ZoneService` → bounce trigger queues on the live `TriggerManager`; Amulet of Vigor + Primeval Titan tutors Simic Growth Chamber → bounce-land ETB-tapped replacement applies + post-move tap is no-op + Amulet's untap trigger queues alongside the bounce trigger (CR 614 / CR 603.6a); direct `CardMovedEvent` observation verifies the tutor publishes one event with `FromZone=Library` + `ToZone=Battlefield`; no-registered-service fallback path produces the same end state via raw zone mutation so existing dispatcher / shape tests stay green). All 5748 Core + 304 Server + 149 Api + 135 Bot + 3 Bot.Integration tests pass. Coverage NamedFactory tier unchanged (engine retrofit, not new factories). Cards-db is being removed — pure engine work, no JSON / SQL paths touched. Unblocks Amulet Titan combo lines: Titan ETB → fetches Simic Growth Chamber tapped → Karoo's ETB self-bounce returns the originally-played land → Amulet of Vigor untaps Simic Growth Chamber → tapped-Karoo state cleared → bot evaluator sees the bounce-and-untap tempo correctly. Same fix sequence covers Scapeshift's "sacrifice N lands → fetch N lands" Karoo abuse and Search for Tomorrow's suspend / hardcast paths.)

**Last updated:** 2026-05-25 (Sakura-Tribe Scout + Explore + Amulet Titan engine audit. Two new factories under `Majik.Core/CardData/Factories/`: `SakuraTribeScoutFactory` (Champions of Kamigawa {G} Creature — Snake Scout 1/1) ships the printed `{T}: You may put a land card from your hand onto the battlefield.` as a proper `ActivatedAbility` with sole cost `AdditionalCost.Tap(card)` (CR 605.1 — NOT a mana ability; effect doesn't add mana to a pool); resolution prompts the controller's agent via `IPlayerAgent.ChooseYesNoAsync(BotIntent.Ramp)` for the "you may" opt-in then `IPlayerAgent.ChooseFromHandAsync(controller, lands, BotIntent.Ramp)` for which land card; deterministic no-agent fallback auto-accepts + first land. Hand → battlefield routes through `ZoneService.MoveCard` when supplied so ETB triggers + replacements on the played land fire (CR 603.6a / CR 614) — the Amulet Titan engine bridge: bounce-land ETB self-bounce + Amulet of Vigor's tapped-ETB trigger both fire off the published `CardMovedEvent`. NOT a CR 305.2 land drop (CR 305.9 / 113.6c — "put onto the battlefield" bypasses per-turn / phase / stack gate); doesn't touch `LandDropTracker`. Introduces `CardSubtype.Snake` (creature subtype, CR 205.3m — Ohran Frostfang Snow Snake snapshot updated to surface the subtype). `ExploreFactory` (Worldwake / Modern Horizons 2 {1}{G} Sorcery) ships the card shape + `BuildResolveEffect(caster, landDropTracker?)`: (1) CR 305.2 bump — reads `LandDropTracker.MaxLandDropsThisTurn(caster)` and writes `current + 1` via `SetMaxLandDropsThisTurn` (audit conclusion: PR #447's tracker exposes only `Set` + `Max` reader — no additive `AddExtraLandDrop` method; Explore reads + writes; stack-safe + turn-safe via `LandDropTracker.ResetTurn`'s per-turn-change clear); (2) `Fx.DrawCards(caster, 1)` (empty library stamps CR 704.5b loss flag). Multi-Explore stacking is additive (each cast reads the post-prior cap). The two halves of the spell are independent — null tracker shape-path skips the bump but the draw still runs. **Amulet Titan engine audit findings**: (a) `AmuletOfVigorFactory`'s `CardMovedEvent` subscriber correctly fires on bounce lands fetched via `Sakura-Tribe Scout` / cast-from-hand because `ZoneService.MoveCard` applies the `ConditionalEntersTappedReplacement` (predicate always-false) before publishing the event, so `Permanent.IsTapped == true` when Amulet's trigger condition evaluates (CR 614.6 — replacements fully resolve before triggered abilities see the event). (b) **Known gap (not fixed here)**: `PrimevalTitanFactory.MoveToBattlefieldTapped` does manual `caster.Zones.Battlefield.AddCard(pick)` + `perm.Tap()` instead of routing through `ZoneService.MoveCard`. Consequences: (i) `CardMovedEvent` is NOT published, so Amulet of Vigor's tapped-ETB trigger does NOT fire on lands fetched by Primeval Titan — the printed Amulet Titan combo line "Titan ETBs → fetches Karoo, Amulet untaps Karoo" only works through the cast-side land-drop, not the Titan's own fetch; (ii) bounce land ETB self-bounce triggers also do NOT fire on Titan-fetched bounce lands. The end-state tapped flag is correct (Titan manually taps), so the bot's static evaluation isn't broken — but the dynamic combo lines that read `CardMovedEvent` won't surface. This is the established v1 pattern (Scapeshift / Search for Tomorrow share the same raw-zone-fetch shape) and migrating to `ZoneService`-routed fetches is documented as a separate engine-wide pass — flagged here so the next Amulet Titan polish PR can address the cycle holistically rather than piecemeal. New tests: `Majik.Core.Tests/CardData/SakuraTribeScoutTests.cs` (7 tests — identity (1/1 Snake Scout), `NamedCardFactory` dispatch, activated-ability cost shape (one ICost + one effect), land-in-hand moves to battlefield, no-land clean no-op, mixed hand picks first land, `ZoneService`-routed move publishes `CardMovedEvent`) + `Majik.Core.Tests/CardData/ExploreTests.cs` (7 tests — identity ({1}{G} Sorcery), `NamedCardFactory` dispatch, bumps max 1→2 + draws one, null tracker skips bump + still draws, two Explores stack additively (1→2→3), `ResetTurn` drops the bump, empty library stamps loss flag + bump still applies). Snapshot drift fix: `Majik.Core.Tests/Snapshots/snapshots/ohran-frostfang.json` updated to include `"Snake"` in subtypes now that the enum exists. All 5746 Core + 304 Server + 149 Api + 135 Bot + 3 Bot.Integration tests green. Coverage NamedFactory tier 370 → 372. Cards-db is being removed — pure factory work, no JSON / SQL paths touched. Amulet Titan engine unlock: Sakura-Tribe Scout completes the cast-side ramp loop (turn-1 Forest + Scout, turn-2 Amulet, turn-3 Karoo + Scout untaps it for {G}{G} + bounce + drop again); Explore is the cantrip ramp insurance pillar.)
**Last updated:** 2026-05-25 (Untap-stax cycle — Stasis + Static Orb + Winter Orb + Smoke. Builds on PR #532's untap-skip primitive by extending `Majik.Core/Effects/UntapStepRestrictions.cs` with a third predicate flavour: **untap count cap**. New `MarkUntapCountCap(token, maxCount, filter, isActive)` registers an "at most N permanents matching <filter> may untap per untap step" rider with an `isActive` re-check gate (so "as long as <source> is untapped" conditional caps re-check the source's live tap state at consultation time without needing a TapEvent surface). New `ApplyCountCaps(candidates, untappingPlayer)` returns the set of permanents blocked by at least one active cap — greedy first-fit, order-sensitive on the printed iteration order, caps stack as intersections. New `UntapCountCap` record + `HasCountCaps` short-circuit getter. `Majik.Core/Game/TurnDriver.cs`'s `UntapStep(Player active)` now does a two-pass thinning: (1) collect tapped permanents NOT gated by `ShouldSkipUntap`, (2) ask `ApplyCountCaps` to return the blocked subset; permanents not in the blocked subset and not in ShouldSkipUntap untap normally. `ResetTurnState()` still runs for every permanent regardless of gate (summoning sickness / loyalty flags clear normally). New `Majik.Core/Effects/UntapCountCapStaticEffect.cs` reusable lifecycle binder — same `Attach`/`Detach`/`Sync` shape as `DoesNotUntapStaticEffect` / `SubtypeDoesNotUntapStaticEffect` — `(source, maxCount, filter, isActive, eventBus)`. Four new factories under `Majik.Core/CardData/Factories/`: `StasisFactory` (Alpha Enchantment {1}{U}) — MaxCount=0 on `_ => true` filter (functionally "skip the untap step"), unconditional `isActive: () => true`, plus an upkeep `TriggeredAbility` over `Triggers.OnStepBegin(owner, Upkeep)` whose resolve auto-pays {U} from the controller's pool via `Player.PayMana` and on failure sacrifices Stasis (Battlefield→Graveyard via raw zone move, same shape as `NihilSpellbombFactory.SacrificeSelf`); `StaticOrbFactory` (Mirrodin Artifact {3}) — MaxCount=2 on `_ => true` filter, conditional `isActive: () => !card.IsTapped`; `WinterOrbFactory` (Alpha Artifact {2}) — MaxCount=1, filter restricted to `p.HasType(CardType.Land)`, conditional gate; `SmokeFactory` (Alpha Enchantment {1}{R}) — MaxCount=1, filter restricted to `p.HasType(CardType.Creature)`, unconditional gate. All four ship two overloads: shape-only `Create(Player)` and live-wired `Create(Player, [TriggerManager?,] IEventBus?)` (Stasis carries the upkeep trigger on the shape path too). New tests: `Majik.Core.Tests/CardData/Factories/UntapStaxTests.cs` (13 tests — identity per card, `NamedCardFactory` dispatch, Stasis end-to-end skips both Alice's + Bob's untap steps symmetrically, Stasis upkeep pays {U} → stays / can't pay → sacrificed, Static Orb cap-of-2 thins three tapped lands to two untaps, Static Orb tapped → cap dormant → all untap, Winter Orb cap-of-1 thins three tapped lands to one untap but a tapped non-land bear untaps freely, Smoke cap-of-1 thins three tapped creatures to one but a tapped land untaps freely, Smoke LTB lifts the cap — fresh tapped creatures untap normally). All 5743 Core + 304 Server + 149 Api + 135 Bot + 3 Bot.Integration pass. Coverage NamedFactory tier +4 (Stasis / Static Orb / Winter Orb / Smoke — none are Modern-legal, so the seed `IsImplemented` flag is unaffected; same posture as Mana Vault). Cards-db is being removed — pure factory work, no JSON / SQL paths touched. Infrastructure: the count-cap primitive unblocks every "more than N <filter> can't untap" stax piece — Frozen Æther variants, the Force of Negation-adjacent Howling Mine-shaped 2-cap shells, Imposing Sovereign-style enter-tapped riders that compose with caps via the dual-gate ShouldSkipUntap + ApplyCountCaps pipeline.)

**Last updated:** 2026-05-25 (Untap-skip primitive (CR 502.1) + Mana Vault + Choke retrofit. New `Majik.Core/Effects/UntapStepRestrictions.cs` is a process-level registry (sibling of `Majik.Core/Rules/CastingRestrictions.cs`) holding per-source-token (token, target) entries in two flavours: per-permanent skip (`MarkPermanentDoesNotUntap(token, permanent)`) and subtype-scoped symmetric skip (`MarkSubtypeDoesNotUntap(token, subtype)`); `ShouldSkipUntap(permanent, untappingPlayer)` returns true when any entry matches by reference (per-permanent) or `permanent.HasSubtype(entry.Subtype)` (subtype). `RemoveAll(token)` lifts every entry under the source — the same idempotent (token, target) shape `CastingRestrictions._sorcerySpeed` / `_castFromHandOnly` already use so multiple sources stack without trampling. The `untappingPlayer` arg is reserved for future controller-scoped filters; v1 predicates are either self-targeted or symmetric over a subtype. Two reusable lifecycle binders ship alongside: `Majik.Core/Effects/DoesNotUntapStaticEffect.cs` (self-targeted — Mana Vault, Stasis-style self-skip; subscribes to `CardMovedEvent`, registers on ETB, lifts on LTB — same `Attach`/`Detach`/`Sync` shape as `SorcerySpeedRestrictionEffect` / `CastFromHandOnlyRestrictionEffect`) and `Majik.Core/Effects/SubtypeDoesNotUntapStaticEffect.cs` (symmetric over a `CardSubtype` — Choke for Islands; identical lifecycle shape). `Majik.Core/Game/TurnDriver.cs`'s private `UntapStep(Player active)` now consults `UntapStepRestrictions.ShouldSkipUntap(card, active)` before calling `card.Untap()` — on a hit the permanent is skipped but `card.ResetTurnState()` still runs (summoning sickness / loyalty-once-per-turn flags clear normally). `ManaVaultFactory` retrofit: new `Create(Player, TriggerManager?, IEventBus?)` overload — when an event bus is supplied the `DoesNotUntapStaticEffect` lifecycle attaches so the printed "Mana Vault doesn't untap during your untap step" clause (long-deferred) is fully wired; the no-arg and `(owner, triggers)` overloads still build shape-only without auto-attaching so the existing structural test suite keeps working unchanged. `ChokeFactory` retrofit: new `Create(Player, IEventBus?)` overload — `SubtypeDoesNotUntapStaticEffect(card, CardSubtype.Island, eventBus)` attaches on ETB; Choke graduates from shape-only marker to fully Vintage-correct (the long-deferred PR #531 marker comment lifted). Symmetric — affects every Island regardless of controller, matching the printed oracle's "their controllers' untap steps" wording. New tests: `Majik.Core.Tests/Effects/UntapSkipTests.cs` (12 tests — registry permanent-skip blocks + lifts; registry idempotent under repeat MarkPermanentDoesNotUntap; registry subtype-skip symmetric across controllers + non-Island unaffected; multiple sources stack with per-source RemoveAll independence; `DoesNotUntapStaticEffect` lifecycle attach-on-ETB + lift-on-LTB; `SubtypeDoesNotUntapStaticEffect` lifecycle attach-on-ETB + lift-on-LTB; end-to-end via `TurnDriver.RunTurnAsync` — Mana Vault on battlefield + tapped stays tapped after untap step; Mana Vault leaves battlefield → restriction lifts (fresh permanent untaps normally); Choke on battlefield gates Alice's own + Bob's Islands; non-Island Mountain untaps normally under Choke; two Chokes stack — one leaving keeps the other's gate live; two Mana Vaults independent per-permanent — one leaving lifts only its own restriction). Test fixture calls `UntapStepRestrictions.Clear()` in ctor + `Dispose` to prevent process-level registry leakage across cases. All 5730 Core + 304 Server + 149 Api + 135 Bot + 3 Bot.Integration pass. Coverage NamedFactory tier unchanged (retrofits, not new factories). Infrastructure unblocks Stasis (per-permanent self-skip + per-turn upkeep maintenance), Smoke (subtype-style "creatures don't untap" symmetric filter), Static Orb (controller-scoped "more than two permanents don't untap"), Winter Orb (active-player-only variant), Frozen Æther-style filters — all slot through one of the two existing binder shapes plus the registry, no new TurnDriver wiring required.)

**Last updated:** 2026-05-25 (Ravnica bounce land cycle — Azorius Chancery + Boros Garrison + Dimir Aqueduct + Golgari Rot Farm + Gruul Turf + Izzet Boilerworks + Orzhov Basilica + Rakdos Carnarium + Selesnya Sanctuary + Simic Growth Chamber. New `Majik.Core/CardData/Factories/BounceLandCycleFactory.cs` is a single parametric named-card factory carrying 10 `[CardName(name, colourA, colourB)]` attributes for the Ravnica + Ravnica Allegiance "Karoo" cycle. Two-overload shape mirrors `BojukaBogFactory` / `HiveOfTheEyeTyrantFactory`: single-arg dispatcher path (no replacements / no triggers / no zone service — shape-only, lands enter untapped on this code path matching every other always-tapped factory's posture); full overload `(owner, args, zoneService?, eventBus?, triggers?, replacements?)` wires ETB-tapped replacement + ETB-bounce trigger registration + ZoneService-routed move. Tests: `Majik.Core.Tests/CardData/Factories/BounceLandCycleTests.cs` (55 tests — 10 dispatch + 10 colour-pair mana-ability + 10 ETB trigger shape + 10 ETB-tapped replacement registration + 10 cycle-wide ETB-bounce-first-controller-land + single-arg-dispatch shape-only + ETB-bounce-returns-other-land + ETB-bounce-no-other-lands-no-op + ETB-bounce-does-not-pick-self + ETB-bounce-does-not-bounce-opponents-land). All 5667 Core tests pass. Coverage NamedFactory tier +10 (10 bounce lands all on one factory). Cards-db is being removed — pure factory work, no JSON / SQL paths touched. Amulet Titan deck-unlock pillar — the cycle is the only Modern shell that consistently abuses Amulet of Vigor + the Karoo's untap-and-bounce-for-tempo line; also slots into Lotus Field, Tron, and Lands.dec sideboards.)

Cycling `{3}{B}{B}` routed through the shared primitive. On-cycle trigger (CR 702.32d) wired as a `TriggeredAbility` over `EventTriggerCondition<CardCycledEvent>` gated to `ReferenceEquals(e.Card, card)` (printed self-cycle gate, distinct from Curator of Mysteries' "another card" gate) with `ActiveZones = {Graveyard}` (post-discard zone — Decree has already moved hand → graveyard by the time the cycling resolve body publishes the event); trigger-resolve body built on demand via `BuildCycleEffect(allPlayers)` — registers `PumpUntilEndOfTurnEffect(c, -2, -2)` per creature on every supplied battlefield against `Card.ActiveEffects` (CR 613.4 / CR 514.2 — same Layer 7c EOT-expiry shape `AllCreaturesPumpTemplate` uses for the Infest / Toxic Deluge family; toughness ≤ 0 → standard creature-death SBA). Single-arg dispatcher paths attach the triggers + cycling structurally without a bus (shape-only); `(owner, triggers, eventBus)` overloads register the triggers for bus-driven firing and publish `CardCycledEvent` end-to-end. Decree of Pain is NOT in Modern (printed Scourge → Legacy / Vintage / Commander pool), but the factory ships for cycle-card / EDH coverage and clears one of the four CR 702.32d primitive doc's flagged consumers (Decree of Justice / Decree of Pain / Decree of Annihilation / Akroma's Memorial). **Last updated:** 2026-05-25 (Cycling primitive (CR 702.32) + Tranquil Thicket + Lonely Sandbar + Lightning Rift + Street Wraith retrofit. New `Majik.Core/Keywords/CyclingFactory.cs` ships the canonical activated-from-hand builder for the Cycling keyword (CR 702.32a): `Build(source, cycleCost, eventBus?)` attaches a `KeywordAbility("Cycling")` marker + an `ActivatedAbility` with cost stack `[cycleCost, DiscardSelfCost(source)]` whose resolve body calls `Fx.DrawCards(owner, 1)` then publishes `CardCycledEvent` against the optional bus. The `DiscardSelfCost` hand-zone gate (CR 702.32a) is the activated-from-hand surface — no separate ActiveZones flag needed on `ActivatedAbility`. New `Majik.Core/Events/CardCycledEvent.cs` + `EventType.CardCycled` is the surface CR 702.32d "Whenever a player cycles" triggers subscribe to via `EventTriggerCondition<CardCycledEvent>`. New `Majik.Core/CardData/Factories/LightningRiftFactory.cs` ships the Onslaught Enchantment {1}{R} with a `TriggeredAbility` over `EventTriggerCondition<CardCycledEvent>` (ActiveZones=Battlefield); resolve consults `IPlayerAgent.ChooseYesNoAsync(BotIntent.Burn)` for the optional "you may pay {1}" rider (v1 falls back to auto-pay-if-able when no agent — Daze / Mana Leak posture), pays {1} through `PayMana`, then routes 2 damage through `Fx.DealDamageAny` (Player / Creature / Planeswalker — CR 306.7 loyalty removal on planeswalker targets) against the chosen `ChosenTargets[0][0]`. Single 1..1 "any target" `TargetRequest` (BotIntent.Burn). **Last updated:** 2026-05-25 (Wishboard surface + Mastermind's Acquisition + Karn, the Great Creator retrofit. **Last updated:** 2026-05-25 (Cast-uncounterable primitive (CR 701.5b) + Emrakul, the Aeons Torn. `Spell.CannotBeCountered` (new `bool` on `Majik.Core/Spells/Spell.cs` + getter on `ISpell`) is the per-spell sentinel; `SpellCastFlow` stamps it `true` at cast time when the card carries a `KeywordAbility("Uncounterable")` marker (same scan shape as Annihilator / Flying / Indestructible — discoverability marker + cast-time read). `OracleSpellBinder.RemoveFromStack` now returns `bool`: `false` short-circuits the pop when `ISpell.CannotBeCountered` is set so the uncounterable spell stays on the stack and resolves normally. Every counter primitive that pairs the pop with a "card → graveyard" tail (`Fx.Counter` + the three `CounterSpellFactory` shapes — plain "counter target spell", typed counter, "counter unless pay {N}") gates the tail on the bool so the card's tracking zone doesn't drift out of sync with the live stack. `ProtectionAbility` grows an optional `Func<ISpell, bool>? SpellPredicate` field so "protection from coloured spells" / future "from multicoloured" / "from non-Eldrazi" clauses can ship without inventing one-off colour-string tokens; `Rules.Protection.HasProtectionFromSpell(ICard, ISpell)` is the canonical read site (the legacy `HasProtectionFromColor` colour-string surface is untouched). `EmrakulTheAeonsTornFactory` ships the Rise-of-the-Eldrazi 15/15 Legendary Eldrazi {15}: `KeywordAbility("Flying")` + `KeywordAbility("Uncounterable")` markers + `KeywordAbility("Annihilator", arg: 6)` discoverability marker + the wired Annihilator 6 trigger via `AnnihilatorFactory.Build(card, n: 6, agentSelector)` (PR #496) + a `ProtectionAbility("coloured spells", spell => CardColors.GetColors(spell.Card).Count > 0)` predicate + a `SpellCastEvent`-gated cast trigger (`activeZones: [Stack]`) whose resolution enqueues an extra turn via `TurnManager.AddExtraTurn(controller)` (`TurnManager.AddExtraTurn` already existed — no new primitive). `Create(Player)` shape-only + `Create(Player, TurnManager?, TriggerManager?, Func<Player, IPlayerAgent?>?)` fully-wired overloads mirror Ulamog's wiring posture. Dispatches via `[CardName]` source-gen. Tests: `CannotBeCounteredTests` (6 facts — direct `RemoveFromStack` veto bool, `Fx.Counter` zone-tail skip, non-ISpell stack-object control, `SpellCastFlow` cast-time stamp) + `EmrakulTheAeonsTornFactoryTests` (6 facts — identity, NamedFactory dispatch, marker presence, protection predicate accepts colourless / rejects coloured, cast trigger enqueues extra turn). Coverage NamedFactory tier 300 → 301; previously deferred by PR #490 + PR #496 — now unblocked.)

**Last updated:** 2026-05-25 (Opening-hand alt-cost surface + Leyline cycle — Void / Sanctity / Anguish / Lightning / Combustion. New `OpeningHandCheckEvent` (CR 103.5) published once per player by `GameDriver.RunGameAsync` AFTER the London mulligan loop resolves but BEFORE the turn loop begins, carrying a Player + an independent post-mulligan opening-hand snapshot in starting-player-first turn order. New `OpeningHandLeylineAlternativeCost` subscriber (CR 702.95) attached at game-start when an event bus is present: for each card in the published opening-hand snapshot carrying a `KeywordAbility("OpeningHandLeyline")` marker (constant `OpeningHandLeylineAlternativeCost.LeylineKeyword`), prompts the player's `IPlayerAgent.ChooseYesNoAsync` with new `BotIntent.OpeningHandLeyline` (bit 25, upside-classified — default agent posture accepts) "Begin with [name] on the battlefield?" and on yes stamps `Card.WasCast = false` (CR 113.5 — Leyline is "put onto the battlefield", not cast, so Containment Priest's cast-gated rider sees the correct posture) before routing hand → battlefield via `ZoneService.MoveCard(controller: player)` so ETB triggers and `ReplacementBus` consumers fire normally. Multiple Leylines in the same opening hand each get their own prompt; a "no" on one does not skip the others. `LeylineOfTheVoidFactory` retrofit: adds the keyword marker and drops the "opening-hand alt-cost deferred" comment; the existing CR 614 opponent-graveyard-to-exile static replacement is unchanged. Four new sibling Leyline factories: `LeylineOfSanctityFactory` ({2}{W}{W} from M11 — opening-hand marker only; player-targeted hexproof rider deferred behind a future player-protection layer, same gap Veil of Summer's "you and permanents you control gain hexproof" already documents); `LeylineOfAnguishFactory` ({2}{B}{B} from MH3 — opening-hand marker only; controller-partitioned +1/+0 / -1/-0 power statics deferred behind a global-static Layer-7c surface); `LeylineOfLightningFactory` ({2}{R}{R} from MH3 — opening-hand marker only; "first spell each turn → 1 damage" trigger deferred behind a per-turn first-cast gate on the existing `Triggers.OnSpellCastByController` rail); `LeylineOfCombustionFactory` ({2}{R}{R} from M20 — opening-hand marker only; "opponent targets you or your permanent → 2 damage to that player" trigger deferred behind a targeting-resolution trigger predicate). All five Leylines dispatch through the `[CardName]` source-gen rail (`NamedCardFactory.Create("Leyline of Sanctity", owner)` etc.). New `Majik.Core.Tests/Costs/OpeningHandLeylineTests.cs` (15 tests) covers: every Leyline carries the marker; identity / type / mana cost for each new factory; `NamedCardFactory` dispatch lights up; subscriber accepts → hand → battlefield; subscriber declines → stays in hand; multiple Leylines each prompted in event-snapshot order; non-Leyline hand cards aren't prompted (no spurious yes/no consumption); `WasCast` clears to false on the put-to-battlefield even when pre-stamped true; event-bus delivery; `GameDriver` publishes exactly one `OpeningHandCheckEvent` per player; opening-hand check fires AFTER mulligan resolution (post-bottom snapshot, 6-card hand after one mulligan + bottom-1). `GameDriver.cs` constructor stores `ZoneService` so the subscriber can be wired at game-start. `BotIntent.OpeningHandLeyline` upside-classification matches `CheatIntoPlay`'s default-accept posture in `IPlayerAgent.ChooseYesNoAsync` default body. Coverage: +4 NamedFactory tier (Sanctity / Anguish / Lightning / Combustion). Infrastructure unblocks the rest of the Leyline cycle — Sanctity's hexproof / Anguish's power statics / Lightning's first-spell trigger / Combustion's targeting trigger and the remaining 5 unprinted Leylines slot into the same `KeywordAbility("OpeningHandLeyline")` marker with no further per-card wiring.)

Short battlefield halts cleanly (sacrifice-as-many-as-available). Multiple Annihilator creatures attacking each fire independently per CR 702.86b — one trigger per source per attack event. Tokens sacrificed via the effect hit the graveyard and the next `TokensCeaseToExistCheck` SBA pass removes them per CR 110.7 / CR 704.5d (verified end-to-end in tests). `IPlayerAgent.ChooseFromBattlefieldAsync` mirrors `ChooseFromHandAsync` (PR #483) shape — same default-impl + `ScriptedAgent.QueueFromBattlefield(Func / ICard?)` test helpers + same `BotIntent.Removal` posture. `KeywordAbility` extended with optional `int? Arg` (and `arg:` ctor parameter) so parameterised keywords (Annihilator N, future Ward N / Fading N / Vanishing N / Bushido N) carry the N value alongside the marker name; `Description` shows "Annihilator 2" when Arg is set, plain Keyword otherwise. `UlamogsCrusherFactory` ships the Rise-of-the-Eldrazi 8/8 Eldrazi {8}: discoverability `KeywordAbility("Annihilator", arg: 2)` + must-attack `KeywordAbility("AttacksEachCombat")` markers + the wired Annihilator trigger via `AnnihilatorFactory.Build(card, n: 2, agentSelector)`. Must-attack (CR 702.43 — combat-restriction wiring) shipped as marker only — same posture as Reality Smasher's non-mana Ward discard rider. Dispatches via `[CardName]` source-gen. Emrakul, the Aeons Torn still deferred — the cast-uncounterable surface (`Spell.CannotBeCountered`) doesn't exist yet in the engine; counter logic in `OracleSpellBinder.RemoveFromStack` doesn't gate on a per-spell uncounterable flag.
