**Last updated:** 2026-05-25 (Eldrazi cycle — Reality Smasher + Thought-Knot Seer + Ulamog, the Ceaseless Hunger. `RealitySmasherFactory` ships the OGW 5/5 Eldrazi {4}{C}: Trample + Haste + Ward keyword markers (printed non-mana 'discard a card' rider deferred — `WardEffect` helper exposed via `BuildWardEffect` with `ManaCost.Zero` and the discard cost surfaced as documentation constant `WardDiscardCost`; same mana-only-Ward-effect gap Kappa Cannoneer's {4} mana Ward sits behind). `ThoughtKnotSeerFactory` ships the OGW 4/4 Eldrazi {3}{C}: ETB triggered ability (CR 603.6a) over self-enter `CardMovedEvent` with a 1..1 'target opponent' `TargetRequest` (`BotIntent.HandHate` — new flag bit at 1UL<<23 for opponent-hand-pick prompts, distinct from `Discard` because the chooser is not the card's owner); resolution reveals the target's hand, controller's `IPlayerAgent.ChooseFromHandAsync(victim, nonlandHand, BotIntent.HandHate)` picks (deterministic first-nonland fallback, illegal-pick guard mirrors Liliana of the Veil's posture); exile via `ZoneService.MoveCard` when wired (`CardMovedEvent` publishes for Containment Priest / Tormod's Crypt subscribers), raw zone manipulation otherwise. LTB triggered ability (CR 603.6c / 603.10c) matches any FromZone == Battlefield, 1..1 'target opponent' request, `Fx.DrawCards(target, 1)` on resolution (`Player.MarkTriedToDrawFromEmptyLibrary` state-loss path preserved per CR 704.5b). Both triggers' active zones = Battlefield ('looks back' LTB semantics per CR 603.6d). `UlamogTheCeaselessHungerFactory` ships the BFZ Legendary Eldrazi {10} 10/10: Indestructible keyword marker (CR 702.12 — SBA reads via `CombatAbilities.HasIndestructible`); cast trigger (CR 603.6a / 603.10) over `SpellCastEvent` filtered to `ReferenceEquals(e.Spell.Card, card)` with `ActiveZones = Stack` matching Cascade self-cast posture (Crashing Footfalls / Living End), 2..2 'target permanent' `TargetRequest` tagged `BotIntent.Removal`, resolution exiles each chosen permanent (CR 701.21 — exile bypasses indestructible per CR 702.12b — `ZoneMoveReason.Other` implicit on raw zone path) with illegal-on-resolution Zone == Battlefield gate per CR 608.2b. Attack trigger (CR 508.1f / 603.1) over `Triggers.OnAttackSelf`-shaped predicate that captures the `DefendingPlayerOrPlaneswalker` as the victim (Player-only — planeswalker defender → no library so no-op); resolution loops 20 iterations exiling the top of `victim.Zones.Library` (short-library halts cleanly — CR 704.5b state-loss the next SBA pass handles, not this effect). All three dispatch via the `[CardName]` source-gen rail. Emrakul, the Aeons Torn intentionally deferred — Annihilator (CR 702.85) primitive doesn't exist yet (only mentioned in `KeywordAnalyzer.cs` classifier list); extra-turn primitive exists via `Game.ExtraTurnQueue` + `TurnManager.AddExtraTurn` but Annihilator gates the whole card's value posture, so deferred as a unit until the Annihilator-N primitive ships. Coverage NamedFactory tier 260 → 263; raw cover 27.4% → 27.4% (+13 distinct from cards-db refresh + 3 named); weighted 92.3% → 95.5%.)

**Latest origin/main:** Layer-4 type-strip primitive + Heliod devotion gate. New `Majik.Core/Effects/Layer4TypeStripEffect.cs` is a CR 205.2 / 613.1d Layer 4 (type-changing) continuous effect that conditionally strips `CardType.Creature` from a permanent while a controller-supplied `Func<bool>` predicate evaluates true. Source-anchored — `IsActive` gates on the source being on the battlefield, so the effect self-terminates on LTB; predicate is re-evaluated on every `ContinuousEffectsService.Compute` pass so live conditions (devotion, life totals, colour counts) drive the gate without re-registering. Layer-4-only — does NOT touch P/T / abilities / subtypes; stacks alongside other Layer 4 effects via timestamp + dependency sort (latest-timestamp wins per CR 613.7 when paired with a Layer 4 ADD-Creature effect like `KarnAnimateArtifactEffect` / `MutavaultFactory`'s animate). `HeliodSunCrownedFactory` gains a `(owner, triggers, effects)` overload that stamps the supplied `ContinuousEffectsService` onto `Card.ActiveEffects` and registers `Layer4TypeStripEffect(card, () => ComputeDevotionToWhite(controller) < 5)` (clears PR #506's deferred type-strip — Heliod now correctly becomes a non-creature while devotion < 5 and recovers Creature when devotion hits 5+ via cast/LTB without effect re-registration). Tests: `Layer4TypeStripTests` covers strip-while-true / lifts-when-false / live-predicate / scope-to-source / source-LTB termination / stacks-with-other-Layer-4 (AddSubtype additive, KarnAnimate timestamp-wins) / null-arg guards. `HeliodSunCrownedTests` gains four scenarios: devotion-4 strips Creature, ineligible as a Doom-Blade target (creature-only predicate fails), devotion bumps to 5 restores Creature, devotion drops below 5 strips again. On top of Storm-pillar audit + Past in Flames (Sorcery {3}{R}, Innistrad). Audit confirms `Majik.Core/Keywords/StormHelper.cs` is the single shared on-cast trigger helper used by `BrainFreezeFactory` + `TendrilsOfAgonyFactory` (Storm-keyword cards); both use it identically (capture-into-closure storm-count read at trigger-evaluation time from `TurnState.SpellsCastByPlayer - 1`, then N `SpellCopier.PushCopyOfTopSpell` invocations on resolution). CR 702.40a "you may choose new targets" + copies-as-distinct-stack-objects gaps remain as documented (inherited from `SpellCopier`). `Manamorphose` already shipped with `ManamorphoseFactory` (Instant {1}{R/G}, "Add two mana in any combination of colors. Draw a card.") — audit confirmed no new `IPlayerAgent.ChooseManaColorsAsync` hook exists; existing caller-supplied `Func<Player, ManaColor[]>` colour-picker + deterministic `{R}{G}` default is the correct posture. New `PastInFlamesFactory.Create` ships the sorcery shape; `BuildResolveEffect(controller, eventBus)` walks `controller.Zones.Graveyard.GetCards()`, filters via `HasType(CardType.Instant) || HasType(CardType.Sorcery)`, skips Past in Flames itself by name (its printed Flashback {4}{R} is the authoritative cost, not {3}{R}), and stamps `Card.GrantRuntimeFlashback` with each card's own `ManaCostValue` per CR 702.34. EOT cleanup via one-shot `StepStartedEvent`→`Cleanup` subscription clears every grant from the same resolution (CR 514.2 — same shape as `FlashbackFactory` + `SnapcasterMageFactory`). Printed Flashback {4}{R} on Past in Flames itself wired via `GetFlashbackAlternativeCost()` producing `FlashbackAlternativeCost(ManaCost.Parse("{4}{R}"))`. Bot-side discovery free via existing `RuntimeFlashbackAltCostProbe`. `Majik.Core.Tests/CardData/Factories/PastInFlamesFactoryTests.cs` covers identity + dispatcher + grant-each-instant-sorcery + skips-creatures + skips-opponent-graveyard + skips-self + empty-graveyard no-op + EOT cleanup via bus + printed Flashback {4}{R} alt-cost. On top of Sorcery-speed activation gate (CR 117.1a / 307.5 — infrastructure PR). `ActivatedAbility` grows a `bool sorcerySpeed` ctor parameter + `IsSorcerySpeed` getter on `IActivatedAbility`; `ActivateAbilityAction` mirrors the cast-side `CastSpellAction.SorcerySpeedAvailable` pattern with a `(ability, player, sorcerySpeedAvailable)` overload (single-arg ctor defaults `SorcerySpeedAvailable: true` so the engine's hundreds of legacy activate-call sites are unaffected). `ActionValidator.ValidateActivateAbility` rejects sorcery-speed-only activations when the caller signals the window is closed, returning `RuleViolation("307.5", "activate-only-as-a-sorcery")`. `CardDefinitionFactory.BuildActivatedAbility` threads the JSON `sorcerySpeed` flag onto the runtime ability so JSON-defined cards (Walking Ballista's {4}: put counter) light up automatically. Nine named-factory retrofits flip the printed "Activate only as a sorcery" abilities through `sorcerySpeed: true`: `WishclawTalismanFactory`, `PriestOfFellRitesFactory`, `TasigurTheGoldenFangFactory`, `ColossusHammerFactory` / `SkullclampFactory` / `UmezawasJitteFactory` / `SwordOfFireAndIceFactory` / `SwordOfFeastAndFamineFactory` / `CoriSteelCutterFactory` (Equip {N}, CR 702.6a). `Majik.Core.Tests/Rules/SorcerySpeedActivationTests.cs` covers the four-timing-windows matrix on Walking Ballista plus instant-speed-rejection on Wishclaw and Tasigur, the instant-speed-ability-bypass path, and the backward-compat default. Mechanic-deps scanner: cluster #4 ("Activate only as a sorcery") moves from 9 blocked factories → 0 real (TirelessTracker false-positive remains). On top of Meltdown (Sorcery {X}{R} from Urza's Destiny — "Destroy each artifact with mana value X or less." `MeltdownFactory.Create` ships the sorcery shape; `BuildResolveEffect(caster, allPlayers, x)` returns a single `IEffect` that snapshots every supplied player's battlefield, filters via `OfType<Card>()` + `HasType(CardType.Artifact)` + `ManaCostValue.TotalValue ≤ x`, HashSet-dedupes via `ReferenceEqualityComparer.Instance`, and routes each victim to its owner's graveyard via `OracleSpellBinder.MoveToGraveyard` (CR 701.7). Mirrors `WrathOfGodFactory.BuildResolveEffect`'s multi-player iteration shape with the Pernicious-Deed-style `mv ≤ X` predicate narrowed to Artifact only. X provenance: callers pass the resolved X directly (same simplification as Pernicious Deed / Engineered Explosives). Single-arg dispatcher path produces card shape only — no resolve effect, since X is unknown until cast. Indestructible bypass is the same lossy gap inherited from `OracleSpellBinder.MoveToGraveyard` — same as Slaughter Pact / Murderous Rider / Drown in the Loch / Embereth Shieldbreaker / Wrath of God) on top of Monastery Swiftspear (Creature — Human Monk {R} 1/2 from Khans of Tarkir + many reprints — Modern Burn / Izzet Prowess one-drop. `MonasterySwiftspearFactory.Create` ships the 1/2 Human Monk shape with the printed Haste `KeywordAbility` marker (CR 702.10 — same wiring as `EarthshakerKhenraFactory` / `GoblinChieftainFactory` / `GoblinRabblemasterFactory`) AND a Prowess `KeywordAbility` marker (CR 702.108) for shape-only keyword inspection alongside the prowess `TriggeredAbility` mechanic itself via the shared `ProwessFactory.Build(card, effects)` helper (sibling of `MonasteryMentorFactory`'s prowess wire-up — same `ContinuousEffectsService`-driven `ProwessPumpEffect`-on-Layer-7c posture). The `(owner, eventBus, triggers, effects)` overload registers the prowess trigger with the supplied `TriggerManager` so a controller-cast noncreature `SpellCastEvent` fires the prowess pump on Swiftspear (CR 702.108's "noncreature spell" predicate enforced by `ProwessFactory.Build`'s `EventTriggerCondition<SpellCastEvent>` — controller-match + `!HasType(CardType.Creature)`); `Creature.ActiveEffects` is wired to the supplied service so `Creature.Power` / `Creature.Toughness` reads flow through the layers compute. Single-arg dispatcher path attaches both keyword markers but does NOT wire the trigger (no `ContinuousEffectsService` available at dispatch — shape-only posture, same as `MonasteryMentorFactory` / `GoblinChieftainFactory`). Distinct from Mentor's prowess wiring in one observable respect — Swiftspear surfaces a standalone `KeywordAbility("Prowess")` marker because the printed card line is literally "Prowess" (the vanilla keyword), whereas Mentor's reminder text is implicit in the trigger description and doesn't carry the marker. Vanilla-printed Prowess body — no ETB, no activated, no LTB clauses to defer; the only deferred surface is the same `TriggerManager` shape-vs-runtime split that every other prowess-bearing factory ships. Bumps Burn rollup to ~87% and Murktide / Izzet Tempo rollup to ~78%) on top of Geralf's Messenger (Creature — Zombie {B}{B}{B} 3/2 from Dark Ascension — Modern Mono-Black Aggro / Zombies finisher and a Yawgmoth-pillar Undying body. `GeralfsMessengerFactory.Create` ships the 3/2 Zombie shape, Undying `KeywordAbility` marker (CR 702.93) wired alongside the return-with-+1/+1-counter mechanic via the shared `UndyingFactory.Build` helper (same wiring Young Wolf / Strangleroot Geist would use). Enters-tapped replacement (CR 614.1c) via `EntersTappedReplacement` on the supplied `ReplacementBus` — unconditional, no gate (contrast Valakut's 5-Mountain check), so the replacement applies to every ETB including Undying returns. Single-arg dispatcher path omits the replacement (Messenger enters untapped on shape-only paths, mirroring Creeping Tar Pit / Valakut). ETB triggered ability (CR 603.6a + CR 119.3) wired via `Triggers.OnEnterBattlefieldSelf` with a 1..1 "target opponent" `TargetRequest`; on resolution the chosen player loses 2 life via `Player.LoseLife`. Mirrors `HidetsugusSecondRiteFactory.BuildSpellDefinition`'s target-opponent shape and Earthshaker Khenra's chosen-target gating (CR 608.2b — no/illegal target = clean no-op). Agent-driven "target opponent" prompt deferred — v1 honours pre-supplied `ChosenTargets`. Use the `(owner, eventBus, triggers, replacements)` overload for fully-wired behavior) on top of Drannith Magistrate (Creature — Human Wizard {1}{W} 1/3 from Ikoria: Lair of Behemoths — `DrannithMagistrateFactory.Create` ships the 1/3 Human Wizard shape plus the printed static "Your opponents can't cast spells from anywhere other than their hands" (CR 113.6 / CR 601.2a). Wired via a new `CastFromHandOnlyRestrictionEffect` lifecycle binder (sibling of `SorcerySpeedRestrictionEffect` — same Attach/Detach + `CardMovedEvent` ETB/LTB sync shape + same opaque-token + lock-gated registry pattern). While Drannith is on the battlefield, every player returned by `opponentResolver` is registered into a new `_castFromHandOnly` slot on `CastingRestrictions`; `ActionValidator.ValidateCastSpell` consults `CastingRestrictions.MustCastFromHand(player)` and rejects any cast whose declared source zone (`CastSpellAction.FromZone`) is not `ZoneType.Hand` — including spells cast from exile (cascade, suspend, foretell, alt-cost from-exile flows), the graveyard (flashback / disturb / aftermath / escape / jump-start), the library, or the command zone. Returns `RuleViolation 113.6`. Single-arg dispatcher path produces the correct card shape with no live opponent restriction (mirrors Teferi, Time Raveler's single-arg posture). Backward-compat: `CastSpellAction.FromZone` is optional — callers that don't stamp a source zone treat the restriction as no-op on the from-zone axis, since the production `SpellCaster` / `SpellCastFlow` pipeline does not yet stamp `FromZone` for every cast path. `CastSpellAction` adds a `(card, player, sorcerySpeedAvailable, fromZone)` overload preserving the existing 2/3-arg call sites. Emblem-with-static support inherited from the binder shape: pass `source: null` + `activeWhile: () => true` and the emblem registers immediately for the rest of the game (CR 114)) on top of Force of Vigor (Instant {2}{G}{G} from Modern Horizons — `ForceOfVigorFactory.Create` ships the instant shape and `BuildDefinition(targetResolver)` delegates to `DestroySpellFactory.DestroyUpToArtifactEnchantmentSpell(resolver, 2)` so the resolve behaviour is identical whether the spell is bound via `NamedCardFactory` dispatch or via the data-driven `DestroyUpToArtifactEnchantmentTemplate` oracle path (CR 601.2c — "up to two" allows 0..2 legal targets; CR 608.2b — non-Artifact/Enchantment target at resolution is a clean no-op). Pitch alternative cost (CR 118.9) reuses the shared `PitchAlternativeCost` (`RequiredColor: Green, LifeCost: 0`) — same Force-of-Negation timing pattern (not-your-turn predicate via `IsLegalInContext`) without the life rider. Bot probe via `PitchAltCostProbe.DefaultLookup` extended to recognise Force of Vigor as `(Green, 0)`; surfaces one candidate per green card in the caster's hand on the opponent's turn) on top of Aether Hub (Land from Kaladesh — `AetherHubFactory.Create` ships the Land shape, ETB triggered ability (CR 603.6a — "Aether Hub enters with an energy counter on it") that grants the controller one energy via `Player.GainEnergy` (CR 106.13) AND stamps a `CounterType.Energy` marker on the land for shape inspection, `{T}: Add {C}` mana ability, and five WUBRG `ManaAbility` instances each carrying the 3-arg-plus-cost overload — `canActivateCheck` = `!IsTapped` AND `controller.EnergyCounters >= 1` (CR 119.4 — you can't pay a resource you don't have); `additionalCostPayer` = `controller.PayEnergy(1)` running after the {T} tap. Same shape pattern as `CavernOfSoulsFactory` / `DelightedHalflingFactory` / `FieryIsletFactory`'s pain-cost overload. Single-arg dispatcher path attaches the ETB trigger without TriggerManager registration; `(owner, eventBus, triggers)` overload wires bus-driven firing (mirrors `TheOneRingFactory`'s two-arg pattern). Introduces `CounterType.Energy` as an on-card bookkeeping marker — the player-scoped `Player.EnergyCounters` ledger remains the source of truth) on top of Gurmag Angler (Creature — Zombie Fish {7}{B} 5/5 from Khans of Tarkir — `GurmagAnglerFactory.Create` ships the 5/5 Zombie Fish shape with a single Delve `KeywordAbility` marker (CR 702.66). Vanilla delve creature — no printed triggered or activated abilities — so the factory ends right after the keyword marker, sibling shape of `MurktideRegentFactory` minus the ETB trigger. The Delve mechanic itself lives in `DelveCost` + `SpellCastFlow`; callers cast via the cast-flow's `delveCost` parameter to substitute exiled graveyard cards for generic mana. Bot-side delve discovery deferred — same gap as Murktide Regent / Treasure Cruise; tests construct `DelveCost` explicitly and hand it to the cast flow. Introduces `CardSubtype.Fish` (creature subtype, CR 205.3m)) on top of Earthshaker Khenra (Creature — Minotaur Warrior {1}{R} 2/1 from Hour of Devastation — `EarthshakerKhenraFactory.Create` ships the printed Haste `KeywordAbility` marker (CR 702.10 — same wiring as Goblin Chieftain's and Goblin Rabblemaster's printed Haste) plus a single ETB triggered ability (CR 603.6a) wired via `Triggers.OnEnterBattlefieldSelf` with a 1..1 "target creature with power 2 or less" `TargetRequest` (Intent: `BotIntent.Removal`). On resolution the effect reads the trigger's `ChosenTargets`, walks the standard SolitudeFactory-shaped gates — illegal-target check for still-on-battlefield + resolution-time power-≤-2 recheck per CR 608.2b — and registers a `CombatRestrictionEffect(CombatRestriction.CannotBlock, target)` against the target's own `ContinuousEffectsService` (`Creature.ActiveEffects`) where the combat validator's `HasRestriction` lookup runs (CR 509.1c). EOT-scoped via the default `ExpiresAtEndOfTurn=true` matching the printed "this turn" rider (CR 514.2 — same scope as the `UpToNCantBlockTemplate` family in `Misc/StubBindTemplates.cs`). When the target has no live `ActiveEffects` wired (shape-only tests) the grant silently no-ops — same posture as `KraulHarpoonerFactory`'s `PumpUntilEndOfTurnEffect` gate. Eternalize {5}{R}{R} (CR 702.117 — exile from graveyard, return as a 4/4 black Zombie Minotaur Warrior token copy with no mana cost) DEFERRED — needs an exile-cost / graveyard-to-token alt-cost (sibling of Unearth + the Priest-of-Fell-Rites path); same gap noted on the top-20 entry. "Power 2 or less" target legality at choose-time is intentionally deferred to the resolve-time recheck — `LegalCandidates` left empty so the targeting prompt accepts any Creature (same posture as Solitude / Kraul Harpooner), and a production caller wanting agent-side filtering populates `LegalCandidates` itself. Resolution exile mirrors Path to Exile's owner-routed zone move) on top of Oko, Thief of Crowns (Legendary Planeswalker — Oko {1}{G}{U} loyalty 4 from Throne of Eldraine — pillar of WAR/Eldraine standard and the modern poster child for "becomes-Elk" power-level concerns. +2 `TokenFactory.CreateFood` (CR 111.10 — {2}, {T}, Sacrifice: gain 3 life); +1 registers three continuous effects on the supplied `ContinuousEffectsService` — Layer 4 `OkoBecomesElkTypeEffect` (adds Creature + rewrites creature-subtype slot to Elk; Source anchored on Oko himself so a same-turn Layer 6 strip on the now-Elk creature doesn't recursively kill Oko's rider via the CR 613.8 dependency filter), Layer 6 `LoseAllAbilitiesEffect` scoped to the target (clears `chars.Keywords` per CR 613.6), Layer 7b `BecomesPTEffect(3, 3)` for Creature C# instances / `KarnAnimatedShimPTEffect` for non-Creature artifact targets (mirroring Karn's "shim for the future Compute-promotion world" pattern); colour-set to green DEFERRED (no Layer 5 primitive exists yet); deterministic auto-pick of first artifact/creature in supplied battlefield resolver. -5 exchange-control mechanic — deterministic auto-pick of first opponent-permanent (artifact or creature) + first Oko-controller creature; swaps `Permanent.Controller` and moves each card between the two players' battlefield zones (CR 110.2 ownership unchanged); counter-removal half is structural intent only (no CR 611 counter surface on `Permanent` yet). Single-arg dispatcher path attaches all three loyalty bodies; +1/-5 effect bodies gate on the (effects / battlefieldResolver / allPlayersResolver) wiring and no-op without it (loyalty changes still apply per CR 606.3); +2 still spawns a Food token on the single-arg path because `TokenFactory.CreateFood` operates on controller zones directly. Introduces `CardSubtype.Elk` (creature subtype) and `CardSubtype.Oko` (planeswalker subtype)) on top of Archmage's Charm (Instant {U}{U}{U} from Modern Horizons — modal "Choose one —" (CR 700.2d) with three printed modes: (0) counter target spell, (1) target player draws two cards, (2) gain control of target nonland permanent with mana value 1 or less. Card shape only at the dispatcher; bound `SpellDefinition` is built on demand via `ArchmagesCharmFactory.BuildDefinition(caster, targetResolver, stack[, effects])`. Three `TargetRequest`s with `MinTargets=0` so unchosen modes don't gate the cast (mirrors `CrypticCommandFactory`). Mode 0 reuses `OracleSpellBinder.RemoveFromStack` + graveyard zone-move (same shape as Cryptic Command's counter). Mode 1 pulls 2 cards from the *target player's* library — distinct from Cryptic Command's controller-only draw. Mode 2's "nonland permanent with mv ≤ 1" gate is enforced at resolution time (CR 608.2b — illegal target → effect does nothing) since the engine has no agent-side filter yet; on legal target, registers a `ControlChangeEffect` (CR 613.2, Layer 2) against the supplied `ContinuousEffectsService` swapping `EffectiveController` to the caster (underlying `Permanent.Controller` left untouched per Wishclaw Talisman's pattern). New `CounterType.Burden` registered alongside the existing Poison/Void/Time/Charge/Defense/Loyalty markers) on top of Atraxa, Grand Unifier (Legendary Creature — Phyrexian Angel {3}{W}{U}{B}{R}{G} 7/7 from Phyrexia: All Will Be One. Flying + Vigilance + Deathtouch + Lifelink keyword markers (CR 702.9 / 702.20 / 702.2 / 702.15). ETB triggered ability (CR 603.6a) reveals top 10 of controller's library; the resolver walks every `CardType` value in declaration order, picking the first peeked card that matches each type (a multi-type card claims a single slot — Artifact Creature is taken once, not twice). Picks go to hand; remainder re-bottoms shuffled via `Random.Shared` (CR 701.20a). Iteration is enum-driven so Battle (MoM+) lights up automatically once `CardType.Battle` is added. Selector exposed as `SelectOnePerCardType` for unit testability; resolver invoked directly by tests / bots since the dispatcher path attaches the trigger without TriggerManager wiring) on top of Sneak Attack (Enchantment {2}{R} from Urza's Saga — classic Reanimator / Sneak-and-Show enabler. `{R}` activated ability (CR 602) puts a creature card from the controller's hand onto the battlefield, grants Haste, and registers a one-shot `DelayedTriggeredAbility` (CR 603.7) that sacrifices the placed creature at the start of the next end step (CR 500.4 / CR 701.16 — controller's battlefield → owner's graveyard, fence-checked with `e.Timestamp > resolvedAt` to skip the current end step). Each activation closes over its own resolve-time creature pick + delayed sac trigger so multiple activations in the same turn each sacrifice their cheated-in creature independently. v1 deterministic first-creature-in-hand pick (auto-accepts the "you may" when a candidate exists — same shape as Through the Breach / Aether Vial / Goblin Lackey). Hand → battlefield routes through `ZoneService.MoveCard` so ETB triggers fire (CR 603.6a); Haste via `GrantKeywordUntilEndOfTurnEffect` (CR 613.1c Layer 6) plus `HasSummoningSickness=false` for attack-declaration (CR 702.10b — the EOT-scoped grant is observationally equivalent to the printed no-duration wording since the creature is sacrificed at the same boundary). No-creature-in-hand is a clean no-op) on top of Sheoldred, the Apocalypse (Legendary Creature — Phyrexian Praetor {2}{B}{B} 4/5 — Deathtouch keyword marker (CR 702.2); controller-only `CardDrawnEvent` triggered ability via `Triggers.OnCardDrawnByPlayer` filtered to the controller, fires once per drawn card (CR 603.1 / 603.2c), draining 2 life from every opponent supplied by an optional `opponentResolver` and gaining 2 for the controller — single-arg dispatcher path gains the 2 life on execute and silently no-ops the drain without a resolver, mirroring Liliana of the Veil's player-list-resolver shape; introduces a new `CardSubtype.Praetor` for the New Phyrexia / DMU praetor cycle) on top of Through the Breach (Instant {2}{R}{R} from Champions of Kamigawa — Modern Emrakul / Griselbrand enabler. **Last updated:** 2026-05-24 (Companion deck-construction (CR 702.139) — `ICompanionRestriction` + `CompanionValidator`; Lurrus exposes its mv ≤ 2 predicate; runtime cast-from-outside-game half still deferred pending sideboard zone)
**Latest origin/main:** Companion deck-construction (CR 702.139 — infrastructure PR). New `ICompanionRestriction` interface (`Majik.Core/Rules/CompanionRestriction.cs`) declares `Description` + `IsSatisfiedBy(IEnumerable<ICard>)`; `CompanionValidator.Validate(companion, restriction, startingDeck)` aggregates into the existing `DeckValidationResult` shape, sibling to `DeckValidator`. `LurrusOfTheDreamDenFactory.CompanionRestriction` exposes a static singleton implementing "Each permanent card in your starting deck has mana value 2 or less" — it reuses `LurrusGraveyardCastGate.IsPermanentSpell` (CR 110.4a) for permanent-card identification and `MaxGraveyardCastManaValue` for the mv ceiling, so the deck-builder predicate and the runtime grave-cast predicate stay in sync. The runtime "cast from outside the game" half (companion-tax + once-per-game ledger) is still deferred — engine has no sideboard zone (`Majik.Core/Zones/ZoneType.cs`), so the cast pipeline has no source to draw from. `MechanicPrimitiveRegistry`'s `companion` entry narrows scope to runtime cast-from-outside (deck-construction half done). Tests: `Majik.Core.Tests/Rules/CompanionValidatorTests.cs` covers mv ≤ 2 legal deck, 3-mv permanent rejection, instant/sorcery exemption, predicate↔validator agreement, and null-arg guards. On top of Kicker additional-cost (CR 702.33 — infrastructure PR). `KickerAdditionalCost` ships as a real `IAdditionalCost` primitive (sibling of `BuybackAdditionalCost` / sacrifice-rider shape) carrying `(ICard card, ManaCost kickerCost)`; `Pay` drains the kicker mana via `Player.PayMana` and stamps a new `Card.WasKicked` sentinel (mirror on the resolving `Spell.WasKicked` set by `SpellCastFlow` when a `KickerAdditionalCost` is layered onto the cast). `SpellCastFlow.CastAsync` detects the cost in the existing `additionalCosts` merge loop, stamps the spell-level mirror, and appends a cleanup `Effect` to the wrapped effect list that clears `Card.WasKicked` after the spell's printed body resolves so the sentinel doesn't leak to copies / blinks / re-casts (CR 400.7 — new object on each zone change). Bot-side discovery via the new `KickerAltCostProbe` registered in `AlternativeCostProbeRegistry.CreateDefault` alongside Pitch / Delve / Overload / Cascade / Energy / Escape; mirrors `CascadeAltCostProbe`'s discovery-only posture (yields zero `IAlternativeCost` candidates because kicker is an additive cost, not an alt-cost) and exposes `KickerCostFor(card, caster)` + `BuildAdditionalCost(card)` for the bot's "should I pay the kicker?" decision. `KickerAltCostProbe.DefaultLookup` recognises Burst Lightning as a {4}-kicker card. `BurstLightningFactory` retrofitted off the structural `wasKicked` parameter: `BuildSpellDefinition(card, resolver)` reads `Card.WasKicked` at resolve time and branches between 2 / 4 damage, and `BuildAdditionalCost(card)` returns the `{4}` kicker cost as a convenience builder. `Majik.Core.Tests/Costs/KickerAdditionalCostTests.cs` covers the cost primitive (CanPay / Pay / mana-pool drain / Card.WasKicked stamp / failure atomicity), the cast-pipeline integration through Burst Lightning (not-kicked → 2 dmg, kicked → 4 dmg, post-resolve flag clear), and the probe discovery surface (hand / zone / owner pre-filter + registration in the default registry). Mechanic-deps scanner: cluster #9 "Kicker alt-cost (CR 702.33)" drops to zero real blocking factories (SlickshotShowOff's docstring referenced Kicker as a sibling-shape comparison and is rewritten to point at Plot as its own pending cluster). On top of Sorcery-speed activation gate (CR 117.1a / 307.5 — infrastructure PR). `ActivatedAbility` grows a `bool sorcerySpeed` ctor parameter + `IsSorcerySpeed` getter on `IActivatedAbility`; `ActivateAbilityAction` mirrors the cast-side `CastSpellAction.SorcerySpeedAvailable` pattern with a `(ability, player, sorcerySpeedAvailable)` overload (single-arg ctor defaults `SorcerySpeedAvailable: true` so the engine's hundreds of legacy activate-call sites are unaffected). `ActionValidator.ValidateActivateAbility` rejects sorcery-speed-only activations when the caller signals the window is closed, returning `RuleViolation("307.5", "activate-only-as-a-sorcery")`. `CardDefinitionFactory.BuildActivatedAbility` threads the JSON `sorcerySpeed` flag onto the runtime ability so JSON-defined cards (Walking Ballista's {4}: put counter) light up automatically. Nine named-factory retrofits flip the printed "Activate only as a sorcery" abilities through `sorcerySpeed: true`: `WishclawTalismanFactory`, `PriestOfFellRitesFactory`, `TasigurTheGoldenFangFactory`, `ColossusHammerFactory` / `SkullclampFactory` / `UmezawasJitteFactory` / `SwordOfFireAndIceFactory` / `SwordOfFeastAndFamineFactory` / `CoriSteelCutterFactory` (Equip {N}, CR 702.6a). `Majik.Core.Tests/Rules/SorcerySpeedActivationTests.cs` covers the four-timing-windows matrix on Walking Ballista plus instant-speed-rejection on Wishclaw and Tasigur, the instant-speed-ability-bypass path, and the backward-compat default. Mechanic-deps scanner: cluster #4 ("Activate only as a sorcery") moves from 9 blocked factories → 0 real (TirelessTracker false-positive remains). On top of Meltdown (Sorcery {X}{R} from Urza's Destiny — "Destroy each artifact with mana value X or less." `MeltdownFactory.Create` ships the sorcery shape; `BuildResolveEffect(caster, allPlayers, x)` returns a single `IEffect` that snapshots every supplied player's battlefield, filters via `OfType<Card>()` + `HasType(CardType.Artifact)` + `ManaCostValue.TotalValue ≤ x`, HashSet-dedupes via `ReferenceEqualityComparer.Instance`, and routes each victim to its owner's graveyard via `OracleSpellBinder.MoveToGraveyard` (CR 701.7). Mirrors `WrathOfGodFactory.BuildResolveEffect`'s multi-player iteration shape with the Pernicious-Deed-style `mv ≤ X` predicate narrowed to Artifact only. X provenance: callers pass the resolved X directly (same simplification as Pernicious Deed / Engineered Explosives). Single-arg dispatcher path produces card shape only — no resolve effect, since X is unknown until cast. Indestructible bypass is the same lossy gap inherited from `OracleSpellBinder.MoveToGraveyard` — same as Slaughter Pact / Murderous Rider / Drown in the Loch / Embereth Shieldbreaker / Wrath of God) on top of Monastery Swiftspear (Creature — Human Monk {R} 1/2 from Khans of Tarkir + many reprints — Modern Burn / Izzet Prowess one-drop. `MonasterySwiftspearFactory.Create` ships the 1/2 Human Monk shape with the printed Haste `KeywordAbility` marker (CR 702.10 — same wiring as `EarthshakerKhenraFactory` / `GoblinChieftainFactory` / `GoblinRabblemasterFactory`) AND a Prowess `KeywordAbility` marker (CR 702.108) for shape-only keyword inspection alongside the prowess `TriggeredAbility` mechanic itself via the shared `ProwessFactory.Build(card, effects)` helper (sibling of `MonasteryMentorFactory`'s prowess wire-up — same `ContinuousEffectsService`-driven `ProwessPumpEffect`-on-Layer-7c posture). The `(owner, eventBus, triggers, effects)` overload registers the prowess trigger with the supplied `TriggerManager` so a controller-cast noncreature `SpellCastEvent` fires the prowess pump on Swiftspear (CR 702.108's "noncreature spell" predicate enforced by `ProwessFactory.Build`'s `EventTriggerCondition<SpellCastEvent>` — controller-match + `!HasType(CardType.Creature)`); `Creature.ActiveEffects` is wired to the supplied service so `Creature.Power` / `Creature.Toughness` reads flow through the layers compute. Single-arg dispatcher path attaches both keyword markers but does NOT wire the trigger (no `ContinuousEffectsService` available at dispatch — shape-only posture, same as `MonasteryMentorFactory` / `GoblinChieftainFactory`). Distinct from Mentor's prowess wiring in one observable respect — Swiftspear surfaces a standalone `KeywordAbility("Prowess")` marker because the printed card line is literally "Prowess" (the vanilla keyword), whereas Mentor's reminder text is implicit in the trigger description and doesn't carry the marker. Vanilla-printed Prowess body — no ETB, no activated, no LTB clauses to defer; the only deferred surface is the same `TriggerManager` shape-vs-runtime split that every other prowess-bearing factory ships. Bumps Burn rollup to ~87% and Murktide / Izzet Tempo rollup to ~78%) on top of Geralf's Messenger (Creature — Zombie {B}{B}{B} 3/2 from Dark Ascension — Modern Mono-Black Aggro / Zombies finisher and a Yawgmoth-pillar Undying body. `GeralfsMessengerFactory.Create` ships the 3/2 Zombie shape, Undying `KeywordAbility` marker (CR 702.93) wired alongside the return-with-+1/+1-counter mechanic via the shared `UndyingFactory.Build` helper (same wiring Young Wolf / Strangleroot Geist would use). Enters-tapped replacement (CR 614.1c) via `EntersTappedReplacement` on the supplied `ReplacementBus` — unconditional, no gate (contrast Valakut's 5-Mountain check), so the replacement applies to every ETB including Undying returns. Single-arg dispatcher path omits the replacement (Messenger enters untapped on shape-only paths, mirroring Creeping Tar Pit / Valakut). ETB triggered ability (CR 603.6a + CR 119.3) wired via `Triggers.OnEnterBattlefieldSelf` with a 1..1 "target opponent" `TargetRequest`; on resolution the chosen player loses 2 life via `Player.LoseLife`. Mirrors `HidetsugusSecondRiteFactory.BuildSpellDefinition`'s target-opponent shape and Earthshaker Khenra's chosen-target gating (CR 608.2b — no/illegal target = clean no-op). Agent-driven "target opponent" prompt deferred — v1 honours pre-supplied `ChosenTargets`. Use the `(owner, eventBus, triggers, replacements)` overload for fully-wired behavior) on top of Drannith Magistrate (Creature — Human Wizard {1}{W} 1/3 from Ikoria: Lair of Behemoths — `DrannithMagistrateFactory.Create` ships the 1/3 Human Wizard shape plus the printed static "Your opponents can't cast spells from anywhere other than their hands" (CR 113.6 / CR 601.2a). Wired via a new `CastFromHandOnlyRestrictionEffect` lifecycle binder (sibling of `SorcerySpeedRestrictionEffect` — same Attach/Detach + `CardMovedEvent` ETB/LTB sync shape + same opaque-token + lock-gated registry pattern). While Drannith is on the battlefield, every player returned by `opponentResolver` is registered into a new `_castFromHandOnly` slot on `CastingRestrictions`; `ActionValidator.ValidateCastSpell` consults `CastingRestrictions.MustCastFromHand(player)` and rejects any cast whose declared source zone (`CastSpellAction.FromZone`) is not `ZoneType.Hand` — including spells cast from exile (cascade, suspend, foretell, alt-cost from-exile flows), the graveyard (flashback / disturb / aftermath / escape / jump-start), the library, or the command zone. Returns `RuleViolation 113.6`. Single-arg dispatcher path produces the correct card shape with no live opponent restriction (mirrors Teferi, Time Raveler's single-arg posture). Backward-compat: `CastSpellAction.FromZone` is optional — callers that don't stamp a source zone treat the restriction as no-op on the from-zone axis, since the production `SpellCaster` / `SpellCastFlow` pipeline does not yet stamp `FromZone` for every cast path. `CastSpellAction` adds a `(card, player, sorcerySpeedAvailable, fromZone)` overload preserving the existing 2/3-arg call sites. Emblem-with-static support inherited from the binder shape: pass `source: null` + `activeWhile: () => true` and the emblem registers immediately for the rest of the game (CR 114)) on top of Force of Vigor (Instant {2}{G}{G} from Modern Horizons — `ForceOfVigorFactory.Create` ships the instant shape and `BuildDefinition(targetResolver)` delegates to `DestroySpellFactory.DestroyUpToArtifactEnchantmentSpell(resolver, 2)` so the resolve behaviour is identical whether the spell is bound via `NamedCardFactory` dispatch or via the data-driven `DestroyUpToArtifactEnchantmentTemplate` oracle path (CR 601.2c — "up to two" allows 0..2 legal targets; CR 608.2b — non-Artifact/Enchantment target at resolution is a clean no-op). Pitch alternative cost (CR 118.9) reuses the shared `PitchAlternativeCost` (`RequiredColor: Green, LifeCost: 0`) — same Force-of-Negation timing pattern (not-your-turn predicate via `IsLegalInContext`) without the life rider. Bot probe via `PitchAltCostProbe.DefaultLookup` extended to recognise Force of Vigor as `(Green, 0)`; surfaces one candidate per green card in the caster's hand on the opponent's turn) on top of Aether Hub (Land from Kaladesh — `AetherHubFactory.Create` ships the Land shape, ETB triggered ability (CR 603.6a — "Aether Hub enters with an energy counter on it") that grants the controller one energy via `Player.GainEnergy` (CR 106.13) AND stamps a `CounterType.Energy` marker on the land for shape inspection, `{T}: Add {C}` mana ability, and five WUBRG `ManaAbility` instances each carrying the 3-arg-plus-cost overload — `canActivateCheck` = `!IsTapped` AND `controller.EnergyCounters >= 1` (CR 119.4 — you can't pay a resource you don't have); `additionalCostPayer` = `controller.PayEnergy(1)` running after the {T} tap. Same shape pattern as `CavernOfSoulsFactory` / `DelightedHalflingFactory` / `FieryIsletFactory`'s pain-cost overload. Single-arg dispatcher path attaches the ETB trigger without TriggerManager registration; `(owner, eventBus, triggers)` overload wires bus-driven firing (mirrors `TheOneRingFactory`'s two-arg pattern). Introduces `CounterType.Energy` as an on-card bookkeeping marker — the player-scoped `Player.EnergyCounters` ledger remains the source of truth) on top of Gurmag Angler (Creature — Zombie Fish {7}{B} 5/5 from Khans of Tarkir — `GurmagAnglerFactory.Create` ships the 5/5 Zombie Fish shape with a single Delve `KeywordAbility` marker (CR 702.66). Vanilla delve creature — no printed triggered or activated abilities — so the factory ends right after the keyword marker, sibling shape of `MurktideRegentFactory` minus the ETB trigger. The Delve mechanic itself lives in `DelveCost` + `SpellCastFlow`; callers cast via the cast-flow's `delveCost` parameter to substitute exiled graveyard cards for generic mana. Bot-side delve discovery deferred — same gap as Murktide Regent / Treasure Cruise; tests construct `DelveCost` explicitly and hand it to the cast flow. Introduces `CardSubtype.Fish` (creature subtype, CR 205.3m)) on top of Earthshaker Khenra (Creature — Minotaur Warrior {1}{R} 2/1 from Hour of Devastation — `EarthshakerKhenraFactory.Create` ships the printed Haste `KeywordAbility` marker (CR 702.10 — same wiring as Goblin Chieftain's and Goblin Rabblemaster's printed Haste) plus a single ETB triggered ability (CR 603.6a) wired via `Triggers.OnEnterBattlefieldSelf` with a 1..1 "target creature with power 2 or less" `TargetRequest` (Intent: `BotIntent.Removal`). On resolution the effect reads the trigger's `ChosenTargets`, walks the standard SolitudeFactory-shaped gates — illegal-target check for still-on-battlefield + resolution-time power-≤-2 recheck per CR 608.2b — and registers a `CombatRestrictionEffect(CombatRestriction.CannotBlock, target)` against the target's own `ContinuousEffectsService` (`Creature.ActiveEffects`) where the combat validator's `HasRestriction` lookup runs (CR 509.1c). Cap is supplied by an optional `Func<int> colorsSpentProvider`; the single-arg path defaults to `DefaultColorsSpent = 1` (the printed {W} pip), since the engine has no per-spell mana-provenance ledger yet. `ColorCount(string)` helper counts distinct WUBRG letters (case-insensitive, dedupes repeats, ignores generics + {C}) for integration adapters once a ledger exists. CR 608.2b — mv-above-cap / land target → no effect. Resolution exile mirrors Path to Exile's owner-routed zone move) on top of Oko, Thief of Crowns (Legendary Planeswalker — Oko {1}{G}{U} loyalty 4 from Throne of Eldraine — pillar of WAR/Eldraine standard and the modern poster child for "becomes-Elk" power-level concerns. +2 `TokenFactory.CreateFood` (CR 111.10 — {2}, {T}, Sacrifice: gain 3 life); +1 registers three continuous effects on the supplied `ContinuousEffectsService` — Layer 4 `OkoBecomesElkTypeEffect` (adds Creature + rewrites creature-subtype slot to Elk; Source anchored on Oko himself so a same-turn Layer 6 strip on the now-Elk creature doesn't recursively kill Oko's rider via the CR 613.8 dependency filter), Layer 6 `LoseAllAbilitiesEffect` scoped to the target (clears `chars.Keywords` per CR 613.6), Layer 7b `BecomesPTEffect(3, 3)` for Creature C# instances / `KarnAnimatedShimPTEffect` for non-Creature artifact targets (mirroring Karn's "shim for the future Compute-promotion world" pattern); colour-set to green DEFERRED (no Layer 5 primitive exists yet); deterministic auto-pick of first artifact/creature in supplied battlefield resolver. -5 exchange-control mechanic — deterministic auto-pick of first opponent-permanent (artifact or creature) + first Oko-controller creature; swaps `Permanent.Controller` and moves each card between the two players' battlefield zones (CR 110.2 ownership unchanged); counter-removal half is structural intent only (no CR 611 counter surface on `Permanent` yet). Single-arg dispatcher path attaches all three loyalty bodies; +1/-5 effect bodies gate on the (effects / battlefieldResolver / allPlayersResolver) wiring and no-op without it (loyalty changes still apply per CR 606.3); +2 still spawns a Food token on the single-arg path because `TokenFactory.CreateFood` operates on controller zones directly. Introduces `CardSubtype.Elk` (creature subtype) and `CardSubtype.Oko` (planeswalker subtype)) on top of Archmage's Charm (Instant {U}{U}{U} from Modern Horizons — modal "Choose one —" (CR 700.2d) with three printed modes: (0) counter target spell, (1) target player draws two cards, (2) gain control of target nonland permanent with mana value 1 or less. Card shape only at the dispatcher; bound `SpellDefinition` is built on demand via `ArchmagesCharmFactory.BuildDefinition(caster, targetResolver, stack[, effects])`. Three `TargetRequest`s with `MinTargets=0` so unchosen modes don't gate the cast (mirrors `CrypticCommandFactory`). Mode 0 reuses `OracleSpellBinder.RemoveFromStack` + graveyard zone-move (same shape as Cryptic Command's counter). Mode 1 pulls 2 cards from the *target player's* library — distinct from Cryptic Command's controller-only draw. Mode 2's "nonland permanent with mv ≤ 1" gate is enforced at resolution time (CR 608.2b — illegal target → effect does nothing) since the engine has no agent-side filter yet; on legal target, registers a `ControlChangeEffect` (CR 613.2, Layer 2) against the supplied `ContinuousEffectsService` swapping `EffectiveController` to the caster (underlying `Permanent.Controller` left untouched per Wishclaw Talisman's pattern). Single-arg dispatcher path no-ops mode 2's control swap when `effects` is null — counter / draw modes still resolve fully) on top of The One Ring (Legendary Artifact {4} — Indestructible keyword + ETB protection-from-everything trigger attached as a structural no-op (pending player-scoped protection layer + cast-marker on Card + "until your next turn" delayed cleanup) + upkeep trigger draining controller life by burden-counter count (CR 500.4 / CR 603.1) + activated {T} adds a `CounterType.Burden` then draws a card per burden counter on it (add-then-draw ordering per CR 608.2c). New `CounterType.Burden` registered alongside the existing Poison/Void/Time/Charge/Defense/Loyalty markers) on top of Atraxa, Grand Unifier (Legendary Creature — Phyrexian Angel {3}{W}{U}{B}{R}{G} 7/7 from Phyrexia: All Will Be One. Flying + Vigilance + Deathtouch + Lifelink keyword markers (CR 702.9 / 702.20 / 702.2 / 702.15). ETB triggered ability (CR 603.6a) reveals top 10 of controller's library; the resolver walks every `CardType` value in declaration order, picking the first peeked card that matches each type (a multi-type card claims a single slot — Artifact Creature is taken once, not twice). Picks go to hand; remainder re-bottoms shuffled via `Random.Shared` (CR 701.20a). Iteration is enum-driven so Battle (MoM+) lights up automatically once `CardType.Battle` is added. Selector exposed as `SelectOnePerCardType` for unit testability; resolver invoked directly by tests / bots since the dispatcher path attaches the trigger without TriggerManager wiring) on top of Sneak Attack (Enchantment {2}{R} from Urza's Saga — classic Reanimator / Sneak-and-Show enabler. `{R}` activated ability (CR 602) puts a creature card from the controller's hand onto the battlefield, grants Haste, and registers a one-shot `DelayedTriggeredAbility` (CR 603.7) that sacrifices the placed creature at the start of the next end step (CR 500.4 / CR 701.16 — controller's battlefield → owner's graveyard, fence-checked with `e.Timestamp > resolvedAt` to skip the current end step). Each activation closes over its own resolve-time creature pick + delayed sac trigger so multiple activations in the same turn each sacrifice their cheated-in creature independently. v1 deterministic first-creature-in-hand pick (auto-accepts the "you may" when a candidate exists — same shape as Through the Breach / Aether Vial / Goblin Lackey). Hand → battlefield routes through `ZoneService.MoveCard` so ETB triggers fire (CR 603.6a); Haste via `GrantKeywordUntilEndOfTurnEffect` (CR 613.1c Layer 6) plus `HasSummoningSickness=false` for attack-declaration (CR 702.10b — the EOT-scoped grant is observationally equivalent to the printed no-duration wording since the creature is sacrificed at the same boundary). No-creature-in-hand is a clean no-op) on top of Sheoldred, the Apocalypse (Legendary Creature — Phyrexian Praetor {2}{B}{B} 4/5 — Deathtouch keyword marker (CR 702.2); controller-only `CardDrawnEvent` triggered ability via `Triggers.OnCardDrawnByPlayer` filtered to the controller, fires once per drawn card (CR 603.1 / 603.2c), draining 2 life from every opponent supplied by an optional `opponentResolver` and gaining 2 for the controller — single-arg dispatcher path gains the 2 life on execute and silently no-ops the drain without a resolver, mirroring Liliana of the Veil's player-list-resolver shape; introduces a new `CardSubtype.Praetor` for the New Phyrexia / DMU praetor cycle) on top of Through the Breach (Instant {2}{R}{R} from Champions of Kamigawa — Modern Emrakul / Griselbrand enabler. Resolve picks the first creature in caster's hand (v1 deterministic "you may" auto-accept like Aether Vial), moves it hand → battlefield through `ZoneService.MoveCard` so ETB triggers fire (CR 603.6a), grants Haste EOT via `GrantKeywordUntilEndOfTurnEffect` (CR 613.1c Layer 6) and clears summoning sickness (CR 702.10b), then registers a one-shot `DelayedTriggeredAbility` (CR 603.7) that sacrifices the placed creature at the start of the next end step (CR 500.4 / CR 701.16 — controller's battlefield → owner's graveyard, fence-checked with `e.Timestamp > resolvedAt` to skip the current end step). No-creature-in-hand is a clean no-op. Splice onto Arcane (CR 702.46) deferred — no splice alt-cost primitive yet. Top-20 #5 cleared) on top of Show and Tell (Sorcery {2}{U} — each player may put an artifact, creature, enchantment, or land card from their hand onto the battlefield. Card shape only at the dispatcher; per-player resolve effect (iterate `allPlayers`, deterministic first-`Permanent`-in-hand pick via `OfType<Permanent>()` so CR 110.4 filters out Instant/Sorcery cards in hand, optional `ZoneService` routing so ETB triggers / replacements on the put-in permanent fire per CR 603.6a / CR 614, custom picker may return `null` to model the per-player "may" decline clause per CR 605.1 / 117.x) is built on demand via `ShowAndTellFactory.BuildResolveEffect(allPlayers, zoneService, picker)`. Mirrors the multiplayer-iteration shape of Wheel of Fortune and Stoneforge Mystic's hand→battlefield + attach activated ability) on top of Reanimate + Animate Dead (Sorcery {B} + Aura {1}{B} — single-target reanimation pair. Reanimate's `BuildResolveEffect` picks the first creature card from the caster's graveyard (`allPlayersResolver` overload scans every player's graveyard), moves it to the caster's battlefield via `ZoneService` so ETB triggers fire (CR 603.6a), then `LoseLife` = mana value (CR 202.3b). Animate Dead's `BuildSpellDefinition` surfaces creature CARDs across all supplied graveyards as a bespoke `TargetRequest`; on resolve the chosen card is reanimated and the aura auto-attaches BEFORE its own ETB (CR 303.4f) so `AttachedBoostEffect` and the LTB trigger see a populated `AttachedTo`. -1/-0 static at Layer 7c, LTB trigger sacrifices the attached creature (CR 701.16). v1 collapses the printed ETB Enchant-clause mode-shift into a single resolve effect) on top of Sol Ring + Mana Vault (Artifacts {1} — colourless mana rocks. Sol Ring is a clean single tap mana ability adding +2 generic via `ManaCost.Parse("CC")`. Mana Vault wires the +3 generic tap mana ability plus the upkeep "if tapped, pay {4} or take 1 damage" `TriggeredAbility` (CR 603.1 / 603.4) — v1 "may" collapses to pay-if-able via `PayMana({4})` with `LoseLife(1)` fallback, same prompt gap as the Pact cycle; the "doesn't untap during your untap step" static is deferred because no skip-untap engine surface exists today) on top of Dark Ritual + Lotus Petal (classic ritual + petal pair — Instant {B} adds {B}{B}{B} on resolution via `Player.AddManaToPool` (sibling of Cabal Ritual minus threshold); Artifact {0} with five WUBRG `ManaAbility` instances each gated on `!IsTapped && Zone == Battlefield` carrying an inline `additionalCostPayer` that performs the CR 701.16 sacrifice (controller's battlefield → owner's graveyard) so CR 605.1 holds and the activation stays off the stack) on top of Burst Lightning (Instant {R} — 2 damage to any target with a structural kicked-branch deals-4 instead; ships not-kicked because Kicker (CR 702.33) is not yet an `IAdditionalCost` primitive in the engine, so the wasKicked bit is supplied by the caller through `BurstLightningFactory.BuildSpellDefinition`) on top of Goblin Engineer (Creature — Goblin Artificer {1}{R} 1/2 — ETB tutor first artifact card from library → graveyard, NOT hand; {R}, {T}, Sacrifice an artifact: reanimate target artifact card from your graveyard; sacrifice resolved in effect body since no "sacrifice any one of N permanents" cost primitive exists yet) on top of Slaughter Pact + Pact of the Titan (Instants {0} — Future Sight Pact cycle on top of Pact of Negation; destroy target nonblack creature (CR 701.7 + CR 105 colour check via `CardColors`) and 4/4 Giant creature token (CR 111 / 111.6) respectively, each registering a delayed upkeep `DelayedTriggeredAbility` (CR 603.7) that tries `PayMana({2}{B})` / `PayMana({4}{R})` and falls back to `MarkLost()` on failure (CR 104.3 / 118.3)) on top of Vampiric Tutor (Instant {B} — search library for ANY card → top of library + lose 2 life; sibling of Mystical Tutor with no type predicate and an unconditional 2-life payment via `Player.LoseLife`; shuffle deferred — same rationale as the rest of the tutor surface) on top of Tendrils of Agony (Sorcery {2}{B}{B} — target opponent loses 2 life + you gain 2 life + Storm trigger (CR 702.40) reusing `StormHelper`; second storm spell after Brain Freeze, sharing the same copy-via-`SpellCopier` v1 semantics) on top of Pernicious Deed (Enchantment {1}{B}{G} — {X}, Sacrifice: destroy each artifact, creature, and enchantment with mv ≤ X across all battlefields; mirrors Engineered Explosives' v1 X-provider + sacrifice-stub pattern) on top of Dragon's Rage Channeler + Wheel of Fortune + Brain Freeze + Lightning Helix + Sword of Feast and Famine + Boros Reckoner + Mishra's Workshop + Goblin Welder + Sword of Fire and Ice + Reckless Charge + Trinket Mage + Sun Titan + Sensei's Divining Top + Inkmoth Nexus + Spell Queller + Goblin Matron + Mutavault + Skullclamp + Umezawa's Jitte + Wasteland + Swords to Plowshares + Mystical Tutor + Path to Exile + Daze + Ponder + Preordain + Splinter Twin + Sythis, Harvest's Hand + Pyromancer's Goggles + Plague Engineer + Manabarbs + Yawgmoth's Will + Wishclaw Talisman + Searing Blaze + Goblin Lackey + Damping Sphere.



## Headline numbers

| Metric | Count |
|---|---|
| Named factories | 1223 |
| Bespoke templates | 28 |
| Generic templates | 97 |
| JSON-defined cards | 143 |
| Distinct implemented card names (`[CardName]`) | 822 |
| Estimated Modern played-staple coverage | ~50% |

(Coverage estimate, 2026-05-26: a sampled diff of the live mtgtop8 Modern
metagame — 273 unique non-basic cards across 15 decklists / 14 archetypes —
found ~51% covered by named factories + land cycles + JSON cards. This is a
**lower bound**: it excludes regex spell-template coverage, so some "missing"
simple spells actually resolve. Gaps cluster in recent Universes Beyond
crossovers (Final Fantasy / Marvel / LOTR / Avatar) and 2025–26 set lands.
Distinct names > factory classes because cycle factories — fetch / shock /
surveil / pain / check / filter / horizon / bounce / manland — each register
many `[CardName]`s from one class.)

| Delver of Secrets | Creature | TBD | Creature — Human Wizard {U} 1/1 — DFC front face (Innistrad). Resolve closure: (1) peek the top of the controller's library (no zone move — CR 701.19 "look at"; empty library is a clean no-op, distinct from a draw so no `TriedToDrawFromEmptyLibrary` flag is set), (2) check the peeked card for `HasType(Instant) || HasType(Sorcery)`, (3) on match: publish `CardRevealedEvent` from `ZoneType.Library` (so portal/log subscribers can flash the revealed top) + flip `MdfcState.Transform()` to the back face (CR 701.28). Non-instant/sorcery top is silent — no reveal event and no transform. Two-overload shape: `Create(Player)` is shape-only (no bus / no TriggerManager registration — same posture as Dark Confidant single-arg path); `Create(owner, eventBus, triggers)` registers the trigger for bus-driven firing on `StepStartedEvent(Upkeep, controller)`. Deferred (v1 gaps): back-face hot-swap on the live Creature object (the MdfcState flips but the 1/1 Human Wizard P/T + Flying are not characteristic-replaced — Layer 0 / per-face cardpool support deferred, same v1 limit as `AjaniNacatlPariahFactory`); "you may reveal" agent prompt (v1 auto-reveals when the peeked card is instant/sorcery — non-instant/sorcery peeks skip the reveal, equivalent observable outcome since only instants/sorceries trigger the transform). Modern UR Delver pillar one-drop — pairs with Snapcaster Mage + Young Pyromancer + Lightning Bolt + Brainstorm-style cantrips for the canonical tempo shell. "Destroy target nonblack creature." `DoomBladeFactory.Create` ships the Instant shape via `CardDef.Instant`; `BuildDefinition(targetResolver)` exposes a single 1..1 "target nonblack creature" `TargetRequest` (`BotIntent.Removal`) with a candidate-gatherer that filters every player's battlefield to `Creature`s whose `CardColors.GetColors` does not contain `ManaColor.Black` (CR 105 — colour derived from mana cost pips; colourless / multicoloured-without-{B} all count as nonblack). | Endbringer | Creature | TBD | Creature — Eldrazi {5}{C} 5/5 (Oath of the Gatewatch). `EndbringerFactory.Create` ships the 5/5 colourless Eldrazi at {5}{C} with `KeywordAbility("Vigilance")` (CR 702.20) + `KeywordAbility("Reach")` (CR 702.17) markers (same combat-side consumer posture as Atraxa / Sun Titan / World Breaker — `CombatAbilities.HasReach` / `HasVigilance`). Three `ActivatedAbility` instances: (1) `{T}: 1 damage to any target` — sole cost `AdditionalCost.Tap(card)`, 1..1 "any target" `TargetRequest` (`BotIntent.Burn`); resolution routes through `Fx.DealDamageAny` so Planeswalker targets convert to loyalty removal (CR 306.7) — Lightning Bolt / Pyrite Spellbomb posture. (2) `{C}, {T}: target player draws a card` — cost stack `[ManaCostCost("{C}"), AdditionalCost.Tap(card)]`, 1..1 "target player" `TargetRequest` (`BotIntent.Draw`); resolution reads `ChosenTargets[0][0] as Player` with controller fallback (Nihil Spellbomb posture) and routes one draw through `Fx.DrawCards` so future `DrawCardIntent` replacement subscribers (Dredge / etc.) participate. (3) `{C}, {T}: tap target creature` — cost stack `[ManaCostCost("{C}"), AdditionalCost.Tap(card)]`, 1..1 "target creature" `TargetRequest` (`BotIntent.Removal` — "soft removal" semantics); resolution re-checks the chosen target is still a `Creature` on the battlefield (CR 608.2b) and taps via `Fx.Tap`. Tapping an already-tapped permanent is a no-op (CR 701.21b — `Permanent.Tap` is idempotent). **Deferred (v1)**: colourless-only payment enforcement on the {C} pips — `ManaCost.Parse` currently buckets `{C}` into generic and `ManaPaymentResolver` accepts generic mana for what should be a colourless-only slot (same engine-wide gap noted in the `Eldrazi Temple` row pending per-slot provenance work). Modern Eldrazi Tron utility staple — vigilance + reach + flexible activated abilities all on a {5}{C} chassis |

| Ensnaring Bridge | Artifact | TBD | Artifact {3} (Stronghold). "Creatures with power greater than the number of cards in your hand can't attack." `EnsnaringBridgeFactory.Create(owner, effects?)` ships the {3} artifact shape + a `StaticAbility` marker carrying the printed text gated on `card.Zone == ZoneType.Battlefield` (CR 113.3d / 603.6e). Working enforcement is a single predicate-mode `CombatRestrictionEffect` (`CombatRestriction.CannotAttack`) registered on the supplied game-level `ContinuousEffectsService`: the predicate is `c => c.Power > bridge.Controller.Zones.Hand.Count` and an `isActiveGate: () => bridge.Zone == Battlefield` suppresses the restriction off-battlefield (pruned by `ContinuousEffectsService.Prune`). `CombatRestrictionEffect` was extended with a new predicate-mode constructor (`Func<Creature, bool> predicate`, `Func<bool>? isActiveGate`) so dynamic per-creature conditions are recomputed on every `CombatValidator.CanAttack` pass — both the queried creature's live power (via the layer system) and the Bridge controller's live hand size track immediately. `ContinuousEffectsService.HasRestriction` evaluates the predicate when set, falling back to the existing single-target / mass modes otherwise. "Your" routes to the Bridge controller (CR 109.5) so the threshold is colour-blind / controller-blind — the Bridge controller's own big creatures are caught too (the classic 8-Rack / Lantern Control drawback). Shape-only `Create(owner)` path skips registration. Bot agent surface that proactively respects `CannotAttack` restrictions in attack proposals is deferred (same posture as Leyline Binding). Tests: `Majik.Core.Tests/CardData/Factories/EnsnaringBridgeFactoryTests.cs` (14 — identity + dispatch + static marker + battlefield gate + shape-only no-op + predicate `power > hand`/`==`/`<` + symmetric controller drawback + "your" scoping + draw lifts / discard reimposes + off-battlefield suppression + LTB pruned). Modern Lantern Control / 8-Rack / artifact-prison staple. |
| Explore | Sorcery | TBD | Sorcery {1}{G} (Worldwake / Modern Horizons 2). `ExploreFactory.Create` ships the sorcery card shape (`new Sorcery("Explore", "{1}{G}")`); the resolve body is built on demand via `ExploreFactory.BuildResolveEffect(caster, landDropTracker?)`. The closure runs two independent statements: (1) CR 305.2 — bumps the controller's per-turn land-drop cap by reading `LandDropTracker.MaxLandDropsThisTurn(caster)` and writing `current + 1` via `SetMaxLandDropsThisTurn`. (2) CR 121.1 — `Fx.DrawCards(caster, 1)` (empty-library path stamps the CR 704.5b / CR 120.3 `TriedToDrawFromEmptyLibrary` loss flag via `Fx.DrawCards`'s internal `MarkTriedToDrawFromEmptyLibrary` call). **`LandDropTracker` "add" surface audit**: as of PR #447 (LandDropTracker introduction) the tracker exposes only `SetMaxLandDropsThisTurn` (absolute setter) + `MaxLandDropsThisTurn` (reader) — there is no additive `AddExtraLandDrop` / `BumpMaxLandDropsThisTurn` method. Explore (and Azusa / Exploration / Oracle of Mul Daya when they ship) read current + write current+1; stack-safe (bump applied at resolution) + turn-safe (`LandDropTracker.ResetTurn` clears the per-turn max on every turn change via `TurnDriver`, so multi-turn carry-over isn't possible). Multi-Explore stacking is additive (each cast reads the post-prior cap). `BuildResolveEffect` accepts a null tracker for shape-only test paths — the draw still runs, bump is skipped. The printed "you may" is auto-accepted at v1 (same posture as Sneak Attack / Through the Breach pre-prompt). Amulet Titan ramp cantrip — gas + extra land drop in one card. |

Move Graveyard → Hand routes through `ZoneService.MoveCard` when supplied (publishes `CardMovedEvent` so any zone-change triggers fire per CR 603.6a / CR 701.20); otherwise direct-zone mutation. "You may" auto-accepted at v1 — same posture as Tireless Tracker / Phlage / Snapcaster Mage's ETB grant. Card-type agnostic (Instant / Sorcery / Creature / Land all returnable — verified via theory test). Single-arg dispatcher path attaches the trigger shape without TriggerManager registration; (owner, zoneService, eventBus, triggers) overload wires bus-driven firing + ZoneService-routed return. Modern Yawgmoth / Junk / Bant Eldrazi value-recursion staple |
| Esper Sentinel | Creature | TBD | Creature — Human Soldier {W} 1/1 (Modern Horizons 2). `EsperSentinelFactory.Create` ships the 1/1 Human Soldier shape + a single triggered ability over `SpellCastEvent` (CR 603.1) that fires on each opponent's FIRST noncreature spell each turn. Per-player first-cast tracking uses a `Dictionary<Player, int>` closure keyed by caster reference (mirrors Ledger Shredder's per-turn count shape but keyed per opponent so multi-opponent games surface each opponent's own first-cast independently). Predicate filters out (a) the controller's own spells ("an opponent casts") and (b) creature spells ("noncreature spell"); the exact transition from 0→1 fires, subsequent casts that turn don't retrigger (CR 603.2 — trigger only fires when the condition becomes true). On resolve, X is recomputed at resolve time as `owner.Zones.Battlefield.GetCards().Count(c => c.HasType(CardType.Creature) && c.Controller == owner)` per CR 608.2 (X = 0 is treated as a trivial auto-pay per CR 117.5 → no draw). The opponent caster is consulted via `Player.PayMana(ManaCost.Zero.AddGenericCost(x))` — same auto-pay-from-pool posture as Cursecatcher / Daze / Mana Leak (`ChooseYesNoAsync` agent prompt deferred — same queue as the Pact cycle / Cursecatcher's "unless pay" clause). On failure the controller draws 1 via `Fx.DrawCards`. Per-turn closure is reset on `TurnStartedEvent` (CR 500.1). Single-arg dispatcher path attaches the trigger shape without `TriggerManager` / bus wiring; `(owner, eventBus, triggers)` overload wires per-player count reset + trigger registration. Modern white prison / Hammer Time / Death and Taxes card-advantage staple. |

Resolve runs in two passes (`Resolve(chosenTargets)`): (1) destroy each chosen target still on the battlefield AND still Artifact or Creature via `Fx.MoveToGraveyard(ZoneMoveReason.Destroy)` (CR 701.7 — indestructible per CR 702.12 cancels, regen shield per CR 701.15 consumed normally); only count the destroy when the permanent actually left the battlefield (indestructible / regen → no reveal trigger per the "destroyed this way" clause); (2) for each destroyed permanent, its captured controller runs `RevealUntilPermanent(controller)` — peels off the top of the library until `IsNonlandPermanentCard` hits (CR 110.4 — Creature/Artifact/Enchantment/Planeswalker minus Land; Battle deferred since the engine enum doesn't carry it yet), the hit enters the battlefield under the revealing player's control, remaining peeled cards (lands / instants / sorceries) return to the library tail, `LibraryShuffle.ShuffleLibrary` shuffles (CR 701.20a). `IndomitableCreativityResolution` record (`Destroyed`, `Reveals`) + `RevealEvent` (`Peeled`, `Hit`) exposed for direct test/bot inspection. v1 gaps: reveal-event emission (same gap as Goblin Charbelcher / Ancient Stirrings); ZoneService routing for the Library → Battlefield cheat (ETB triggers on the reveal-cheated permanent don't fire); X-keyed `MinTargets = X` binding on `TargetRequest`. Top-20 #16 cleared — Modern 4c Creativity pillar (pairs with Dwarven Mine / Goblin Bombardment hits). |

| Inquisitor's Flail | Artifact | TBD | Artifact — Equipment {2} (Innistrad). `InquisitorsFlailFactory.Create` ships the {2} Equipment shape + Equip {2} via the shared `EquipActivatedAbility` primitive with the Puresteel zero-equip cost-provider hook — sorcery-speed gate + target-creature-you-control candidate gathering + attach-on-resolve all encapsulated (same wiring as Bone Saw / Colossus Hammer / Skullclamp / Sword of Fire and Ice). Two-overload shape: single-arg `Create(Player)` (shape-only dispatcher path — no `ReplacementBus` wiring; combat-damage doubling no-ops) + `Create(owner, ReplacementBus?)` for fully-wired behaviour. Both printed damage-doubling replacements (CR 614 / CR 510.1c) ship via two `DamageDoubleReplacement` registrations on the supplied bus: (1) **source-side** — "if equipped creature would deal combat damage, it deals double that damage instead" gates on `intent.Source == card.AttachedTo` AND `intent.IsCombatDamage`; (2) **target-side** — "if a source would deal combat damage to equipped creature, it deals double that damage instead" gates on `intent.TargetCreature == card.AttachedTo` AND `intent.IsCombatDamage`. Both gate on the Flail being on the battlefield AND currently attached, so detach / blink / bounce automatically suspends doubling without explicit deregistration. Non-combat damage paths (spells, ping abilities) leave `DamageIntent.IsCombatDamage` false and the gates short-circuit (no doubling of Lightning Bolt's targeted damage to the equipped creature, no doubling of the equipped creature's `{T}` ping ability). `DamageDoubleReplacement` is the generic "double a combat-damage intent matching a per-side predicate" replacement primitive — future retrofits (Furnace of Rath, Gisela the Broken Blade, Boros Charm's third mode) can lean on the same shape with their own gates |

| Into the Flood Maw | Instant | DONE | {U} Bloomburrow. `IntoTheFloodMawFactory.Create` returns the nested `IntoTheFloodMawCard : Instant, IGiftClause` so the cast-time gift hook is on the card instance; `BuildDefinition(caster, zoneService, card?)` wires a `TargetRequest.CandidateGatherer` that flips the live pool between "creature an opponent controls" (base) and "nonland permanent an opponent controls" (gifted) by reading `Card.HasGiftPromised` (stamped by `SpellCastFlow` after the cast-time gift prompt and BEFORE target collection so the gather closure sees the flip). Resolve body branches on the same flag: base mode bounces a Creature, gift mode bounces any nonland permanent — CR 701.20 + CR 701.59 upgrade. CR 608.2b resolution gate: still-on-battlefield + still-the-right-type + controller is an opponent of the spell's caster — illegal at resolution → no effect. Tokens follow CR 111.7 / SBA 704.5d: the bounce moves the token to its owner's hand, then `TokensCeaseToExistCheck` removes it on the next SBA pass. Gift delivery via `IntoTheFloodMawCard.DeliverTo` routes through `DeliverFishGift(recipient)` — `TokenFactory.CreateOnBattlefield` with `TokenSpec(Name: "Fish", Power: 1, Toughness: 1, Subtypes: [Fish], Colors: [Blue])` then `Tap()` (CR 701.59c — gift Fish enters tapped). **Engine v1 deviation**: gift delivery is CAST-TIME (strict CR 701.59 places it inside resolution); cast-time delivery keeps the gift on the recipient's board even when the gift-bearing spell is countered. Documented on `IGiftClause` + at the `SpellCastFlow` delivery call site. First Gift-bearing card in the engine + first Bloomburrow card. Tests: `Majik.Core.Tests/Spells/GiftMechanicTests.cs` (no-gift base bounce, gifted upgrade + Fish delivery + sentinel cleared post-resolve, counter survives, Description pinned, HeuristicBot defaults to promise) + relaxed `IntoTheFloodMawTests` shape assertion to `BeAssignableTo<Instant>` |

"Knight of the Reliquary gets +1/+1 for each land card in your graveyard. {T}, Sacrifice a Forest or Plains: Search your library for a land card, put that card onto the battlefield, then shuffle." `KnightOfTheReliquaryFactory.Create` ships the 2/2 Human Knight shape with two pieces: (1) Layer 7c self-pump (CR 613.1g) via `LandsInGraveyardPumpEffect` — counts the controller's graveyard land cards on every `ContinuousEffectsService.Compute` and applies +N/+N on top of the printed 2/2 base (mirrors `TerritorialKavuFactory`'s Domain-pump shape; printed base P/T 2/2 stands; reads `_source.Controller` live so control-change retargets correctly per CR 109.5). Lifecycle (ETB register / LTB unregister) via `ReliquaryPumpLifecycle` over `CardMovedEvent` — same pattern as Tarmogoyf's `TarmogoyfCdaLifecycle` + Territorial Kavu's `DomainPumpLifecycle`. (2) Activated ability "{T}, Sacrifice a Forest or Plains: tutor any land → battlefield, shuffle" — sole printed cost `AdditionalCost.Tap(card)` (Sacrifice payment performed inside the effect closure — first matching Forest / Plains on the controller's battlefield, v1 deterministic; matches Expedition Map / Mind Stone's sac-self closure-driven posture until the generic `AdditionalCost.Pay` sacrifice path lands). Tutor walks `controller.Zones.Library`, filters to `HasType(CardType.Land)`, prompts `IPlayerAgent.ChooseLibraryPickAsync` with deterministic first-land fallback (mirrors Expedition Map / Stoneforge Mystic / Sylvan Scrying), then moves Library → Battlefield via `ZoneService.MoveCard` when supplied (so ETB triggers + replacements on the tutored land fire — CR 603.6a / CR 614, PR #537) with raw zone manipulation fallback. CR 305.9 / 113.6c — putting a land directly onto the battlefield bypasses the per-turn land-drop cap. Tutored land enters untapped (printed text doesn't say "tapped"). Sacrifice-cost agent prompt + land-pick agent prompt + reveal-event emission all deferred — same gaps as every tutor factory. Pure helper `CountLandsInGraveyard(player)` exposed for tests + the pump evaluator. Single-arg dispatcher path attaches the pump effect structurally without ContinuousEffectsService / TriggerManager wiring; `(owner, effects, eventBus, zoneService)` overload wires the full lifecycle. Modern Junk / Death & Taxes / Knightfall combo pillar. Tests: `Majik.Core.Tests/CardData/Factories/KnightOfTheReliquaryFactoryTests.cs` |

"you may" auto-takes the free gain (v1 — no per-trigger decline prompt yet). Single-arg path attaches the trigger structurally; `Create(owner, TriggerManager?)` registers it for live `SpellCastEvent` dispatch. Modern/Legacy anti-burn sideboard staple. Tests: `Majik.Core.Tests/CardData/Factories/KorFirewalkerFactoryTests.cs`. |

Defensive chooser-validation guard (mirrors `AmpedRaptor`) rejects picks outside the eligible pool. Deferred (v1 gaps): `IActivatedAbility.CanActivate` hook (the legacy `canActivateCheck` lives only on `ManaAbility` today — see ChromaticStar / MoxOpal / NobleHierarch precedent; once the predicate surface lands, the static gate predicate is the natural attachment site), agent prompt for re-bottom ordering, reveal-event emission for the picked card. On resolve, the flicker effect inspects the latch (CR 603.4 intervening-if), then exile-and-returns the card through `ZoneService.MoveCard` (battlefield → exile → battlefield) with controller set back to owner — mirrors the Ephemerate / Restoration Angel flicker shape using the same `Permanent` instance (no clone — v1 simplification, identity preserved; the bus-level leaves/enters pair fires through `ZoneService.MoveCard`). (1) Upkeep ping (CR 603.1 / CR 500.4) via `Triggers.OnStepBegin(owner, PhaseStateType.Upkeep)`: 1 damage to each player on the controller's upkeep; iteration goes through the supplied `allPlayersResolver` (controller-only fallback mirrors Pernicious Deed / Meathook Massacre). Damage routes through `Player.LoseLife` (same v1 non-combat-damage shape as Manabarbs / Dark Confidant — no `DamageDealtEvent` route). (2) Free-cast 3-damage trigger (CR 603.1) over `SpellCastEvent` gated on the new `Spell.WasFreeCast` sentinel stamped by `SpellCastFlow` when the collapsed `totalCost.IsZero` (printed + alternative + cost reductions + +X + Delve) — captures Cascade, Suspend, Memnite-style {0} casts, and free pitch alt-costs (Force of Will / Misdirection). The casting player is captured via closure (Manabarbs pendingActivator pattern) and drained 3 on resolve. (3) `{1}{R}, Sacrifice Roiling Vortex: deal 3 damage to any target` activated ability (CR 602) with `ManaCostCost("{1}{R}")` + `AdditionalCost.Sacrifice(card)`; resolution dispatches damage via `OracleSpellBinder.DealDamage` (Player / Creature target). `AdditionalCost.Sacrifice` is the v1 no-op stub (same gap as Relic of Progenitus / Nihil Spellbomb) so the activation does not actually graveyard Vortex in v1. (4) "Players can't gain life" static (CR 614 / CR 119.6) registers a new `LifeGainIntent` lambda replacement on the supplied `ReplacementBus` rewriting every gain to `Amount = 0`. Players opt into the bus via the new `Player.AttachReplacementBus`; `Player.GainLife` consults the bus when attached and otherwise takes the direct mutation path it has always taken (backward-compat). Single-arg dispatcher path attaches both triggers + activated ability for shape but omits all service wiring (mirrors Valakut's posture); use `RoilingVortexFactory.Create(owner, triggers, replacements, allPlayersResolver)` for fully-wired behaviour. Clears top-20 #11. |

The self-exile branch is only reachable today via an opposing "you can't gain energy" replacement (none modelled), so in v1 the ETB always lands the prison with ≥2 stasis counters. Each-upkeep triggered ability (CR 500.4 — printed "at the beginning of each upkeep" scopes to BOTH players' upkeeps; raw `EventTriggerCondition<StepStartedEvent>` filtered to `PhaseStateType.Upkeep` with NO controller filter, contrast `Triggers.OnStepBegin` which restricts to controller's own upkeep): `CounterCollection.Remove(CounterType.Stasis)` — 0-safe (CR 122.6). Printed static "Permanents enter tapped while it has a stasis counter on it" (CR 614.1c) wired via a global `LambdaReplacement<ZoneMoveIntent>` on the supplied `ReplacementBus` whose Applies predicate matches any non-Static-Prison card entering the battlefield AND gates on (a) Static Prison on battlefield + (b) ≥1 stasis counter on it; Replace rewrites the intent with `EntersTapped=true` so `ZoneService` taps the entering permanent on landing. The replacement is board-symmetric (any controller). The replacement automatically deactivates when stasis hits 0 (upkeep drain) — gate check short-circuits without unregistering. III — search controller's library for an artifact card with `ManaCost.Parse(c.ManaCost).TotalValue ≤ 2`, put it onto the battlefield (routed through `ZoneService.MoveCard` when supplied so ETB triggers fire — CR 603.6a; raw zone fallback otherwise), then shuffle via `LibraryShuffle.ShuffleLibrary` (CR 701.20a). Self-sacrifice after III handled by the generic `SagaSacrificedCheck` SBA (CR 714.5 / 704.5r) — `SagaState.ShouldBeSacrificed` flips once lore counters hit final chapter (3). `SagaBinder.Bind` extended to take optional `ContinuousEffectsService` + `ZoneService` parameters; `ScryfallCardFactory` forwards its `_effects` + `_zones` so the production load path wires the Construct CDA rider + tutor ETB routing automatically. Agent-driven tutor pick (CR 701.19a) deferred — v1 picks first matching artifact by library order (same posture as Stoneforge Mystic's ETB tutor + Chord of Calling's no-agent fallback). |
