Both triggers re-check legality at resolve (CR 608.2b — Land + !Basic + on-battlefield for ETB; coloured + on-battlefield for attack) before exiling via `ZoneService.MoveCard` when supplied or raw zone manipulation otherwise (`CardMovedEvent` posture parallels Ulamog's `ExileFromBattlefield` helper). Graveyard-zone activated ability — `{G}, Exile this card from your graveyard: Return this card to its owner's hand` (CR 113.6 + CR 117.1a) — wired as `ActivatedAbility` with `ManaCostCost("{G}")`; the exile-self-from-graveyard cost half is performed inside the resolve closure (no `ExileSelfFromGraveyardCost` primitive yet, same posture as Priest of Fell Rites / Phlage). Zone guard rejects activations from non-graveyard zones to keep shape tests honest while engine zone-scoping of activated abilities (CR 113.6) is deferred. Single-arg dispatcher path + `(owner, zones, triggers)` overload mirror Ulamog's wiring posture. Modern Eldrazi Tron / Mono-G Tron staple |
| Wrenn and Six | Planeswalker | #178 | Legendary Planeswalker — Wrenn {R}{G} starting loyalty 3 (Modern Horizons). `WrennAndSixFactory.Create` ships the Legendary Planeswalker — Wrenn shape with three `LoyaltyAbility` arms at +1 / -1 / -7. **+1** "Return up to one target land card from your graveyard to your hand" — v1 deterministic auto-pick (first `HasType(Land)` card in controller's graveyard); empty graveyard is a legal no-op ("up to one" — CR 115.1b); loyalty still applies. **-1** "Lands you control gain reach and `{T}: This land deals 1 damage to any target` until end of turn" — body is a deferred no-op (CR 613.4 — engine has no until-EOT batch-grant primitive for keyword + activated-ability grant onto controlled lands yet); loyalty change still applies so the click is legal. **-7** "You get an emblem with `Instant and sorcery cards in your graveyard have retrace`" — creates a structural `Emblem` (CR 114) in the controller's `Player.Emblems` collection with `SourceName = "Wrenn and Six — retrace emblem"` and `abilities = []`; retrace cost-replacement (CR 702.81 — discard a land card as an additional cost to cast an instant or sorcery from the graveyard) NOT wired (same posture as Yawgmoth's Will's grave-cast surface — emblem is shape-only). Targeting prompt for +1 deferred (LoyaltyAbility doesn't declare `TargetRequest`s yet; deterministic pick suffices for engine tests). Tests: `Majik.Core.Tests/CardData/WrennAndSixTests.cs` (7 — identity (Legendary + Wrenn subtype + loyalty 3 + {R}{G}), loyalty-ability shape (+1 / -1 / -7), +1 returns land to hand, +1 no-land-in-graveyard legal no-op + loyalty still applies, +1 prefers Land over Instant in mixed graveyard, -7 emits emblem into controller command zone, NamedCardFactory dispatch). Audit verdict: +1 + -7 wired end-to-end (modulo agent prompt + retrace cost-replacement); -1 deferred behind the engine's missing until-EOT batch-grant primitive — clearly documented in xmldoc + table row. |

"This artifact enters tapped" (CR 614.1c) is applied on the production load path by `EntersTappedBinder` (matches the seed oracle text, registers an `EntersTappedReplacement` on the live `ReplacementBus`); the factory builds the rock untapped — same JSON/binder division of labour as the surveil-land cycle. |

Card shape only at the named-factory dispatcher; resolve effect (sweep every `Creature` on every supplied player's battlefield → owner's graveyard via `OracleSpellBinder.MoveToGraveyard`, CR 701.7) is built on demand via `WrathOfGodFactory.BuildResolveEffect(allPlayers)`. Distinct from `DestroyAllCreaturesTemplate.Rehydrate` which only sweeps the caster's battlefield — the factory carries the multi-player sweep locally. "Can't be regenerated" rider + indestructible bypass are lossy at v1 (no CR 701.15 regen plumbing, no CR 702.12 indestructible surface on `MoveToGraveyard`) — same gaps as the sweep template |

| Wrath of the Skies | Sorcery | TBD | {X}{W}{W} — "You may pay {E}{E}{E}{E} rather than pay this spell's mana cost. Destroy each nonland permanent with mana value X or less." Modern Horizons 3. Card shape only at the named-factory dispatcher; resolve effect built on demand via `WrathOfTheSkiesFactory.BuildResolveEffect(caster, allPlayers, x)` — snapshot every supplied battlefield, filter to nonland permanents whose `Card.ManaCostValue.TotalValue` is ≤ X, HashSet-dedupe, route each through `OracleSpellBinder.MoveToGraveyard` (CR 701.7). `BuildSpellDefinition(caster, allPlayers)` wires `HasVariableX = true` with no `TargetRequest`s (untargeted sweep, CR 701.7 + CR 109.5). Printed energy alt-cost (CR 118.9 + CR 106.13) wired via the new `EnergyAlternativeCost` surface — `WrathOfTheSkiesFactory.BuildAlternativeCost()` returns a 4-pip `EnergyAlternativeCost` and the bot-side `EnergyAltCostProbe` surfaces the candidate when the caster has ≥4 energy. Per CR 107.3b — when the energy alt-cost replaces the printed mana cost, X = 0 (no value specified by the alt-cost) → cast via energy sweeps only mv-0 nonland permanents (tokens). Indestructible bypass is lossy at v1 — same gap as `WrathOfGodFactory` / `EngineeredExplosivesFactory` |

### Template-covered (notable)

Cards implemented through generic or bespoke templates without a named factory. Selected highest-value Modern staples:

- Lightning Bolt — `Damage/DamageAnyTargetTemplate`
- Lava Spike — `Damage/DamagePlayerTemplate`
- Lava Dart — `Damage/DamageAnyTargetTemplate` (flashback handled by KeywordBinder)
- Skewer the Critics — `Damage/DamagePlayerTemplate`
- Mana Tithe — `Counter/CounterUnlessPayTemplate`
- Spell Pierce — `Counter/CounterUnlessPayTemplate` (regex broadened to recognize the "noncreature" type qualifier; pay rider consults the target spell's controller's mana pool)
- Counterspell — `Counter/CounterTargetSpellTemplate`
- Boros Charm — `Bespoke/StriveTemplate`-adjacent
- Boltwave — `Damage/DealsNDamageEachOpponentTemplate`
- Goblin Guide — vanilla keyword binding (Haste + reveal-trigger TBD)
- Eidolon of the Great Revel — triggered-ability binder (cheap-spell pattern)
- Monastery Swiftspear — Prowess keyword (`Keywords/ProwessFactory`)
- Fulminator Mage — `FulminatorMageFactory` (sacrifice-self → destroy nonbasic land, Wasteland-shape twin minus tap)
- Force of Vigor — `Destroy/DestroyUpToArtifactEnchantmentTemplate` + pitch cost
- Fatal Push — `Destroy/DestroyCreatureCmcLimitTemplate`
- Thoughtseize — `Bespoke/ThoughtseizePatternTemplate`
- Inquisition of Kozilek — `Bespoke/InquisitionOfKozilekPatternTemplate` (nonland + mv ≤ 3 cap; no life cost)
- Expressive Iteration — `Bespoke/ExpressiveIterationTemplate`
- Brainstorm — `Bespoke/BrainstormTemplate` (draw 3, put 2 from hand on top of library; v1 deterministic return order)
- Malevolent Rumble — `Bespoke/MalevolentRumblePatternTemplate` + `MalevolentRumbleFactory` (named-factory parallel: `Create(owner)` ships the Sorcery {1}{G} shape, `BuildResolveEffect(caster)` mirrors the template's reveal-4 / first-permanent-to-hand / rest-to-graveyard / `TokenFactory.CreateEldraziSpawn` body; `NamedCardFactory` dispatches the printed name to the factory so bot-deck loaders / shape-only fixtures bypass the Scryfall round-trip).
- Fetch lands cycle (10 of them) — `OracleLandActivatedAbilityBinder`
- Shock lands (Overgrown Tomb + cycle starts) — `Effects/ShockLandReplacement`
- Sacred Foundry, Steam Vents — shock-land binding
- Undying creatures (Young Wolf, Strangleroot Geist, Butcher Ghoul, Geralf's Messenger) — `Keywords/UndyingFactory`
- Ashiok, Dream Render — loyalty-ability binder (mill -1)
- Fable of the Mirror-Breaker — Saga chapter binder (partial)
- The Legend of Roku — Saga chapter binder (partial)

Cast-as-Adventure from hand routes through `SpellCastFlow` with the Adventure mana cost (sorcery-speed gate enforced for sorcery Adventures via `IsLegalInContext`); on resolve `Spell.PostResolutionZoneOverride = Exile` re-routes the card to exile (CR 715.3d) instead of the printed-type default (battlefield for creatures), and `AdventureAlternativeCost.OnResolved` stamps a runtime exile-cast grant via `Card.GrantRuntimeExileCast` so the owner may cast the creature face from exile via `ExileCastAlternativeCost` for the printed cost. Bonecrusher Giant / Murderous Rider / Embereth Shieldbreaker factories rewired through this surface. |

Attaches a `KeywordAbility "Modular N"` marker, registers `EntersWithCountersReplacement(card, N)` against the supplied ReplacementBus so the ETB +1/+1 stamp routes through `ZoneService` → `CountersService.Add` (Hardened Scales bumps apply — PR #494), and wires the Battlefield → Graveyard death trigger that snapshot-reads the dying permanent's +1/+1 counter total off the graveyard object (Undying-shape) and routes the bestowal through `CountersService.Add` onto the controller-side first-artifact-creature target. The "you may" rider consults `IPlayerAgent.ChooseYesNoAsync(BotIntent.CardAdvantage)` — default = yes. Deferred: per-target prompt (deterministic v1 pick), cross-battlefield target enumeration (controller-only scan in v1). | Arcbound Ravager, Arcbound Worker, Arcbound Stinger |
| Dredge | Done | `Keywords/DredgeFactory.cs` (CR 702.52) — `Build(source, n, replacementBus?, eventBus?)` attaches a `KeywordAbility "Dredge"` marker (Arg = N) plus a graveyard-anchored `LambdaReplacement<DrawCardIntent>` on the supplied `ReplacementBus`. The replacement gates on (a) would-draw player == source.Owner, (b) source.Zone == Graveyard (CR 702.52a), (c) owner.Library.Count >= N (CR 702.52b), (d) agent yes (`IPlayerAgent.ChooseYesNoAsync` with `BotIntent.CardAdvantage`). On yes: mill N via `MillAction.Apply`, return source Graveyard → Hand, cancel the underlying draw (return null). New `Effects/DrawCardIntent.cs` intent + `Fx.DrawCards` routes each individual draw through the player's attached bus when present. No-bus shape-only path attaches the marker without firing a replacement. | Life from the Loam (Dredge 3), Stinkweed Imp (Dredge 5), Golgari Grave-Troll (Dredge 6) |

Attaches a `KeywordAbility "Persist"` marker + the Battlefield → Graveyard death trigger with the `InterveningIf` "no -1/-1 counters at death" gate (CR 603.4). Resolution body raw-moves Graveyard → Battlefield (UndyingFactory-shape — no `CardMovedEvent` re-publish), clears the counter bag (CR 121.2), then adds exactly one `CounterType.MinusOneMinusOne` via `CountersService.Add(replacements)` for symmetry with the Undying / Modular return-side counter route. ActiveZones = {Battlefield, Graveyard} so the trigger evaluates after the death zone-move. | Kitchen Finks, Murderous Redcap, Glen Elendra Archmage |

Stormchaser's Talent retrofit ships both level-up activated abilities (`{1}{U}{R}: Level 2`, `{3}{U}{R}: Level 3` — `sorcerySpeed: true` enforced by `ActionValidator` per PR #460) + the Level-2 trigger ("Whenever you cast a noncreature spell, the Mercenary deals 1 damage to any target") + the Level-3 trigger ("draw a card, then discard a card"), both gated by `TriggeredAbility.InterveningIf` on `ClassState.CurrentLevel >= N` (CR 603.4). "The Mercenary" referent captured via a per-Class spawned-token holder. "Any target" picker is the v1 deterministic resolver (`opponentResolver` closure — first opponent); the Level-3 loot discard is the deterministic last-in-hand picker (same posture as Faithless Looting). Live Prowess pump on the token + true agent-driven any-target prompt remain deferred behind the broader token-keyword + prompt surfaces. |

### Infra / build

| Surface | Status | File |
|---|---|---|
| `NamedCardFactory` source-gen dispatch | Done | `Majik.Core.SourceGen/NamedCardFactoryGenerator.cs` — Roslyn `IIncrementalGenerator` scans every `[CardName("...")]`-attributed factory class and emits the dispatch switch into a partial `NamedCardFactory.CreateGenerated(string, Player)` method. Replaces the hand-maintained 296-arm switch (3581 → 97 lines) so adding a card is now "drop a factory file with `[CardName("Foo")]`" — no edits to the shared dispatch file required, eliminating the merge-conflict hotspot. Diagnostics: `MJK001` (duplicate `[CardName]` across factories — error), `MJK002` (factory missing `Create(Player)` overload — error). Generated file lives at `Majik.Core/obj/$(Configuration)/$(TargetFramework)/generated/Majik.Core.SourceGen/…` (EmitCompilerGeneratedFiles=true). |
| `scaffold-factory` CLI subcommand | Done | `Majik.Console/Commands/ScaffoldFactoryCommand.cs` + `ScaffoldFactoryGenerator.cs` + `ScryfallNamedLookup.cs` — `dotnet run --project Majik.Console -- scaffold-factory "<Card Name>" [--out <path>] [--force]` pulls the card via `ICardRepository.GetByName` against `cards.db`, falls back to `https://api.scryfall.com/cards/named?exact=…` when unknown locally, then writes a starter `<Slug>Factory.cs` into `Majik.Core/CardData/Factories/` with the `[CardName]` attribute, oracle-text docstring, ctor pre-filled from Scryfall (`Creature`/`Instant`/`Sorcery`/`Enchantment`/`Artifact`/`Land`/`Planeswalker` chosen via the same primary-type preference as `ScryfallCardFactory.PickPrimaryType`), and `// TODO: resolve body` markers. Coverage tier is reported up front via `CoverageClassifier` and prompts before overwriting an already-covered card (skip with `--force`). Refuses to overwrite an existing file without `--force`. Goal: cut per-card writing cost by ~50% by eliminating the boilerplate. |
| `ScryfallCardFactory` golden-snapshot suite | Done | `Majik.Core.Tests/Snapshots/` — 200-card golden-snapshot regression suite for the full `ScryfallCardFactory.Create` pipeline. Each fixture card has a bundled `CardEntity` row (`card-data/<slug>.json`) and a deterministic snapshot of the post-binder output (`snapshots/<slug>.json`): card name + mana cost + primary/super/sub types + P/T + loyalty + per-shape ability counts/descriptions (keyword markers, mana abilities, activated/triggered/static — sorted alphabetically) + ReplacementBus rider counts. Reflection over `ReplacementBus._effects` introspects the riders the binders pushed for snapshotting; no production code touched. Re-record via `SNAPSHOT_RECORD=1 dotnet test` against a local `cards.db`. Selection spans all binders: 30 lands (basics + shocks + fetches + horizon + Tron + manlands), 30 keyword creatures, 20 replacement-effect cards, 7 multi-face, 49 templated spells, 49 named-factory cards, + 15 fill from `[CardName]` registry. Three sanity tests confirm fixture size = 200, every card builds a non-empty snapshot, and two builds of the same card produce byte-identical output. |
| Fluent `CardDef` DSL | Done | `Majik.Core/CardData/Definitions/{CardDef,CardDefBuilder,ResolveBuilder,CardDefRuntime}.cs` — fluent code-side card builder for simple factories: a factory's `Create(Player)` collapses to `public static CardDef Define() => CardDef.Creature(...).WithSubtype(...).WithKeyword(...);` plus an optional one-line typed shim. The source generator detects `static CardDef Define()` and synthesizes the dispatch arm calling `CardDefRuntime.Build(Define(), owner)` directly when no `Create(Player)` is declared (Define-only path), or routes through the typed shim when present. Resolve bodies (`.Resolve(c => c.DealDamage(3).To(TargetKind.AnyTarget))`) materialize into the engine's existing `IEffect` list via inline builders; effects-primitive library integration is gated by `TODO(effects-primitives)` markers awaiting `Majik.Core/Effects/Primitives/`. 9 pilot factories migrated (BadgermoleCub, GurmagAngler, PhoenixOfAsh, OrcishBowmasters, Grist, SolRing, MishrasWorkshop, ForceOfWill, ForceOfNegation, ForceOfVigor) — average factory drops from ~50 LOC to ~10 LOC of fluent calls. |

## Coverage by archetype

- **Burn** — Saturated. Lightning Bolt, Lava Spike, Lava Dart, Skewer the Critics, Boros Charm, Eidolon of the Great Revel, Goblin Guide, Monastery Swiftspear (`MonasterySwiftspearFactory` — 1/2 Human Monk {R} with Haste + Prowess `KeywordAbility` markers + prowess `TriggeredAbility` via the shared `ProwessFactory.Build` helper), Rift Bolt, Searing Blaze, Lightning Helix, Sunscorched Desert (`SunscorchedDesertFactory` — Land from HOU; unconditional ETB-tapped replacement via `EntersTappedReplacement` on the supplied `ReplacementBus` + ETB triggered ability dealing 1 damage to any target via `SearingBlazeFactory.DealDamageWithPlaneswalker` + `{T}: Add {C}`), Roiling Vortex (`RoilingVortexFactory` — {R} Enchantment with the upkeep 1-to-each-player + free-cast 3-to-the-caster + {1}{R}/sac 3-to-any-target activated + "players can't gain life" replacement; closes the Modern Burn sideboard hate slot against UWx control and lifegain decks) all in. ~100%.
- **Death's Shadow** — Mid-high. Thoughtseize, Fatal Push, Snapcaster Mage, Stubborn Denial, Death's Shadow itself (CDA P/T scaled by controller life — Layer 7a) all in. Mishra's Bauble in. Temur Battle Rage absent. ~60%.
- **Murktide / Izzet Tempo** — High. Murktide Regent done, Counterspell done, Snapcaster Mage done, Lightning Bolt done, Expressive Iteration done, Ledger Shredder done, Consider done, Spell Pierce done, Subtlety done. Monastery Swiftspear done (`MonasterySwiftspearFactory` — Modern Izzet Prowess one-drop, Haste + Prowess wiring via the shared `ProwessFactory.Build` helper). Sprite Dragon done (`SpriteDragonFactory` — Modern Izzet Prowess two-drop, Flying + cast-noncreature +1/+1 counter accumulator; same predicate shape as `ProwessFactory` but persistent counters instead of one-turn pump). Soul-Scar Mage done (Prowess 1-drop that converts noncombat damage from controller-owned sources to -1/-1 counters on opponent creatures — first `IReplacementEffect<DamageIntent>` in the engine; pairs with Swiftspear and Sprite Dragon as the Izzet Prowess cheap-creature trio). Cori-Steel Cutter done (`CoriSteelCutterFactory` — {1}{R} Equipment from Tarkir: Dragonstorm; static "+1/+1 + trample + haste" via paired `AttachedBoostEffect` registrations at Layers 7c / 6, Flurry — second-spell-each-turn trigger spawning a 1/1 white Monk token with prowess + auto-attaching; Equip {1}{R}). Demilich done (`DemilichFactory` — {3}{U}{U}{U} 4/3 Zombie Wizard with Flying; `CostReductionAbility(totalReducer)` counts instant+sorcery cards in caster's graveyard for {U}-less-per-card discount floored at the three blue pips per CR 117.7c; on-cast `SpellCastEvent` self-trigger exiles two instants/sorceries from caster's graveyard with agent-prompt-via-`ChooseFromPileAsync` or deterministic first-two fallback). ~95%.

  **Izzet Tempo audit (this PR) — deferred riders enumerated, not fixed:**
  - `LedgerShredderFactory` — `SurveilEvent` shipped (Events/SurveilEvent.cs + Fx.Surveil bus publish + Triggers.OnSurveil) and the surveil-self counter trigger now subscribes to it directly. First-spell-each-turn counter still increments on every controller `SpellCastEvent`, including Ledger Shredder's own cast (intentional, but callers exercising the trigger manually must publish a `SpellCastEvent` for the first spell too).
  - `MurktideRegentFactory` — +1/+1 counters land via the ETB effect rather than a CR 122.1g "as it enters" replacement (no general 122.1g infrastructure). Delve count is stamped on `Card.PendingDelveExiledCount` rather than threaded through a generalised X-counter ETB framework. ETB-exile counts alongside delve exiles in the counter total (matches the v1 spec but not strict CR 122.1g ordering).
  - `ConsiderFactory` — surveil decision quality lives in registered `IPlayerAgent` impl; deterministic fallback sends peeked card to graveyard. No deferred rider on the printed effect itself.
  - `ExpressiveIterationTemplate` — deterministic 3-way split (first→hand, second→bottom, third→exile) instead of player-choice; needs a "choose-among" decision shape on the agent. "You may play the exiled card this turn" rider absent — requires the cast-from-exile-this-turn permission tracker (CR 118.10) shared by Lurrus / Den of the Bugbear / similar.
  - `ArclightPhoenixFactory` — graveyard-resident trigger's `activeZones = {Graveyard}` relies on `TriggerManager` respecting graveyard triggers; bus-driven firing requires the `(owner, bus, triggers)` overload. Cast count is captured at begin-combat-step time — spells cast after the trigger resolves don't retroactively requalify the Phoenix the same turn. "You may" prompt routes through `IPlayerAgent.ChooseYesNoAsync` (`BotIntent.Reanimate | BotIntent.CardAdvantage`) when an agent is supplied, auto-accept otherwise.
  - `DemilichFactory` (new) — flashback-style cast-from-graveyard-by-exiling-four clause from the real Innistrad print is deliberately NOT shipped (this v1 follows MODERN_COVERAGE row #10's engine-friendly spec). Attack-trigger copy-from-graveyard clause from the real print is also not in scope. On-cast exile is non-optional (no "may"). Agent prompt for the two graveyard cards uses `BotIntent.None`.
- **Mono-Green Tron** — High. Ancient Stirrings, Sylvan Scrying, Wurmcoil Engine done. Karn Liberated done. Karn, the Great Creator done. Tron lands (Urza's Mine + Tower + Power Plant) done with the conditional {2} mana ability — note the Power Plant `[CardName]` was registered under the hyphenated subtype spelling ("Urza's Power-Plant") until 2026-05-26 and never matched the real seed card, so Tron silently could not assemble; fixed to the space-form printed name. Ugin's Labyrinth now done (`UginsLabyrinthFactory`). Missing top-end: Emrakul, the Promised End, Devourer of Destiny. ~70%.
- **Living End / Crashing Footfalls cascade** — High. Cascade keyword done (`Keywords/CascadeAction.cs`) + Crashing Footfalls shipped (#219). Living End shipped (this PR) with both the Cascade trigger and the resolve chain (per-player mass-exile-grave + sacrifice-creatures + mass-reanimate; ETB triggers fire on reanimated permanents via PR #174 plumbing). Suspend itself is done (#183). ~75%.
- **Rakdos Scam** — High. Grief done (#205, mirrors Solitude evoke + ETB pattern). Fury done (mirrors Solitude/Grief). Ragavan, Nimble Pilferer done (combat-damage Treasure + exile + may-cast EOT grant; Dash deferred). Dauthi Voidwalker done (Shadow + opponent-grave→exile-with-void-counter replacement effect + {2},{T},remove-void-counter activated cast-from-exile via CastFromExileAlternativeCost; EOT "this turn" timing on the cast permission deferred). Liliana of the Veil done, Fatal Push done, Thoughtseize done. ~75%.
- **Yawgmoth combo** — High. Yawgmoth done. Yawgmoth's Will done (Sorcery {2}{B} — stamps Card.RuntimeGraveyardCastCost on every card in controller's graveyard + EOT-expirable grave→exile ZoneMoveIntent replacement; covers the "play cards from your graveyard" + "exile instead of graveyard" oracle clauses). Undying engine done — Young Wolf (1/1 {G} Wolf, `YoungWolfFactory`), Strangleroot Geist (2/1 {G}{G} Spirit with a Haste rider, `StrangleRootGeistFactory`), and Geralf's Messenger (3/2 {B}{B}{B} Zombie with the unconditional enters-tapped replacement + ETB target-opponent-loses-2-life trigger, `GeralfsMessengerFactory`) all ship as dedicated factories built on the shared `UndyingFactory.Build` helper; Butcher Ghoul still auto-binds via `KeywordBinder` + `UndyingFactory` against any Scryfall row whose oracle prints the Undying keyword. Chord of Calling done. Eldritch Evolution done — tutor-with-sac is a primary engine starter for the deck. Green Sun's Zenith done — `GreenSunsZenithFactory` ({X}{G} Sorcery — tutor green creature mv ≤ X → battlefield with ZoneService-routed ETB triggers + printed self-shuffle into owner's library per CR 608.2c override, mirroring `EldritchEvolutionFactory`'s self-exile sequencing); the deck's premier toolbox tutor, fetches Wall of Roots / Dryad Arbor / Yawgmoth itself / Strangleroot Geist on curve. Eternal Witness done (ETB return-target-card-from-graveyard-to-hand; agent target + first-card fallback; "you may" auto-accepted v1; card-type agnostic) — recursion glue for the deck's Yawgmoth's Will / Chord / Eldritch Evolution loops. Wall of Roots done (Defender + once-per-turn place-(-0/-1)-counter-on-self mana ability via the new no-tap ManaAbility overload — green ramp into turn-3 Yawgmoth + counter-manipulation synergy with the -1/-1 sac engine). Ignoble Hierarch done (mono-G BRG mana-fixer + Exalted on a 0/1 Goblin Shaman body — same shape as Noble Hierarch with the mana colours Jund-aligned for the Yawgmoth shell). Soul-Scar Mage done — every noncombat damage event from a controller-owned source stamps -1/-1 counters on opponent creatures, feeding Yawgmoth's sac-a-Human-for-proliferate engine with a steady stream of -1/-1 fodder (Prowess on a 1-mana 1/2 body is the cheapest Yawgmoth-pillar -1/-1 feeder in the format). Malevolent Rumble done as a dedicated `MalevolentRumbleFactory` alongside the existing `MalevolentRumblePatternTemplate` (both bodies share `TokenFactory.CreateEldraziSpawn`) — the deck's MH3 selection + ramp engine: reveal-4 / take-the-first-permanent acts as a 4-deep tutor for Yawgmoth + Wall of Roots + Grist, the discarded 3 cards feed Dredger's Insight's lifegain trigger when they later leave the graveyard, and the 0/1 Eldrazi Spawn token is a one-shot {C} ritual + free sac fodder for Yawgmoth's pay-1-life-sac-a-creature ability (pairs particularly well with Bowmasters' amass + Grist's -2 self-sac to stretch a single Rumble into multiple Yawg activations). ~87%.
- **Domain Zoo** — Mid. Boros Charm done, fetches done, shocks done, Tribal Flames done, Scion of Draco's domain cost-reduction done (keyword-grant rider deferred), Territorial Kavu done (domain pump + attack loot). Ignoble Hierarch done (mono-G BRG mana-fixer + Exalted — the deck's signature MH3 turn-1 dork, replaces Noble Hierarch in the Jund-leaning colour pie). ~55%.
- **Amulet Titan** — Mid. Amulet of Vigor done (untap-on-enters-tapped trigger) + Primeval Titan done (ETB + attack land-tutor for up to 2, tapped) + Wall of Roots done (cost-counter green ramp — Defender wall that adds {G} once per turn without tapping). No bounce lands. ~35%.
- **Lurrus Companion** — Low-mid. Lurrus of the Dream-Den done (Lifelink + once-per-turn cast-permanent-mv≤2-from-graveyard; companion deck-construction rule deferred). Pairs with the existing low-mv permanent suite (Mishra's Bauble, Dryad Arbor, Stoneforge Mystic, Walking Ballista, Dark Confidant, etc.). Deck-construction enforcement absent. ~30%.
- **Hammer Time / Equipment** — Mid-high. Stoneforge Mystic done. Colossus Hammer done (+10/+0 + lose flying via AttachedBoostEffect + new LoseKeywordEffect). Sigarda's Aid done (flash-grant on Equipment/Aura via FlashGrantRegistry + ETB-attach rider). Puresteel Paladin done (Equipment-ETB draw trigger + zero-equip-cost lifecycle binder that gates on ≥3 artifacts; equip cost now wired through `EquipActivatedAbility.CostProvider` so the printed cost flips to {0} at pay time when controller has ≥3 artifacts). Sword of Fire and Ice done (markers-on-the-card path; DEBT-A grant deferred). Sword of Feast and Famine done (lifecycle-attached `ProtectionAbility("black")` + `ProtectionAbility("green")` via two `AttachedAuraAbilityGrantStaticEffect` so the equipped creature surfaces protection through `Rules.Protection.HasProtectionFromColor` — closes the grant-on-attach gap for this Sword; combat trigger has damaged player discard + untaps controller's lands). `EquipActivatedAbility` primitive (CR 702.6) now ships as a first-class `ActivatedAbility` subclass that encapsulates the sorcery-speed gate, target-creature-you-control candidate-gathering, attach-on-resolve, and a `CostProvider` delegate for Puresteel-style zero-equip — all 6 retrofitted equipment factories (Colossus Hammer, Skullclamp, Umezawa's Jitte, Sword of Fire and Ice, Sword of Feast and Famine, Cori-Steel Cutter) replace their hand-rolled `ActivatedAbility` + closure with one constructor call. Shadowspear done (+1/+1 + Trample + Lifelink via paired AttachedBoostEffect; `{1}, {T}` activated keyword-strip via new `LoseKeywordUntilEndOfTurnEffect` — generic sibling of `LoseKeywordEffect` for "target creature loses X EOT" Layer 6 grants). Inquisitor's Flail done (Equipment {2} + Equip {2} + both printed damage-doubling replacements — source-side + target-side `DamageDoubleReplacement` registrations gated on `DamageIntent.IsCombatDamage` + `AttachedTo`; CR 510.1c discriminator added to `DamageIntent` so combat damage is distinguishable from spell / ping damage at the replacement layer). ~65%.
- **Merfolk** — Mid-high. Aether Vial done (mana-free creature-cheater that's the deck's engine). Spreading Seas done. Lord of Atlantis done (symmetric lord). Master of the Pearl Trident done (controller-only lord). Mystic Sanctuary done (ETB recur). Noble Hierarch done (Exalted + 3-color mana). Silvergill Adept done (ETB cantrip — reveal-or-pay enforcement deferred). Cursecatcher done (sac-counter activated ability). Merfolk Trickster done (Flash + ETB tap + lose-abilities EOT). ~85%.
- **Death and Taxes** — Low-mid. Aether Vial done (mana-free creature drop — the deck's signature). Stoneforge Mystic done. Solitude done. Thalia, Guardian of Thraben done (noncreature tax + First Strike). Skyclave Apparition + Leonin Arbiter absent. ~45%.
- **Boros Energy / Boros Convoke** — Mid-high. Guide of Souls done (1-drop {W} 1/2 anchor: ETB energy on every small-creature drop including itself + {E}{E} pump-and-flying = mana engine + finisher pumper). Aether Hub done (energy land + WUBRG fixer via Pay {E}). Amped Raptor done (ETB exile-4 cast-mv-≤-2-for-free body that doubles as cascade engine). Monastery Swiftspear done (Prowess 1-drop). Phlage done (recursion finisher — ETB 3-damage-and-3-life Helix body + Escape alt-cost from graveyard). Ocelot Pride done (1/1 Cat anchor: Lifelink + attack-trigger token spawn + EOT flicker on combat-damage-to-player latch; Ascend / city's blessing "2 tokens" branch deferred). Static Prison done ({2}{W} stax: ETB gain {E}{E} + each-upkeep stasis-drain + permanents-enter-tapped global replacement). Wrath of the Skies done ({X}{W}{W} sweeper with `EnergyAlternativeCost` of {E}{E}{E}{E} — pay-4-energy alt-cost route nukes mv-0 nonland permanents, hard-cast at X nukes ≤X). Ajani, Nacatl Pariah done (DFC front face: 1/1 Cat with Vigilance + end-step transform-on-sac trigger; back-face Planeswalker loyalty abilities deferred — needs full DFC characteristic-replacement). Voltage Surge done (instant {R} with optional sac-an-artifact additional cost — 2 or 4 damage to any target, unconditional {E}{E} gain). Recruitment Officer done ({W} 1/1 Human Soldier with KeywordAbility("Reach") rider + {2}{W} look-6-may-reveal-creature-mv≤2 activated ability). `Player.EnergyCounters` + `Player.GainEnergy` / `Player.PayEnergy` ledger + `PayEnergyCost` ICost shape + `EnergyAlternativeCost` make the energy substrate first-class. **Boros Energy pillar audit (this PR)**: all 7 cards in the audit list (Ajani Pariah, Guide of Souls, Ocelot Pride, Amped Raptor, Wrath of the Skies, Static Prison, Phlage) verified end-to-end at the named-factory level — every dispatch test passes, every resolve body executes against the live energy ledger, no missing wiring discovered. Deferred riders all documented per-card and are uniformly cross-cutting infra gaps (back-face DFC characteristic-replacement, Ascend / city's blessing primitive, agent-driven "may" / target prompts, deterministic RNG seam, reveal-event emission) that gate broader card classes — not Boros-Energy-specific. Missing from the deck list: Static Discharge (energy-X burn), Voice of Victory (Convoke pump anchor). ~80%.

## Top 20 Modern staples NOT yet implemented

Sorted roughly by build priority (small infra lift × high meta share). **Refreshed 2026-05-26** against a live mtgtop8 Modern metagame diff (273 unique cards / 14 archetypes). The prior "round 5" priority list shipped wholesale — its last survivor, **Simian Spirit Guide**, has since shipped (`SimianSpiritGuideFactory`, #1075). Cursecatcher, Silvergill Adept, Mantis Rider, Reflector Mage, Containment Priest, Meddling Mage, Skyclave Apparition, Leonin Arbiter, Temur Battle Rage, Boseiju, and the entire bounce-land cycle (Simic Growth Chamber et al.) all landed. The current gap is dominated by **recent Universes Beyond crossovers** (Final Fantasy / Marvel / LOTR / Avatar) and **2025–26 set lands** — the surveil-land cycle was completed in the PR that wrote this refresh (Commercial District, Hedge Maze, Lush Portico, Meticulous Archive, Raucous Theater, Shadowy Backstreet, Undercity Sewers), and the long-standing **Urza's Power Plant** dispatch bug (registered under the hyphenated subtype name, never matched the seed) was fixed so Tron can assemble.

Vintage / Legacy-only cards (Power Nine, original dual lands, Mind's Desire, Yawgmoth's Bargain, Library of Alexandria, Channel, Mox Pearl / Sapphire / Jet / Ruby / Emerald, Sneak Attack, Show and Tell, etc.) are deliberately excluded — this list tracks Modern-format staples only. (Dark Ritual, Sol Ring, Mana Vault, Lotus Petal, and Mana Crypt landed as named factories for Commander/historical-format coverage even though they're not Modern-legal.)

|---|---|---|---|

Mana ability + a sorcery-/combat-speed activated haste-grant (Layer 6). Boros / aggro staple; appears in the live top-20 most-played. |
| 2 | Eldrazi Ramp top-end — Emrakul, the Promised End; Devourer of Destiny | med-high | Large EOE / MH3 Eldrazi with cast-time + ETB value. Tron / Eldrazi Ramp finishers. (Sire of Seven Deaths, **Ugin's Labyrinth** (`UginsLabyrinthFactory`), Writhing Chrysalis, and Kozilek's Command all shipped.) |
| 3 | Universes Beyond crossovers — Final Fantasy (Sephiroth, Fabled SOLDIER), Marvel (Superior Spider-Man; Krang, Master Mind), LOTR (Samwise Gamgee, Oliphaunt), Avatar (The Legend of Roku, Origin of Metalbending, Dai Li Indoctrination, Ruinous Waterbending) | varies | Bespoke per-card; the **bulk of the current gap** is post-2025 UB product now seeing Modern play. ## How to update this doc

After merging a card-shipping PR:
1. Append the new card to the **Named factories** table (or **Template-covered (notable)** if it's a template port).
2. Remove it from **Top 20 not yet implemented** if it appears there.
3. Run `./scripts/update-coverage-headline.sh` to refresh the **Headline numbers** table from disk.
4. Bump the **Last updated** date at the top.

After merging a mechanic-infra PR:
1. Flip the relevant row in **Costs**, **Effects**, **Keywords**, or **Targeting / cast flow** to **Done (#PR)**.
2. If a top-20 entry's blocker is resolved, drop its difficulty one tier in the priority table.

### Multi-`[CardName]` for functional reprints

When two cards share an **identical resolve body** (e.g. Wrath of God +
Damnation — same sweep, different printed cost/colour), don't write two
factories. Stack multiple `[CardName]` attributes on the canonical factory
and expose a `public static <Card> Create(Player owner, string cardName)`
overload that branches on the requested name to produce the right printed
name + cost. The source generator
(`Majik.Core.SourceGen.NamedCardFactoryGenerator`) detects the second-arg
overload and dispatches each `[CardName]` through `Create(owner, name)`;
the plain `Create(owner)` overload stays as a convenience for callers
that don't care about the reprint variant. Headline factory-count drops
by one per collapsed wrapper — that's expected and the script handles it.

Don't apply this to **parametric cycles** (fetchlands, pact cycle, Mox
cycle) — those have per-card numeric or per-card-flavour differences and
stay as their own factories.

CI/pre-commit can call `./scripts/update-coverage-headline.sh --check` to fail when the headline counts drift from on-disk reality.

Keep this file terse. New cards = one row. New mechanics = one row. Long-form rationale lives in PR descriptions, not here.
